Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.
2. Editorial notes or additions are enclosed in ().

Text:

<1>
<1.1>
atha prathamo'dhyāyaḥ
sarvalokeśvaro viṣṇuḥ sṛṣṭyādyaṣṭakṛdīśvaraḥ |
yathā sasarja nikhilaṃ tanme brūhi jagadguro || 1.1 ||
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
nārāyaṇo'nantaśaktiḥ pūrṇānando'kṣaraḥ svarāṭ |
jñanānandātmakānantatanurāsīditi śrutiḥ || 1.2 ||
tasya svatantrasya hareḥ sākalyena mahātmanaḥ |
svarūpaṃ naiva jānanti ramābrahmādayopi tu || 1.3 ||
tathāpi te pravakṣyāmi yathā jānāsi tatvataḥ |
pūrṇakāmasya tasyāsya sṛṣṭyādyairna prayojanaṃ || 1.4 ||
svabhāvastādṛśastasya tadarthaṃ tatkarotyajaḥ |
sa eva bhagavānātmā carācaraniyāmakaḥ || 1.5 ||
aṇḍamāvaraṇānsarvānprākṛtānpuruṣottamaḥ |
saṅkarṣaṇo nṛsiṃhena rūpeṇāgrasadīśvaraḥ || 1.6 ||
tataḥ sarvānpūrvakalpamuktānbaddhāṃśca cetanān |
svodare sthāpya bhagavānsvaprakāśo'svapatsukhī || 1.7 ||
daśāvaraṇasaṃyuktabramhāṇḍasya stithau mitiḥ |
yāvatpradeśastattulyarūpā śrīrudarūpiṇī || 1.8 ||
tāvadrūpavatī bhūmiḥ vaṭapatrābhavatpurā |
durgā tāvatparimitā andhakārasvarūpiṇī || 1.9 ||
tadā ramāvinā kaścinnānyadāsīdbahisthale |
śūnyanāmnaḥ śiśorgarbhe sarve jīvāstadāvasan || 1.10 ||
tadūrdhvakukṣigā muktāḥ prabhavanti mahālaye |
muktiyogyā madhyakukṣau nityabaddhāstu nabhitaḥ || 1.11 ||
adhosurāḥ kaṭitaṭe prāptaduḥkhāstadūrdhvagāḥ |
vasanti klptānandaste muktā bhuñjanti netarāḥ || 1.12 ||
prāptavyānpralayenaiva teṣāṃ dāsyati mādhavaḥ |
hitvā prāptavyamānandaṃ klptānandaikabhojināṃ || 1.13 ||
vaikuṇṭhādi vihārañca hitvā garbhasthitirhareḥ |
pralaye sarvamuktānāṃ nāśa ityucyatebjaja || 1.14 ||
sukhaduḥkhādi bhogārhāḥ icchājñānasvarūpiṇaḥ |
dveṣadharmādharmayatnaniratā jīvarāśayaḥ || 1.15 ||
liṅgabaddhāḥ samastāśca sṛṣṭau jīvāścaranti hi |
sāmyāvasthāmupagatāḥ liṅgadeha samanvitāḥ || 1.16 ||
laye vasantīśagarbhe pūrvaśaktistadānahi |
tadāsarve gāḍhanidrāmupagacchanti tādṛśāḥ || 1.17 ||
laya ityucyate sadbhiḥ na layosti svarūpataḥ |
sarvajīvasvarūpasthāḥ jñānavyañanaśaktayaḥ || 1.18 ||
manovṛttijñānadānaśaktiryāśrutermatā |
layejīvādyabhāvena sā śaktiḥ kuṇṭhitā bhaveth || 1.19 ||
vyāpteśvaramatisthānāṃ śrutīnāṃ tādṛśo layaḥ |
aṇḍādijaḍajīvānāṃ vyomāvyākṛtasaṃjñakaṃ || 1.20 ||
āśrayaṃ sṛṣṭikāletatpralaye kuṇṭhitaṃ bhavet |
deśasyāvyākṛtasyāsya nāśa ityucyate laye || 1.21 ||
samasta liṅgadehānāṃ tattatkarmānusārataḥ |
nānāyoni prāpikāsti śaktiḥ sṛṣṭau laye na tu || 1.22 ||
sukhaduḥkhapradāśaktiḥ samastakṛtakarmaṇāṃ |
sṛṣṭau laye na sā viṣṇorājñayā kuṇṭhitā bhaveth || 1.23 ||
tādṛśo hi layastasya na svarūpalayasmṛtaḥ |
varṇeṣu sarvabhāṣābhivyañjakā śaktiriṣyate || 1.24 ||
sṛṣṭau na sāsti pralaye teṣāṃ nāśo hi tādṛśaḥ |
tamaḥ sthānāṃ pūrvaklptaduḥkhabhogo layaḥ smṛtaḥ || 1.25 ||
prakṛtyātmakaliṅgasya jaḍāyāḥ prakṛtestathā |
sūkṣmāṇāṃ sthūlakatvānāṃ triguṇasyāpi kṛtsnaśaḥ || 1.26 ||
sraṣṭṛtvaśaktiḥ sṛṣṭau hi na laye syattato layaḥ |
hitvā jagatsarjanādyaṃ sarvadā śrīpatau ratiḥ || 1.27 ||
atisāmīpyasevā hi layo lakṣmyāḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
anityasyācetanasya aṇḍāntarbahireva vā || 1.28 ||
kramātsaṅkarṣaṇāgnestu dāho nāśa udahṛtaḥ |
deśataḥ kālato vyāptā varṇāsarvepi śāśvatāḥ || 1.29 ||
jaḍā ca prakṛtirnityā jaḍarūpāṇureva ca |
deśataḥ kālato vyāptācidrūpāḥ śrīhareḥḥsadā || 1.30 ||
anantaguṇato nīcā guṇato'nantariśvare |
laye samāśritāḥsarve vasanti kṣīṇaśaktayaḥ || 1.31 ||
sampūrṇaśakte bhagavatyanante deśakālataḥ |
paramāṇumitānantānantarūpaḥ sadā hariḥ || 1.32 ||
tadabhinnānantasūkṣmasthūlarūpo hariḥ paraḥ |
samastajīvarūporusūkṣmasthūlasvarūpakaḥ || 1.33 ||
samastajaḍarūpānantānantarūpo jagadguruḥ |
anantānantarapairapyabhinno nirguṇo hariḥ || 1.34 ||
sarvataḥ pāṇipādātmā sarvato'kṣiśiromukhaḥ |
sarvatra sarvendriyāvānsarvamāvṛtya tiṣṭhati || 1.35 ||
aprākṛtendriyānantarūpakaḥ paramādbhutaḥ |
asaṅgaḥ sarvaviccaiva nirguṇo guṇapūritaḥ || 1.36 ||
bahirantaśca bhūtānāṃ acaraścara eva ca |
avyaktovyaktarūpaśca sādhuḥsatsākṣigocaraḥ || 1.37 ||
ekopyanekavadbhāti nityasatyo mahāsukhaḥ |
anantavedagamyaśca sṛṣṭyādyaṣṭakṛdīśvaraḥ || 1.38 ||
svatantrovarṇanīyorurupānantastu cittanuḥ |
svānantānantacinmātradehamadhyaikadeśagaḥ || 1.39 ||
yoganidrāmupagato vinidro'svapadīśvaraḥ |
aṇḍasyāvaraṇasyopacayaṃ dagdhvā laye hariḥ || 1.40 ||
upādānaṃ prakṛtyaikībhūtaṃ kṛtvā nṛkesarī |
aniruddhādi mukhagaṃ saṅkarṣaṇa nṛsiṃhataḥ || 1.41 ||
sarvasūkṣmaśarīropacayaṃ dagdhvākhileśvaraḥ |
tadupādānabhūtāni liṅgairekīkarotyajaḥ || 1.42 ||
tadāstaraṇarūpābhūḥ śrīrbhāryārūpiṇī parā |
durgā prāvaraṇākārā hareramitatejasaḥ || 1.43 ||
svāneva suguṇānbhuñjanasuptābhūtsusuptavat |
muktābhuktodaro viṣṇuḥ anantānantabhānuruk || 1.44 ||
svaprakāśosvapadyoganidrayāvaṭapatragaḥ |
sṛṣṭikāle sarvajīvāḥ nānāduḥkhasamākulāḥ || 1.45 ||
bhramanti sarvajīvānāṃ tattacchramanivṛttaye |
laye svāpaṃ prāpayati liṅgabaddhānsvadehagān || 1.46 ||
anādiyogtākarmabhāvājñānākulānbahūn |
sṛjyānvilakṣaṇāntāratamyasthāntrividhātmakān || 1.47 ||
svalpanidrānmahānidrāngaḍhanidrāsamanvitān |
cinmātrānandabhuṅmuktānsadā duḥkhabhujo'surān || 1.48 ||
tamaḥsaṃsthānsadā baddhānjaṭhare sthāpya keśavaḥ |
vaṭapatre svapaddevo nidrāmācaratīva saḥ || 1.49 ||
teṣu sṛjyānsvayaṃ sṛṣṭvā yathāyogyaphalaṃ vibhuḥ |
sukhaṃ miśraṃ tathā duḥkhaṃ dātumaicchallayāntime || 1.50 ||
layāsyaṣṭamo bhāgo layāntima udāhṛtaḥ |
laye samāliṅya hariṃ vinidrā parameśvarī || 1.51 ||
nidrite vā sadānandavāridhiṃ samupāśritā |
layaṣṭamāṃśe śrīdevī asvatantrā svatantrataḥ || 1.52 ||
prabuddhācājñayā viṣṇoḥ stotuṃ samupacakrame |
deśataḥ kālato'nantā guṇānantyavivarjitā || 1.53 ||
brahmavāyvoḥ koṭiguṇaiḥ adhikā viṣṇuvallabhā |
laye sṛṣṭau sarvanityapadārtheṣvabhimāninī || 1.54 ||
sṛṣṭyādyaṣṭakarī viṣṇoḥ ājñayānantarūpiṇī |
chāyeva sūkṣmasthūloru rūpaṃ viṣṇuṃ samāśritā || 1.55 ||
viṣṇoranantavedeṣu proktāntadadhikānapi |
guṇānsaṃjānatī sākṣātsarvavedābhimāninī || 1.56 ||
avarṇaṇīyasaundaryā sarvajīvābhimāninī |
śubhreṣu muktiyogyeṣu śubhrā (muktā) śrīrabhimāninī || 1.57 ||
rakteṣu sarvabaddheṣu raktābhūrabhimāninī |
nīleṣvayogyajīveṣu nīlā durgābhimāninī || 1.58 ||
prakṛti prakṛtau sāmyavaiṣamyādi pravartakā |
achinnā bhagavadbhaktā jaḍeṣu jaḍarūpiṇī || 1.59 ||
citsucidrūpiṇī strīṣu strīceṣṭādi pravartakā |
puruṣākṛtirutkṛṣṭā puṃbhāvaparivarjitā || 1.60 ||
avyaktatatkāryamaya dehaśūnyā tathāvidhā |
svayaṃ śrīḥ viṣṇubhāvajñā vedaistuṣṭāva vedavith || 1.61 ||
te vedāḥ ṛgyajuḥsāmātharvaṇāḥ śrīharervaśāḥ |
catvāropi pṛthagjātyā anantā doṣavarjitāḥ || 1.62 ||
udāttādi svarārthādi varṇakramasamāgamāḥ |
visargabindupūrvoruśabdayogyaguṇoccayaiḥ || 1.63 ||
anantakālamarabhyariṣadvetyāsavarjitāḥ |
sāṃśā samastadeśeṣu vyāptāḥ śuddhajaḍāḥ sadā || 1.64 ||
guṇākhyānaparā viṣṇoḥ śabdadoṣavivarjitāḥ |
jñatajñāpakatā teṣu viparītārthavaktṛtā || 1.65 ||
aspaṣṭatvādayasteṣu vādadoṣānasantyalaṃ |
dhvanirūpā sṛṣṭikāle vāksthā vāgdevatāvaśāḥ || 1.66 ||
laye lakṣmīvaśāḥ sṛṣṭau brahmādyairabhimānibhiḥ |
rakṣitāḥ pralaye sṛṣṭāvekarūpā bhavanti hi || 1.67 ||
anantānantasaṃkhyātāḥ viṣṇoḥ jñāne ca sādhakāḥ |
sarvadeśagatānantā kaṇṭhasthāstasya śārṅgiṇaḥ || 1.68 ||
kaṇṭhenodgīyamanāste jīvānāṃ yogyatāvaśāt |
tattatsvarūpavijñānavyañjakāḥ śrīharīcchayā || 1.69 ||
vṛttijñānotpādaśauṇḍāḥ a(yogyā)thājñānāmamabodhakāḥ |
śabdarūpāḥsākṣiyuktamanaḥ śrotraikagocarāḥ || 1.70 ||
sarvaśabdātmakahareḥ bimbasya pratibimbakāḥ |
śabdarūpā vedagatā durgā sānantarūpiṇī || 1.71 ||
puruṣodbodhituṃ jñātvā tuṣṭāva puruṣaṃ paraṃ |
anantātmānantaśaktiḥ oṃ(ā)kārādi śrutivrajān || 1.72 ||
nityānsavyañjayanteṣāṃ sṛṣṭau durgāmupādiśat |
pralayasyāṣṭame bhāge vedasraṣṭurjagatpateḥ || 1.73 ||
oṃ(a)kāraḥ prathamo vyakto babhūva bhagavanmayaḥ |
oṃānādabindū ca ghoṣaśāntātiśāntakāḥ || 1.74 ||
yatsvarūpāśca viśvādyāḥ yadvācyā yadgatāḥ parāḥ |
oṅkāravācyaḥ puruṣaḥ putrāste nityacinmayāḥ || 1.75 ||
te viśvataijasaprājñaturyātmāntarātmanāṃ |
paramātmajñānātmakānāṃ rūpāṇāṃ vācakā hareḥ || 1.76 ||
tārapraṇavaśabdābhyāṃ vācya oṅkārariritaḥ |
nārāyaṇāṣṭākṣarākhyaḥ taragarbhākṣarairabhūth || 1.77 ||
abhivyaktotha viśvādirvyaktaviśvādayokhilāḥ |
nārāyaṇamanorvācyāḥ devatāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 1.78 ||
tāragarbhākṣarairādyaiścaturbhirabhavanmanuḥ |
vyāhṛtyākhyo'niruddhādyāḥ viśvādyutthā tadīritāḥ || 1.79 ||
tāragarbhākṣarairvyaktāḥ varṇāḥ pañcāśadīritāḥ |
akaucaṭautapau tadvadyaśau aṣṭā udāhṛtāḥ || 1.80 ||
aṣṭavarṇāḥ akārotthāḥ acaḥ ṣoḍaśasaṃmatāḥ |
pañcavarṇāḥ kavargasya ukārotthāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 1.81 ||
makārotthāścavargasya pañcavarṇāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
ṭavargapañcavarṇāstu nādotthā iti saṃmatāḥ || 1.82 ||
pañcavarṇāstavargasya binduvyaktā udāhṛtāḥ |
pavargapañcavarṇāstu ghoṣataḥ sambhavanti hi || 1.83 ||
yavargasya caturvarṇāḥ vyaktiṃ yāsyanti śāntitaḥ |
atiśāntācchavargasya pañcavarṇāḥsamutthitāḥ || 1.84 ||
kaṣayogādabhivyaktaḥ kṣopyanyokṣara ucyate |
pañcāśatāntuvarṇānāṃ tāravarṇāṣṭa daivataiḥ || 1.85 ||
viśvādibhiḥ kramādvyaktāḥ ajādyā devatāmatāḥ |
aja ānanda indreśa ugra ūrja ṛtambharaḥ || 1.86 ||
ṝghaḥlśau lṝjirekātmaira ojobhṛdaurasaḥ |
antordhagarbhaḥ kapilaḥ khapatirgaruḍāsanaḥ || 1.87 ||
gharmoṅgasāraścārvaṅgaśchandogamyo janārdanaḥ |
jhaḍitāriñamaṣṭaṅkī ṭhalako ḍarako ḍharī || 1.88 ||
ṇātmātārasthapodaṇḍī dhanvī namyaḥ paraḥ phalī |
balirbhagomanuryajño rāmo lakṣīpatirvaraḥ || 1.89 ||
śāntasaṃvitṣaḍguṇaśca sārātmā haṃsalālukau |
akārādyaiḥsarvavarṇairvācyā ete prakīrtitāḥ || 1.90 ||
kṣavācyo nṛsiṃhastu sarvajīvaniyāmakaḥ |
ekapañcāśadvarṇātmā matṛkāmantrariritaḥ || 1.91 ||
nārāyaṇāṣṭavarṇāśca caturvyāhṛtibhiḥ kramāt |
vāsudevadvādaśārṇamantrobhūnmahadātmakaḥ || 1.92 ||
keśavādyāḥ dvādaśaiva tanmantrākṣara devatāḥ |
trivāraṃ viṣṇunaivoktānmantrānnārāyaṇādabhūth || 1.93 ||
gayatrī brahmaḥṛṣikā dviguṇādvādaśākṣarāt |
keśavādyāścaturviṃśatgāyatrīvarṇadevatāḥ || 1.94 ||
gāyatryāḥ pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ sūktānmantrāstu vaiṣṇavāḥ |
puṃsūktapratipādyastu puruṣo harirīritaḥ || 1.95 ||
viṣṇumantrodito viṣṇuḥ sarvātmā parikīrtitaḥ |
praṇavoṣṭau vyāhṛtayo mātrṛkādvādaśākṣarau || 1.96 ||
gāyatrī brahmaḥṛṣikā puṃsūktaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ tathā |
aṣṭāvete mahāmantrāḥ vaidikāḥsarvasiddhidāḥ || 1.97 ||
puṃsūktātsarvavedānāṃ vyaktirāsīditi śrutiḥ |
durgayānantarūpiṇyānanta vedodito hariḥ || 1.98 ||
tayārthito'khilādhyakṣaḥ svātmāsṛṣṭimacīklpath || 1.99 ||
iti śrī paratattavanirṇaye prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede prathamo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.2>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
saṃstuto bhagavānevaṃ tadaivotthāya keśavaḥ |
śūnyanāmātha jagṛhe paruṣākhyāṃ tanuṃ hariḥ || 2.1 ||
puruṣaḥ sarvarūpāṇāṃ vyaktābīja ivasthitaḥ |
sonantātmā vyāptatanuḥ viśvakukṣiritīritaḥ || 2.2 ||
puruṣādvāsudevādyāścatvāro hyabhavaṃstataḥ |
vāsudevaḥ saṅkarṣaṇaḥ pradyumnaścāniruddhakaḥ || 2.3 ||
śvetaraktapītanīlāḥ vāsudevādayaḥ kramāt |
muktyaihṛtyai tathā guptai sṛṣṭyai svecchātanurhariḥ || 2.4 ||
śūnyanāmnī ramādevī śrīrbhūtvā puruṣaṃśritā |
saivamāyājayābhūtvā kṛtiḥśāntiritīritā || 2.5 ||
vāsudevādi bhāryāsītsarvadā sānapāyinī |
caturmukho vāsudevaḥ brahmaṇo bimbarūpakaḥ || 2.6 ||
saṅkarṣaṇaḥ sahasrāsyaḥ phaṇirāḍbimbarūpakaḥ |
pradyumno mārabimbātmā hyatisundaravigrahaḥ || 2.7 ||
bimbātmā kāmaputrasyāniruddhāsyāniruddhakaḥ |
sakṛddholkādi pañcātmā viśvādyaṣṭatanurhariḥ || 2.8 ||
vimalādinavātmābhūtsamatsyādi daśākṛtiḥ |
śaktyādi dvādaśātmābhūdvārakādi ṣoḍaśarūpavān || 2.9 ||
ānandādi trayoviṃśadrūpa āsītparātparaḥ |
keśavādi caturviṃśadrūpavānabhavaddhariḥ || 2.10 ||
ajādi pañcāśadrūpaḥ tatobhūtpuruṣottamaḥ |
kālākhyaṣaṣṭhirūpobhūtsaṃvatsaraniyāmakaḥ || 2.11 ||
nārāyaṇādi śatakaḥ sa viśvādi sahasrakaḥ |
parādyanantarūpobhūdātmabheda vivarjitaḥ || 2.12 ||
tadājñayaiva devī sā ramāpi bahurūpiṇī |
sa tayānantarūpiṇyā anantarūpo ramāpatiḥ || 2.13 ||
svarato'pi tayā devyā tatprītyarthaṃ dayānidhiḥ |
layakālāṣṭamāṃśantu nītvā puruṣapuṅgavaḥ || 2.14 ||
prārambhe sṛṣṭikālasya ajādīnsraṣṭumārabhath || 2.15 ||
iti śrī paratatvanirṇaye prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede dvitīyodhyāyaḥ
<1.3>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
jālarandhrātgrahāviṣṭā sūryaraśmiprabhā bhuvi |
yadā bhavanti tasyāḥ samīpe tirgāsthitaḥ || 3.1 ||
keśaṃ yadodgamayato so martyānāṃ paramāṇukaḥ |
martyottamo bhaktipākātmaharādiṣu saṃsthitaḥ || 3.2 ||
yaṃ kālaṃ sūkṣmadṛgveda sa kālaḥ paramāṇukaḥ |
tacchatāṃśo hi devānāṃ tacchatāṃśobjajasya tu || 3.3 ||
bhūmau sthitānāṃ martyānāṃ yaḥ kālaḥ paramāṇukaḥ |
tacchatāṃśo devaloke sthitasya paramāṇukaḥ || 3.4 ||
tatkoṭyaṃśo ramāyāśca tadanantāṃśako hareḥ |
paramāṇuriti proktaḥ tadanyairnyaiva budhyate || 3.5 ||
paramāṇudvayātmātu kālo dvyaṇukariritaḥ |
paramāṇutrayātmasya tryaṇukaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 3.6 ||
truṭiḥ tribhiśca tryaṇukaiḥ vedhaḥ syāttatrayeṇa tu |
lavovedhatrayātmāsyātnimeṣastu tribhirlavaiḥ || 3.7 ||
kṣaṇastrinamiṣaḥ proktaḥ kāṣṭhākhyā kṣaṇapañcakāt |
kāṣṭhābhiḥ pañcadaśabhiḥ laghukālaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 3.8 ||
laghubhiḥ pañcadaśabhiḥ vināḍikā prakīrtitā |
vināḍikā ṣaṣṭhibhiryā nāḍikaikā bhaviṣyati || 3.9 ||
tābhyāṃ muhūrtatriṃśadbhiḥ ahorātramitīritaṃ |
aṣṭamāṃśānnyūnasaptaghaṭikābhiśca saptabhiḥ || 3.10 ||
sapādasaptabhirvāpi sārdhasaptabhireva vā |
yāmaḥ kāloṣṭabhirvāpi kālabhedena coditaḥ || 3.11 ||
yāmaiścaturbhiḥ ahaḥ syāttāvadbhiryāminīmatā |
ahorātratriṃśatā ca māsaḥ pakṣadvayātmakaḥ || 3.12 ||
tābhyāṃ ṛtustatrayeṇaivā ayanaṃ parikīrtitaṃ |
karkādi kārmukānteṣu yadā carati bhaskaraḥ || 3.13 ||
dakṣiṇāyanamuddhiṣṭaṃ devānāṃ rātrisaṃjñakaṃ |
yāvannakrādike bhānau mithunānteṣu tiṣṭhati || 3.14 ||
uttarāyaṇamuddhiṣṭaṃ tāvatkālaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
ayanābhyāṃ vatsarastu dviṣaṇmāsātmakaḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.15 ||
ṣaṣṭhyuttaratriśatakaiḥ ahorātraiśca vatsaraḥ |
aṣṭāviṃśatsahasraistu lakṣasaptadaśābdataḥ || 3.16 ||
klptaḥ kṛtayugastasmāttretāpādonamucyate |
sahasraṣaṇṇavatyetalakṣadvādaśa saṃmitaḥ || 3.17 ||
tretāyugastataḥ pādanyūno dvāparariritaḥ |
catuḥṣaṣṭhisahasreta aṣṭalakṣamitismṛtaḥ || 3.18 ||
dvāpararāttu kalistasmādardhapādamitismṛtaḥ |
dviṣoḍaśasahasretāccaturlakṣātmakaḥ kaliḥ || 3.19 ||
kṛtatretādvāparaśca kaliśceti caturyugāḥ |
caturyugātmakaḥ kālo mahāyuga itīryate || 3.20 ||
viṃśatsahasretalakṣatricatvāriṃśadabdakaiḥ |
klptaḥ kālaḥ kāladṛgbhiḥ mahāyuga itīryate || 3.21 ||
ṣaṣṭhyuttarābdatriṃśatāddevānāmabdariritaḥ |
tādṛgabdasahasrāṇāṃ catuṣkoṇaḥ kṛtāhvayaḥ || 3.22 ||
tretā tu trisahasrābdā dvisahasrābdasaṃmitaḥ |
dvāparastu tato nyūnasahasrābdamitaḥ kaliḥ || 3.23 ||
kṛtasyādau kṛtānte ca pṛthagabdaścatuḥśataṃ |
sandhistretāyugādyante pṛthagabdaśatatrayaṃ || 3.24 ||
sandhistu dvāparādyante pṛthagabdaśatadvayaṃ |
śatābdaḥsaṃmitaḥ kālaḥ sandhirādyantayoḥ kaleḥ || 3.25 ||
evaṃ daivena mānena dvadaśābdāḥsahasrataḥ |
klptaḥ kālaḥ kālavidbhiḥ mahāyuga itīryate || 3.26 ||
sārdhāṣṭādaśalakṣābde yugānāṃ ekasaptati |
milito manusandhātabhogakāla udīritaḥ || 3.27 ||
dviṣaṭśatābdasamito layaḥ svāyambhuvottaraḥ |
svārociṣādikānāntu ṣaṇṇāmapyantare layaḥ || 3.28 ||
pañcādaśaśatābdātmā layaḥ kāla udīritaḥ |
caturdaśaśatābdātmā layaḥ kāla udāhṛtaḥ || 3.29 ||
tadanya manvantareṣu niyamātsarvadaiva tu |
caturyugasahasrantu brahmaṇo dina u(divamu)cyate || 3.30 ||
rītriśca tāvatī tasya ahorātrā tu tādṛśā |
ṣaṣṭhyuttaratriśatakādvatsaro'jasya kīrtitaḥ || 3.31 ||
evaṃ vidhaśatābdātmā kālo'jasyāyuṣo mataḥ |
vāsudevātsūkṣmatanuṃ svaśatābdādime kṣaṇe || 3.32 ||
yaḥ prāpa saśatābdāyuḥ tadrūpāṇītarāṇi tu |
māsatrayādhikādabdaṣaṭkātpūrvatanādabhūth || 3.33 ||
sṛjyānāṃ sūkṣmasāṃśānāṃ sūkṣmasṛṣṭiścatustanoḥ |
sṛṣṭānāṃ sūkṣmataḥ pūrvaṃ sthūladehastato'bhavath || 3.34 ||
nārāyaṇādajādrudrātkramātkālānusārataḥ |
yāvatāṃ sūkṣmataḥ sṛṣṭiḥ sthūlasṛṣṭistu tāvatā || 3.35 ||
aṇḍādūrdhvaṃ cāvṛtayaḥ sthūladehāśca tanmayāḥ |
ādyasya brahmaṇaḥ pūrvaṃ pañcāśadvatsarānti me || 3.36 ||
dine vyatīte tadrātrāvaṇḍasṛṣṭirathābhavat |
tadantaḥ svapato viṣṇorjalamadhyagatasya tu || 3.37 ||
nābhyutthitāllokamayātprākṛtātpadmakośataḥ |
pādmaḥ brahmotthitaḥsārdhaṃ saptatriṃśaccatanmaye || 3.38 ||
dināntime niśāyāṃ ca nāśo'ṇḍasya jagatpateḥ |
vyutkramātpūrvamānena sthūlasūkṣmasya nāśanaṃ || 3.39 ||
evaṃ śatāyuṣaḥ pūrṇedviparārdhātmakāyuṣī |
svayaṃ cātmasvarūpāṇāṃ ekībhāvaṃ karotyajaḥ || 3.40 ||
yāvatkālenātmasṛṣṭirekībhāvastu tāvatā |
ekībhūtepi ramati tathaikībhūtamāyayā || 3.41 ||
tāvatkālaṃ tataḥ paścātsuptavatsvāpayatyajaḥ |
sārdhadviṣaḍbrahmavarṣamiti śiṣṭe niśānti me || 3.42 ||
bhāge punaḥ pūrvavacca ātmasṛṣṭyādimīśvaraḥ |
karoti puruṣastasya nāyurmānaṃ vidhīyate || 3.43 ||
evaṃ vidhāparimitānantānantaparātmake |
kāle bhāvinyapi hariḥ sṛṣṭyādīśaḥ kariṣyati || 3.44 ||
svantantrasyāsya maryādā asvatantrairna śakyate |
laṅghituṃ pūrṇaśakterhi vaśe sarvaṃ carācaraṃ || 3.45 ||
ye'nantakālatastasya mṛtijanmajarādayaḥ |
āyāsaduḥkhadaurbhāgyacintāsantāpapūrvakāḥ || 3.46 ||
ajñānerṣyāsūyapīḍā kāmakrodhabhayādayaḥ |
kṛśatārogaduritapuṇyalepādayastathā || 3.47 ||
āśāhāniśca vṛddhiśca kṣuttṛṭkampādayastathā |
nāsandoṣāḥ nottaratra bhaviṣyantyakhileśvare || 3.48 ||
tattaddurjanasaṅghaiśca ye kalpyāḥ doṣasañcayāḥ |
tattadvirodhi suguṇānantyapūrṇasya mīluṣaḥ || 3.49 ||
taddoṣaleśagandhopi na viṣṇoriti niścayaḥ |
yaḥ kālaḥ paramāṇvādi dviparārdhāvasānakaḥ || 3.50 ||
teṣu sarveṣu kāleṣu tattannāmnā ramāpatiḥ |
tatsāmarthyavyañjakaśca sthitvā rakṣatyajaḥ prabhuḥ || 3.51 ||
nityaḥ kālapravāhoyamanityāstu kṣaṇādayaḥ |
naṣṭeṣu satsukāleṣu vartamāneṣu vai kramāth || 3.52 ||
avināśya svarūposau tatpravāhasya rakṣakaḥ |
śatābdaḥsaṃmitaḥ kālo brahmaṇaḥ parasaṃjñakaḥ || 3.53 ||
sakālastu nimeṣākhyaḥ ahaśca parameśituḥ |
ityajñajanabuddhīnāṃ śuddhyarthamupacaryate || 3.54 ||
ata āhurmahāprājñāḥ kālatonantamīśvaraṃ |
athāpi parakālāntaṃ brahmāṇaṃ janayanvibhuḥ || 3.55 ||
aṅgīkaroti taṃ kālamahoyadakhileśvaraḥ |
rātrimānādi śūnyopi tāvantaṃ kālamīśvaraḥ || 3.56 ||
rātritvena caratyevaṃ brahmādīnsvāpayatyajaḥ |
niśāyāścarame bhāge ātmasṛṣṭyādimīśvaraḥ || 3.57 ||
karoti puruṣastasya āyurmānaṃ vidhīyate |
evaṃ vidhāparimitānantānantaparātmakaḥ || 3.58 ||
kālāvyatītā sṛṣṭyādi karturviṣṇoḥ parātmanaḥ |
svarūpendriyavadyosau kālorūpādivarjitaḥ || 3.59 ||
pūrvapūrvasu kāleṣu naṣṭeṣvanye bhavanti hi |
uttaratrāyāsyamānaparamāṇupravāhataḥ || 3.60 ||
saṃyojyaghaṭikādyāstu kālojñeyaḥ samānataḥ |
ghaṭikāyāmamāsādyā naikadā sambhavanti hi || 3.61 ||
āyurmānavihīnasyānantānantaparātmakaiḥ |
viṣṇoḥ kālānantyamitau kālajñāna(naṃ)prayojakaṃ || 3.62 ||
vedānāmakṣarāṇāñca deśanyāvyākṛtasya ca |
ramāyāḥ sarvajīvānāṃ muktānāṃ liṅgasaṃyujāṃ || 3.63 ||
samastaliṅgadehānāṃ jaḍāyāḥ prakṛtestathā |
eteṣāmasvatantrāṇāṃ svatantravaśavartināṃ || 3.64 ||
kālānantyamitau nityaṃ kālajñāna(naṃ)prayojakaṃ |
anityā(anantā)nāṃ jaḍānāṃ ca sūkṣmasthūlaśarīriṇāṃ || 3.65 ||
āyurmāne śarīrāṇāṃ kālajñanaṃ prayojakaṃ |
evaṃ kālagatiṃ jānankālabhītiṃ tariṣyati || 3.66 ||
ekasminparakāletu ekamaṇḍaṃ jagadguruḥ |
daśāvaraṇasaṃyuktaṃ sṛjatyavati hantyajaḥ || 3.67 ||
naikadājāṇḍabahulaṃ sraṣṭumicchati keśavaḥ |
brahmāṇaṃ garuḍaṃ śeṣaṃ idraṃ prāṇaṃ raviṃ surān || 3.68 ||
ekamekaṃ harirmuktaṃ(kta)parakāle karotyajaḥ |
tathāpyajāṇḍānantānāṃ niyantājāṇḍarūpavān || 3.69 ||
hariḥ tādṛṅmahārūpānantānantāni vai hareḥ |
romāṅkureṣvananteṣu saṃvasantīti hi śrutiḥ || 3.70 ||
tādṛśānantarūpāṇāṃ anantyānantyayogataḥ |
adhaścordhvaṃ cāṣṭadikṣu saṃyuktānāmanantaśaḥ || 3.71 ||
rūpānantyairapūrṇoru deśe pūrṇatanurhariḥ |
atyabhinnorucitsaukhyabalādi guṇavigrahaḥ || 3.72 ||
nirviśeṣaḥ sarvagataḥ sūkṣmasthūlādirūpakaḥ |
samaśaktiḥ samajñānabalādiśubhasadguṇaḥ || 3.73 ||
samādhikavihīnaśca ramayānantarupayā |
sahasvādhīnayā satyāśeṣacijjaḍabhṛddhariḥ || 3.74 ||
svabhinnāyāḥ śriyāḥ bhinnāḥ muktāmaktāsucidguṇaiḥ |
bhinnairnityajaḍaiḥsatyaiḥ bhinnaiḥ jīveśvaraiḥ sadā || 3.75 ||
vibhinno viśvato viṣṇuḥ deśato guṇato vibhuḥ |
anantānantarūpeṇa abhinnobhinnavaddhariḥ || 3.76 ||
pūrṇopi jaḍacidrūpaṃ sṛjatyavati hantyajaḥ || 3.77 ||
iti śrī paratattavanarṇaye prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.4>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
sūkṣmaṃ sarvajagadbījaṃ mitaṃ trātaṃ ca viṣṇunā |
pradhānamavyaktamiti yadvadanti manīṣiṇaḥ || 4.1 ||
mahāmāyetyavidyeti prakṛtirmohinīti ca |
triguṇātmakamavyaktaṃ trirūpaṃ tattriśaktimath || 4.2 ||
avidyākāmakarmādi bījamityapi tāṃ viduḥ |
rakṣitaṃ pralaye sṛṣṭā(vevā)vaṇvanantasvarūpavān || 4.3 ||
anādikarmagranthīti bandhobandhakamityapi |
ajāmananyavaśagāṃ kalāṣoḍaśasaṃyutāṃ || 4.4 ||
tīvraduḥkhātmakāṃ nānāyoniprāpakamīritaṃ |
nārikelasamākāraṃ paricchedatrayātmakaṃ || 4.5 ||
mūrdhnicchidrayutaṃ divyaṃ ratnavadbhāsvaraṃ sadā |
viṣṇunā ramayācchannācchidragarbhāvakāśavath || 4.5 ||
sṛṣṭau laye ramā tasya māninī ca nirantaraṃ |
sṛṣṭāveva brahmavāyū sabhāryāvabhimāninau || 4.7 ||
rajaḥsattvatamaḥsaṃjñabhāgatrayasamanvitaṃ |
satvabhāgaḥ śuklavarṇaḥ raktavarṇastu rājasaḥ || 4.8 ||
nīlavarṇastamobhāga iti bhāgatrayātmakaḥ |
jñānakarmendriyakalāḥ rajobhāge vyavasthitāḥ || 4.9 ||
tamobhāgagatāḥ pañcatanmātrāsaṃjñitāḥ kalāḥ |
manaḥ kalā satvabhāgagatā sarvapradhānikā || 4.10 ||
manobuddhirahaṅkāraścittaṃ ceti caturvidhaṃ |
kāmakrodhamahālobhamadamātsaryamohakāḥ || 4.11 ||
dveṣāhaṅkāramamakārerṣyasūyābhayādayaḥ |
manodoṣāstadvihīnaṃ manojñānāya sādhanaṃ || 4.12 ||
manaścaturvidhaṃ nityaṃ jaḍaprakṛtigaṃ mataṃ |
tajjanyamapyanityaṃ syāttatra cittaṃ na doṣayuk || 4.13 ||
manaḥ svarṇādyanekārthamapekṣāṃ janayiṣyati |
buddhirniṣiddhabhogcchāṃ sadāsaṃkalpayiṣyati || 4.14 ||
ahaṅkāraḥ pumarthānāṃ ghātakaḥḥ sottamaiḥ saha |
spardhāṃ viṣṇusuradveṣaṃ kalpayatyapi sarvaśaḥ || 4.15 ||
cittaṃ satsādhanagataṃ karoti puruṣaṃ sadā |
satsādhanamahaṅkārakalitaṃ pāpamevahi || 4.16 ||
ahaṅkāravihīnaṃ taccittaṃ vijñānasādhanaṃ |
racite bhārate varṣe yathā mānuṣasattanuṃ || 4.17 ||
labdhvā gurukulāvāsaṃ kṛtvānekasujanmabhiḥ |
kṛtvātmayogyasatkarma labdhasadgurusevayā || 4.18 ||
prasannairupadiṣṭoru sacchāstraśravaṇādibhiḥ |
vṛttijñānapradaṃ tacca vṛttibhaktyādi sādhakaṃ || 4.19 ||
tābhyāṃ svārūpikajñānabhaktirvyaktirbhaviṣyati |
svārūpikaṃ mano nityaṃ tataḥ svārūpikī matiḥ || 4.20 ||
vyaktā bhavati nityā sā bhaktiḥ svārūpikī tathā |
saivānandasvarūpeṇa nityā mukteṣu tiṣṭhati || 4.21 ||
indriyāṇāṃ golakāni dṛśyānyāhuḥ samagraśaḥ |
atīndriyāṇīndriyāṇi jñānakarmamayāni ca || 4.22 ||
śrotratvaṅnetrarasanaghrāṇādyā jñānaśaktayaḥ |
vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthāḥ sarve karmaśaktayaḥ || 4.23 ||
śrotraṃ śabdagrāhiśabdo dvividhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
śabdo dhvanyātmako varṇātmakaśceti tadādimaḥ || 4.24 ||
ahaṅkārātsamudbhūtaḥ ākāśotpattikāraṇaḥ |
dhvanyātmakastu śabdastu prathamaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 4.25 ||
jñānakarmendriyamanomātrāḥ sūkṣmāhyanādayaḥ |
nityāśca pralaye sṛṣṭāvanityāste prakīrtitāḥ || 4.26 ||
varṇātmakastu yaḥśabdo nityaḥ sarvagato vibhuḥ |
dravyamityeva sarveṣāṃ na guṇaḥ kasyacinmate || 4.27 ||
vedāḥ padādvarṇataśca śabdataḥ svaratastathā |
anādinityāste sarvepyapauruṣeyaguṇotkarāḥ || 4.28 ||
viṣṇormāhātmyavijñaptau satprameye ca sādhakāḥ |
śāstrāṇica purāṇāni pauruṣeyāṇi kṛtsnaśaḥ || 4.29 ||
yathārthāni praṇītāni hariṇātha surarṣibhiḥ |
nityānyevārthato varṇakramayogādanityakāḥ || 4.30 ||
sātvikāni purāṇāni manvādi smṛtayastathā |
yathārtha eva tatroktaṃ grāhyaṃ śrīharitatparaiḥ || 4.31 ||
vārāhaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ pādmaṃ vāyuproktaṃ ca gāruḍaṃ |
śrīmadbhāgavataṃ caiva sātvikānīti hi śrutiḥ || 4.32 ||
brahmāṇḍaṃ brahmavaivartaṃ mārkaṇḍeyaṃ ca vāmanaṃ |
bhaviṣyaṃ nārasiṃhaṃ ca rājasāni ṣaḍaiva hi || 4.33 ||
mātsyaṃ kaurmyaṃ tathā laiṅgyaṃ śaivaṃ skāndaṃ tathaiva ca |
pāśupatasaṃjñikaṃ ceti tāmasāni vido viduḥ || 4.34 ||
grāhyaṃ sarvaṃ sātvikoktaṃ yojanā bhedatothavā |
vedavedānusāreṣu virodhe'nyārthakalpanā || 4.35 ||
itarāṇi viruddhāni pralambhabhramajānyapi |
vyāsoktāni purāṇāni sātvikānyapi kṛtsnaśaḥ || 4.36 ||
kvacitkvacitpurāṇāni prāyojñānakarāṇyapi |
āsurāveśavaśataḥ tadanyoktānyanantaśaḥ || 4.37 ||
śrotrāgrāhyāṇi santyeva apramāṇāni tānyalaṃ |
paraśuklatrayoktāni nityaṃ viṣṇvājñayaiva tu || 4.38 ||
atipramāṇāni daityāveśājñānādi varjanāt |
badhiratvādayaḥ śrotrṛdoṣāstadvarjitendriyaiḥ(yāḥ) || 4.39 ||
etāni viṣayāṇyāhuḥ śrotavyāni sahasraśaḥ |
śabdadoṣavihīnāni grāhyāṇi manasā saha || 4.40 ||
tatroktānantasuguṇaṃ nirdeṣaṃ jñeyamīśvaraṃ |
gamyaṃ satyaṃ ca sukhacinmayaṃ viṣṇuṃ sanātanaṃ || 4.41 ||
ātmeśvaraṃ hariṃ bhaktyopā(styā)syamuktā bhavanti hi |
kuśāstreṣūktadurmārge heyatā buddhisaṃyutaṃ || 4.42 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede caturtho'dhyāyaḥ
<1.5>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
jñānendriyāṇi pañcāni pañcakarmendriyāṇi ca |
rajobhāgagatānyeṣu śrotramā(traṃ)dyaṃ viśiṣyate || 5.1 ||
śrāvaṇāsyādyabhaktestu sādhakatvādguṇādhikaṃ |
tvakcarmagolake sarvadehage samu(pāśritā)pasthitā || 5.2 ||
kuṣṭhabhaṅurapāmādyāḥ tvakdoṣāḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
avyaktādi sparśadoṣaśūnyāḥ śītoṣṇapūrvakāḥ || 5.3 ||
sparśāstadviṣayāḥ proktāḥ vāyutejombubhūmayaḥ |
padārthāḥ kecanaguṇāḥ satvagindriyagocarāḥ || 5.4 ||
tvaksparśau prakṛtisthau dvau nityau sthūlāvanityakau |
tajjanyau prākṛtau vāyusaṃśrayau naśvarau matau || 5.5 ||
indriyāṇāntu viṣayāḥ guṇāmātrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
jñānātmakānāṃ te sarve dvividhāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 5.6 ||
viṣṇuvaiṣṇavasevāyai anukūlāḥśubhā matāḥ |
aśubhāḥ pratikūlāśca punaste trividhā matāḥ || 5.7 ||
muktiyogyāśritā nityāḥ nityasṛtiṣu madhyamāḥ |
tamoyogyāśritā nīcāstattadyogyasusādhanaiḥ || 5.8 ||
nīcamadhyottamasthānaprāpakāḥ kramayogataḥ |
jīvasvarūpabhūtāni manomātrendriyāṇi ca || 5.9 ||
tatsvarūpāṇi nityāni uttameṣu śubhānyapi |
uttamā muktiyogyāstu nityabaddhāstu madhyamāḥ || 5.10 ||
adhamāstu tamoyogyāḥ manomātrādayastathā |
svārūpikāścaliṅgasthāḥ anityāḥ sthūladehagāḥ || 5.11 ||
viṣṇoramāyābrahmādi muktānāṃ kramayogataḥ |
manomātrendriyajñānasukhadhairyabalādayaḥ || 5.12 ||
svarūpāṇyevaviṣṇostu nityavyāptāni kṛtsnaśaḥ |
svatantrāṇi svatantrasya (karmāṇi tu)puruṣasya mahātmanaḥ || 5.13 ||
svasvarūpasthitau yasya jñānekarmaṇyapi sphuṭaṃ |
sarvasvarūpaceṣṭāsu parāpekṣā na yasya hi || 5.14 ||
sa svantantra iti proktaḥ sa viṣṇurnetaraḥ kvacit |
avyaktatatkāryamaya dehaśūnyāramāpi hi || 5.15 ||
taccittendriyamātrāstu nityā vyāptā svarūpakā(kāḥ) |
viṣṇvaikavaśagāstasyāḥ haryapekṣāsadasti hi || 5.16 ||
parāpekṣayutatvātsā asvatantrā prakīrtitā |
brahmādimuktāmuktānāṃ asvatantryamanāditaḥ || 5.17 ||
siddhameva svarūpe ca jñāne karmaṇyapi sphuṭaṃ |
nīcānīcasvarūpāṇāṃ uttamottamacetasāṃ || 5.18 ||
prasādataḥ pravṛttirhi jñānabhaktyādi cākhilaṃ |
jñānājñānatamomokṣāḥ nityānityajaḍasya ca || 5.19 ||
notpattināśau jīvānāṃ trividhānāṃ samastaśaḥ |
svarūpaduḥkhadveṣerṣyā cintāsantāpapūrvakāḥ || 5.20 ||
na muktānāṃ dehayogo yāvatteṣāṃ bhayādikaṃ |
bhayādīni ca yogyebhyaḥ uttameṣvalpameva ca || 5.21 ||
jñānaṃ muktirayogyasya na ca svapnepi dṛśyate |
svarūpājñānatamasī yogyānāṃ na kadācana || 5.22 ||
sṛṣṭiṃ sthitiṃ saṃhṛtiṃ ca niyatiṃ jagatosya tu |
jñānājñāne tamomokṣau viṣṇunaiva vido viduḥ || 5.23 ||
brahmādīnāṃ svarūpantu atyantāṇutamaṃ mataṃ |
tadindriyāṇi sūkṣmāṇi sūkṣmāṇyeva sthirāṇi tu || 5.24 ||
cakṣurgolakagaṃ netraṃ rūpagrāhyamatīndriyaṃ |
nityamavyaktagaṃ sthūlamanityaṃ janyamakṣi ca || 5.25 ||
svarūpanetrakalitaṃ nityānitye akṣiṇī ubhe |
jñānaprade sākṣisaṃjñe sarvasvārūpakendriyaṃ || 5.26 ||
uktānāṃ vakṣyamāṇānāṃ indriyāṇāṃ samagraśaḥ |
svārūpendriyasāhāyyaṃ vinā śaktirnavidyate || 5.27 ||
vṛddhiṃ hāniṃ ca labhate guṇairdeṣaiḥ svarūpataḥ |
sākṣīndriyantu yogyānāṃ svottamadrohatāṃ vinā || 5.28 ||
tatrāpīṣatkutaścittu tatsarvaṃ kuṇṭhitaṃ bhavet |
paṭalaṃ kāmilā kācaḥ pratidīdhiti saṅgatiḥ || 5.29 ||
netrarogaṃ vraṇādyāśca paityapratihatāstathā |
akṣibhramaṇaśaityādyāḥ netradoṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 5.30 ||
ārasthatvaṃ dūragatvaṃ saukṣmaṃ ca vyavadhānataḥ |
sthitiḥ samānābhighātaḥ arthadoṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 5.31 ||
nirdeṣaviṣayā ye ca nirdeṣekṣaṇagocarāḥ |
śuklaṃ raktaṃ śvetapītaharitādīni kṛtsnaśaḥ || 5.32 ||
citrādīni svarūpāṇi cakṣuṣo viṣayāṇi ca |
udbhūtarūpasaṃyuktāḥ tejovārbhūmayātmakā || 5.33 ||
cakṣuṣo viṣayāḥ proktāḥ kecitsūryodayo'marāḥ |
jihvā golakagaṃ prāhuḥ rasanendriyamuttamaṃ || 5.34 ||
atīndriyamarucyādyāstaddhoṣāsphoṭakādayaḥ |
kaṣāyaśca kaṭustiktaḥ lavaṇo madhurāmlakau || 5.35 ||
tathā citrarasaśceti rasanendriyagocarāḥ |
doṣahīnasya ca rasajñānaṃ janayati sphuṭaṃ || 5.36 ||
jñānaṃ janayati kṣipraṃ prākṛtaṃprakṛtisthitaṃ |
duṣṭā paryuṣitā yāmahīnā pūtiyutā rasāḥ || 5.37 ||
sadoṣā rasasambandhe rasanā jñānadā na hi |
bhūmayāṃmayā arthāḥ rasanendriyagocarāḥ || 5.38 ||
ghrāṇagolakagaṃ proktaṃ nāsikendriyamūrjitaṃ |
pīnasādyāḥ ghrāṇadoṣāḥ gandhagrāhendriyaṃ mataṃ || 5.39 ||
vyāmiśrādyāḥ doṣabhāgāḥ gandhadoṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
vaktragolakagaṃ proktaṃ vāgindriyamatīndriyaṃ || 5.40 ||
nityānityavacāṃstasya satyāsatyavacāṃsi ca |
vacāṃsi yuktāyuktāni gīrvāṇaprākṛtāni ca || 5.41 ||
mūkatvādi mahādoṣahīnavāggocarāṇi ca |
gadgadatvāspaṣṭatādyāḥ vāgdoṣāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 5.42 ||
hastagolakagaṃ prāhuḥ pāṇīndriyamatīndriyaṃ |
vyaṅgulatvādayastasya doṣāḥ nirdeṣāmindriyaṃ || 5.43 ||
gṛhṇātī(va)ha paratrāpi yaddhitaḥ svātmanaḥ sadā |
bhūmayāratnahemādyā bahvarthā viṣayāmatāḥ || 5.44 ||
aśmayāśca tathābhāvāḥ hastayorviṣayāmatāḥ |
kaṇṭakocchiṣṭapāṣāṇavṛścikādyā aniṣṭadāḥ || 5.45 ||
heyā arthāśca hastābhyāṃ parityājyāḥ sadā matāḥ |
sāniṣṭadaṃ hitakaraṃ hastayorhitadaṃ mataṃ || 5.46 ||
aṅghrigolakagaṃ prāhuḥ pādendriyamalaṃ budhāḥ |
paṅgutvādyāḥ aṅghridoṣāḥ nirdeṣagatisādhanaṃ || 5.47 ||
svārūpigaṃ prakṛtigaṃ prākṛtaṃ trividhaṃ mataṃ |
yenendriyeṇa yatkarmakriyate sādhubhiḥsadā || 5.48 ||
tattasya viṣayaḥ proktaḥ tannāmendriya gocaraṃ |
pakvāpakvaṃ ca bhuktaṃ ca vātaṃ jīrṇaṃ ca kukṣigaṃ || 5.49 ||
kālena viparītantu śakṛdityucyate budhaiḥ |
tādṛgarthotsargasaṃjño pāyorviṣayamīritaṃ || 5.50 ||
bhagandharādayo mūlavraṇādyāḥ pāyusaṃsthitāḥ |
doṣāḥ nirodhātisārāḥ pāyorna yadi kāryakṛth || 5.51 ||
strīpuṃsorbhagameḍhrādi golakasthaṃ hyatīndriyaṃ |
upasthaśiśnasaṃjñe dve indriye doṣavarjite || 5.52 ||
ratyutsṛtau prabhavataḥ asṛkśuklāśrayau matau |
mehavraṇādyāḥ taddhoṣāḥ nirdeṣaṃ svārthasādhakaṃ || 5.53 ||
prakṛtaṃ prākṛtaṃ caiva svarūpendriya saṃyutaṃ |
arthakriyākāri taddhi nānyathā bhavati dhruvaṃ || 5.54 ||
jñānendriyāṇi pañcāpi pañcakarmendriyāṇi ca |
rajaḥ paricchedagāni bhinnaśaktiyutāni ca || 5.55 ||
jñānendriyāṇāṃ viṣayāḥ kramācchabdādayomatāḥ |
śabdasparśārūparasagandhāste ca guṇāmatāḥ || 5.56 ||
mātrātamaḥ pariccheda pañcabhūtāntarāmatāḥ |
bhūtāni mātrā garbheṣu līnāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ || 5.57 ||
bhāvarūpājñānagataṃ pañcakaṃ guṇagarbhakaṃ |
mātrāsu līnabhūteṣu saṃsthitaṃ bhāvapañcakaṃ || 5.58 ||
tamomohamahāmohatāmiśrāndhatāmisrakāḥ |
tamaḥ śabdagataṃ prāhuḥ mohaḥ sparśagato mataḥ || 5.59 ||
mahāmoho rūpagataḥ tāmiśro rasagomataḥ |
gandhe andhatāmisrākhyasta ete bhāvarūpakāḥ || 5.60 ||
etāni prakṛtisthāni nityāṇyaṇutarāṇi ca |
sthūlāni bhūtavyaktāni anityānyāmananti hi || 5.61 ||
jīvasvarūpaṃ naivate tadvibhinnaṃ liṅgadehagaṃ |
jīvasvarūpavijñānācchādakaṃ parikīrtitaṃ || 5.62 ||
tatsādhanādvyañjamānasvarūpajñānabādhakaṃ |
tamaḥ svarūpajñānasya viparītapravartakaṃ || 5.63 ||
yo mohaḥ sa upadeśepi mahāmoho'nivartayet |
vidveṣakāraṇaṃ prāhuḥ tāmiśramupadeṣṭṛṣu || 5.64 ||
kuyuktibhiḥ kvāgamyaiśca gāḍhājñānaikasādhanaṃ |
andhatāmiśrasaṃjñākhyaṃ anādyajñānamīritaṃ || 5.65 ||
anādikālamārabhyānantānantāṇumātrakāḥ |
keśādyāyutatulyālpasvarūpāhyātmagocarāḥ || 5.66 ||
tattatsvarūpakākāra hariṇābimbarūpiṇā |
rakṣitāḥ pralaye sṛṣṭā nityaṃ tadanapāyinā || 5.67 ||
etatkalpādibhāvīnāṃ tattakalpeṣu vai kramāt |
sṛjyāḥ vilakṣaṇāstulyāḥ atulyāstrividhātmakāḥ || 5.68 ||
jīvāḥ anāditaḥ sarvepyasvatantrā ramāvaśāḥ |
prakṛtyātmakaliṅgena baddhāḥ śrīharikukṣigāḥ || 5.69 ||
laye sṛṣṭau hariḥsṛṣṭāḥ aṇḍādbahirathāntare |
sātvikānīcamadhyāśca antaḥ sṛjyā atātvikāḥ || 5.70 ||
niraṃśāmartyagandharvapūrvāḥ sarve tṛṇāntikāḥ |
yogyā nityasṛtisthāśca tamo yogyāśca kṛtsnaśaḥ || 5.71 ||
sarvepi liṅgasambaddhā ye te jīvā na cānyathā |
tepi pūrvānantakālpeṣvevaṃ svocita sādhanaiḥ || 5.72 ||
muktāḥ madhyāstamaḥ prāptāḥ santi śrīharigarbhagāḥ |
naiṣāṃ kvāpi kvacitpūrtirbhavitā niyamāddhareḥ || 5.73 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.6>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
niḥśeṣato rātrimāne vyatītetvaharāga(di)me |
kṣaṇe taṃ viśvasṛksraṣṭumicchāṃ cakre'khila(svayaṃ)prabhuḥ || 6.1 ||
tataḥ svagarbhagānjīvānliṅgamuktānvineśvaraḥ |
prakṛtyātmakaliṅgena śarīreṇāvṛtāstridhā || 6.2 ||
vibhinnānāṃ stamasānśuddharājasānsātvikānprabhuḥ |
svodarasthānsamudhṛtyasṛjyānpuruṣanāmakaḥ || 6.3 ||
prādātsavāsudevākhyo sarvānjīvānjagatprabhuḥ |
naiva sṛṣṭistu muktānāṃ baddhānāṃ na hi sarvaśaḥ || 6.4 ||
sṛṣṭiruktāyuktaḥ saṃkhyā niyamātsṛṣṭirucyate |
sṛṣṭiḥ saṃkhyā niyamataḥ tātvikātātvikeṣu ca || 6.5 ||
niraṃśeṣu na saṃkhyāsti bahutvātvādbahudhaiva sā |
brahmatvayogyā ṛjavo nāmato'nantaśo gaṇāḥ || 6.6 ||
teṣu pūrvānantakalpaiḥ muktāḥ santi hyanantaśaḥ |
liṅgabaddhā apyanantānantaśaḥ santi garbhagāḥ || 6.7 ||
haresteṣāṃ sarvaśopi teṣu sṛjyāstu kecana |
ṛjvādi tātvikāḥsarve anādito hyaparokṣiṇaḥ || 6.8 ||
sāmānyato viśeṣeṇa svasvayogyāparokṣiṇaḥ |
klptakālocitamahāsādhanena bhavanti hi || 6.9 ||
yatanti hyaparokṣārthaṃ ṛjavo śatasaṃmitāḥ |
pūrvāparokṣiṇaste ca śatasaṃkhyāmitāḥ sadā || 6.10 ||
anantavedoktaguṇopāsanāyāṃ hareḥ sadā |
yatanti tepi kramaśo hyupāsītaguṇoccayāḥ || 6.11 ||
bhavanti vedhāḥ sampūrṇasadguṇopāstimāniha |
kāle mukto bhavatyeva mucyate naikadaiva hi || 6.12 ||
evañca brahmamānena kalpadviśatakena ca |
pūrṇaśaktiḥ pūrṇaguṇopāstiḥ muktā bhavatyalaṃ || 6.13 ||
mukteḥ pūrvakṣaṇe bhogyasuprārabdhakṣayepi ca |
evamevottaratrāpi saṃkhyā niyamataḥ surāḥ || 6.14 ||
mucyante tātvikā vakṣyamāṇaiḥ kalpairajasya tu |
dvipañcāśatkalpamitāḥ śeṣādyāstu sabhāryakāḥ || 6.15 ||
viṃśatkalpamitāstvindrajīvāḥ dvinavakalpakāḥ |
ahaṃprāṇapadārhaśca gurvādyādvyaṣṭakalpakāḥ || 6.16 ||
pravāhastu dviṣaṭkalpaiḥ sūryādyāḥ daśakalpakāḥ |
mitrādīnāṃ ca navabhistūkta śeṣagaṇasya tu || 6.17 ||
aṣṭabhiḥ saptabhiḥ kalpaiḥ sanakādi gaṇasya tu |
ṣaḍbhiḥ parjanyamārabhya puṣkarāntagaṇasya vai || 6.18 ||
aparokṣataḥ karmadevāḥ pañcakalpāparokṣiṇaḥ |
caturbhirājānajānāntu sārdhakalpatrayeṇa tu || 6.19 ||
kṛṣṇāṅgasaṅgagopīnāṃ tribhiḥ pitṛgaṇasya tu |
gandharvāṇāntu kalpābhyāṃ martyānāṃ tu tathaikataḥ || 6.20 ||
ardhakalpaṃ tṛṇāntānāṃ yogyānāṃ haridarśane |
niyamo'yaṃsarvakalpeṣvevameva na cānyathā || 6.21 ||
klpasaṃkhyā brahmamānādvijñeyā yogyacetanaiḥ |
asurāṇāṃ tamaḥprāptiḥ brahmaṇo dinakalpataḥ || 6.22 ||
bhavatyeva jagaddhāturājñayetthaṃ sadaiva tu |
indrādipuṣkarāntānāṃ guṇopāsanayocitaiḥ || 6.23 ||
proktaiḥ kalpairvimucyante tāvatkalpaistu sādhanaṃ |
tatpūrvamaparokṣasya kartavyamiti niścayaḥ || 6.24 ||
aṇḍāntarāle padmotthabrahmā yāvaddhi tiṣṭhati |
tāvatsaṃvatsaragatapratipratidineṣvapi || 6.25 ||
sāṃśā atātvikāḥ sarve saṃkhyayā vakṣyamāṇayā |
mitāstu pratikalpe'pi padasthā hyaparokṣiṇaḥ || 6.26 ||
urvaśyādyāpsarasaḥ śatāntāṃścāṣṭasaṃkhyayā |
mitā ājānajaistulyāḥ karmadevaiḥ samāḥ parāḥ || 6.27 ||
śataṃ pitṝṇāṃ saptaiva teṣūrvaśyādibhiḥ samāḥ |
anye ājānajebhyastu nyūnāstebhyo'varāḥ kramāth || 6.28 ||
gandharvāstu śataṃ teṣu aṣṭau tulyāstu karmajaiḥ |
śatakoṭimitāḥ sarve ṛṣayo viṃśaduttamāḥ || 6.29 ||
ṛṣīṇāṃ ca śataṃ karmadevaistalyamudīritaṃ |
haryāveśāśca tanmadhye viṃśadīṣadguṇottamāḥ || 6.30 ||
teṣveva tātvikāścāṣṭau śatakaṃ karmajaiḥ samaṃ |
tadanye'jānajebhyastu tulyā agnisutā api || 6.31 ||
dvyaṣṭausahasrāṇiśatantriṃśaccāraṇarakṣasāṃ |
sādhyasiddhāstathānyāstu saptatiḥ sarvajātiṣu || 6.32 ||
ājānajāstesvanyūnaṃ pitṛgandharvasaṃyutāḥ |
pratīkālambanāḥ sāṃśāḥ niraṃśāstu tato'varāḥ || 6.33 ||
pratīkālambanasteṣu śreṣṭhāḥ mānuṣagāyakāḥ |
tato nyūnāstu rājāno martyoccāstu tato'dhamāḥ || 6.34 ||
martyagandharvarāśyādi sarvajīvagaṇeṣvapi |
ye muktiyogyāste sarve pratīkasthāvalambinaḥ || 6.35 ||
niraṃśā aparokṣātprākūrdhvamapyaṇḍa eva hi |
sṛjyānūrdhvaṃ yathā sāṃśāḥ aparokṣavivarjitāḥ || 6.36 ||
aparokṣiṇāntu sāṃśānāṃ tātvikairbhinnacetasām |
atātvikānāṃ karmādi suragandharvamānuṣāṃ || 6.37 ||
etāvadantajīvānāṃ sūkṣmāptiraniruddhataḥ |
ajotpatteḥ purājāṇḍe sthūlāptiriti niścayaḥ || 6.38 ||
tātvikānāṃ bahiścāṇḍātsūkṣmasthūlāptiriṣyate |
asaṃsṛṣṭaśarīrāste'ṇḍotpatteḥ purāḥsurāḥ || 6.39 ||
aṇḍāntarāle sarve'pi saṃsṛṣṭāḥ sthūladehakāḥ |
laye sarve liṅgabaddhāḥ śūnyakukṣā vasanti hi || 6.40 ||
teṣu muktetare saṃkhyā niyamātsṛṣṭisaṃmatāḥ |
ye ca tānnikhilānvāsudevaḥ sraṣṭumupākramath || 6.41 ||
jaḍākhyā prakṛtiḥ sarvajīvānāṃ liṅgarūpiṇī |
prakṛtergarbhagā jīvāḥ liṅgadehayutā matāḥ || 6.42 ||
anādikālamārabhya jīvāḥ saṃsṛtibandhagāḥ |
tatra pūrvānantakalpe jīvāḥ satsādhanena ca || 6.43 ||
miśreṇa viparītena jñānenātyaktabandhanāḥ |
jīvāśca trividhā āsanśatakoṭisahasraśaḥ || 6.44 ||
sṛjyeṣu liṅgabaddheṣu pūrvasṛjyaścaturmukhaḥ |
na caikakāle sarveṣāṃ sṛṣṭiḥ sā kālabhedataḥ || 6.45 ||
bhavatyataḥ pūrvajātāḥ uttamānye tato'varāḥ |
layākhya parakālānte vyatīte sṛṣṭisaṃmate || 6.46 ||
parakālādime'jasya sṛṣṭiranyasya kālataḥ |
brahmaṇaḥ sṛṣṭitaḥ paścādvāyoḥ sṛṣṭiḥ śatābdataḥ || 6.47 ||
tataḥ śatābdato vāṇyāḥ bhāratyāstacchatābdataḥ |
tataḥ sahasrābdataśca sṛṣṭistu vipaśeṣayoḥ || 6.48 ||
nīlādīnāṃ sahasraśca vatsaraistāvatā punaḥ |
vāruṇyāderdaśasāhasrābdatastu tataḥ paraṃ || 6.49 ||
sṛṣṭirindrasya kāmasyāpyayutābdāstathāpare |
sṛṣṭāsyurhariṇā ye ca nīcāste'nuttamottamaiḥ || 6.50 ||
puṣkarāntāstātvikāśca tathaivātātvikāḥ pare |
niraṃśā nityabaddhāśca tamoyogyāśca rākṣasāḥ || 6.51 ||
ayutābdāddhevamānātpūrvapūrvavyavasthayā |
sṛṣṭirbhavati sā sūkṣmasthūlabhedādvidhā matā || 6.52 ||
tātvikānāṃ padasthānāṃ sarveṣāmaparokṣiṇāṃ |
aṇḍātpūrvaiva bhavati tadanyeṣāṃ tadantare || 6.53 ||
aṇḍādbahirathāṇḍāntarjāyamānajanasya tu |
pūrvoktakālaniyamātsṛṣṭiḥ jñeyā yathākramaṃ || 6.54 ||
liṅgabaddhasya tu yadā sūkṣmadehāptiriṣyate |
tataḥ pūrvakṣaṇe liṅge guṇavaiṣamyamiṣyate || 6.55 ||
liṅgadehasya madhyastho rajobhāgaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
bhūrnāmakendirā tatra ceṣṭakā tasya māninī || 6.56 ||
satvabhāgaspṛṣṭarajaḥ pārśvagā rājasāṇavaḥ |
daśapralayakāle tu bhūmyā satvasya pārśvagāḥ || 6.57 ||
bhavanti layakālānte tatra tiṣṭhanti sarvaśaḥ |
rājasāṇuḥ sātvikāṃśagatastvekaikaśaḥ pṛthak || 6.58 ||
daśaiva tādṛśā ete militā dvādaśāṇavaḥ |
sātvikeṣu śatamitānraktavarṇānkarotyalaṃ || 6.59 ||
svabhāvataḥsatvabhāgāḥ śuklāste satvasaṃsthitāḥ |
raktāsahasrasaṃkhyākāḥ daśabhīrājasāṇubhiḥ || 6.60 ||
bhavanti ca rajaḥ spṛṣṭāḥ satvapārśvagatā laye |
evameko rājasāṇuḥ rājasāṃśāpasavyagaṃ || 6.61 ||
tāmasāṃśa bhuvānītaḥ svaśatāṃśantu tāmasaṃ |
atyalpaṃ nīlavarṇaṃ te raktoraktī karotyalaṃ || 6.62 ||
ekaḥ śuddho rājasāṃśaḥ paricchede tu rājase |
madhye tiṣṭhantyapi laye rājasāṇu samā ime || 6.63 ||
tamaḥ paricchedagatāḥ tāmasāṇustu yādṛśāḥ |
laye satvaparicchedapārśvagā rājasāṇavaḥ || 6.64 ||
tāmasāstādṛśā ete militā dvādaśāṇavaḥ |
rājasā tāmasātyalpa paramāṇu pramāṇataḥ || 6.65 ||
adhikaṃ guṇitāstatra śatadvādaśasaṃmitāḥ |
daśarājasasaṃyukta sahasrasātvikāṇavaḥ || 6.66 ||
lakṣābhavanti kāryopayuktā ete bhavanti hi |
tāmasāttu paricchedādviguṇo rājasasmṛtaḥ || 6.67 ||
rājasāttu paricchedā dviguṇaḥsātvikaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ekaikasmanparicchedepyanantāḥ paramāṇavaḥ || 6.68 ||
santi tatra niyantājo viṣṇubrahmaśivātmakaḥ |
nārāyaṇonantaguṇaḥ śrī strīrūpābhimāninī || 6.69 ||
jaḍāyāṃ prakṛtau cāpi liṅgadehe tathaiva ca |
santyānantāṇavasteṣu pūrvoktāḥ kāryasādhakāḥ || 6.70 ||
satvaṅgatā rājasāṃśāḥ laye rājasasātvikān |
vadanti tāmasagataṃ rajorājasatāmasaṃ || 6.71 ||
rajaḥ paricchedagataṃ santo rājasatāmasaṃ |
vadantyeva laye yogo bhavati śrīharīcchayā || 6.72 ||
sāmyāvasthāṃ budhāḥ prāhuḥ etāṃ prakṛtiliṅgayoḥ || 6.73 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ
<1.7>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
nārīkelaphalākāro liṅgadehastrivṛnmataḥ |
mūrdhnicchidrayuto garbhāvakāśakalitaḥ sadā || 7.1 ||
antarnibaddhā jīvāste trividhā api mocane |
na śaktaḥ ramayāścanna mūrdhnicchidrāvasantyalaṃ || 7.2 ||
duḥkhaspṛṣṭā liṅgabaddhāḥ duḥkhāspṛṣṭā ramaiva hi |
yasmannakāle yasya sūkṣmaḥ nāśo bhavati viṣṇunā || 7.3 ||
aniruddhena tannīca kramatonḍāntare bahiḥ |
taduttarakṣaṇe liṅgadehe ca guṇasāmyatā || 7.4 ||
bhūmyākṛtā bhavati sā (svarūpajñānarūpiṇī)jīvānāṃ jñānarodhinī |
svārūpikajñānasukhe prayatnecchāḥ satāṃ laye || 7.5 ||
ruddhā bhavanti sāmye tu vaiṣamye vyaktimañjasā |
yāntyavasthādvayaṃ liṅge laye sṛṣṭau kramātkila || 7.6 ||
kṣaṇakṣaṇena bhramatāṃ sa tāṃ savyena cānyathā |
bhramatāṃ liṅgadehasthaguṇatrayasuyogataḥ || 7.7 ||
sṛṣṭikāle tridhābhinnā jīvānāṃ buddhiriṣyate |
sātvikī rājasī ceti tāmasīti samastaśaḥ || 7.8 ||
yogyānāṃ sātvikībuddhiḥ bahulā anye'lpake mate |
madhyamānāntu jīvānāṃ rājasī tāmasī matiḥ || 7.9 ||
kramātbahutare'nyetu svalpamātre bhaviṣyataḥ |
pañcamātrāgatājñānapañcakaṃ liṅgadehagaṃ || 7.10 ||
anādyajñānamuddiṣṭaṃ bāhyamasvārūpikaṃ tu tat |
sākṣīndriyāṇāṃ (tamasā)manasā yoge taddhi bhaviṣyati || 7.11 ||
sākṣīndriyāṇāṃ satvasthamanasā saṅgamo yadi |
bāhyā svārūpikañjñānaṃ trividhānāṃ citāmiha || 7.12 ||
sākṣīndriyāṇāṃ rajasā manasā saṅgamo yadi |
bāhyamasvārūpikaṃ ca kāmakrodhādikaṃ ca yath || 7.13 ||
anādikāmo jīvānāṃ trividhānāṃ bhaviṣyati |
rajaḥ paricchedagatāḥ jñānendriyakalāḥ yadi || 7.14 ||
sākṣīndriyayutābāhyaśabdādīcchā bhaviṣyati |
anādikāmaḥsā proktā trividhānāṃ citāmiha || 7.15 ||
rajaḥ paricchedagatāḥ karmendriyakalāḥ yadi |
sākṣīndriyutāḥ karmajanayantyapyanādikaṃ || 7.16 ||
bāhyaliṅgagataṃ karma na tu svārūpikaṃ mataṃ |
anādyavidyākāmau cānādi karma ca liṅgagaṃ || 7.17 ||
madīyamitimatvā tu baddho bhavati cetanaḥ |
kālena bhārate varṣe labdhajanmāpi mānuṣaṃ || 7.18 ||
brāhmaṇyaṃ sadguroḥ saṅgaṃ bhaktijñānedhigamya ca |
prasādātśrīhareḥ karma devatānāmathāntare || 7.19 ||
aṇḍādbahistātvikānāṃ vaikuṇṭhaparighālaye |
aṇḍakharparasaṃspṛṣṭa śrībhāgevā harīcchayā || 7.20 ||
parāntyavṛttiparitaḥ sthitevāri nimajya ca |
abhivyaktasvarūpāṇāṃ satāṃ liṅgaviparyaye || 7.21 ||
harīramāsurādveṣādbhārate martyajanmani |
duḥsaṅgavṛddhātaṇḍāntarliṅgadehasya nāśane || 7.22 ||
sativāyorgadāghātādasatāṃ kālayogataḥ |
anādyavidyākāmau cānādikarmāpi naśyati || 7.23 ||
ato bāhyaṃ na svarūpā avidyādyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
jñānājñāne svarūpasthe nitye naiva ca naśvare || 7.24 ||
anādyavidyākāmādyāḥ sāmyāvasthā yadā bhavet |
tadā jīvasya sarvasya na bhavanti kadācana || 7.25 ||
rājasāṇuśatāṃśābhāḥ dviṣaṭkāstāmasāṇavaḥ |
laye teṣu rajasyekaḥ satvapārśvagatādaśa || 7.26 ||
bhavantyekastamo madhye saṃsthito bhavati dhruvaṃ |
rajogatoṇustamasaḥ sato dviguṇarājasaṃ || 7.27 ||
rājasāṇuśatāṃśābhāḥ satvagāstāmasāṇavaḥ |
daśāpidaśatotkṛṣṭānsātvikāṇūnlaye dhruvaṃ || 7.28 ||
nīlīkurvantyevameṣā sāmyāvasthā prakīrtitā |
tāmasī rājasī ceti sāmyāvasthā dvidhā matā || 7.29 ||
sāmyāvasthā sātvike tu na kvāpi kila vidyate |
dvābhyāntu sāmyavasthābhyāṃ liṅgastābhyāṃ janasya tu || 7.30 ||
laye sarvasya tāṃ nidrāṃ nāśaṃ prāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ |
yadā yogecchoparamaḥ tadā jñāpayati prabhuḥ || 7.31 ||
ayogyecchā prayatnādi karturāśāṃ chinatti ca |
vaiṣamye sati liṅge tu jananecchā hi jāyate || 7.32 ||
yadecchā jāyate jantostadā sṛjati taṃ prabhuḥ |
sṛjyānāṃ sarvajīvānāṃ aṇḍādbahi rathāntare || 7.33 ||
naikadā viṣamāvasthā hyajādīnāṃ kramādbhavet |
vyutkramātpūrvamānena aṇḍāntarbahireva ca || 7.34 ||
sāmyāvasthā ca nīcānāṃ prākpaścāduttamasya ca |
laye satvapraviṣṭā ye daśate rājasāṇavaḥ || 7.35 ||
svaśatādhikasatvasthaparamāṇu samanvitāḥ |
sṛṣṭikāle śriyānunnā rajo bhāgaṃ viśanti hi || 7.36 ||
tamaḥ paricchedagata rājasāṇurlaye tu yaḥ |
svaśatāṃśaṃ tāmasāṇuṃ gṛhītvā durgayeritaḥ || 7.37 ||
rajobhāgaṃ praviśanti sṛṣṭikāle samāgate |
laye rajobhāgamadhyagatoṇū rājasasya yaḥ || 7.38 ||
rajo madhye vyaktimeti sṛṣṭikāla upāgate |
prakṛtau liṅgadeho'pi evaṃ yadi guṇasthitiḥ || 7.39 ||
sāmyāvasthāṃ jaguḥ prājñāḥ tayā sṛṣṭirbhaviṣyati |
laye satvapraviṣṭhā ye daśaitattāmasāṇavaḥ || 7.40 ||
rājasāṇu śatāṃśābhāḥ sātvikaiḥ svasamāṇubhiḥ |
yuktāstāmasa bhāgaṃ hi viśanti śrīsamīritāḥ || 7.41 ||
rajoviṣṭastāmasāṇuḥ pūrvāṇu(satveṇa)tulitaḥ svayaṃ |
svato(satvācca) dviguṇenaiva saṃyukto rājasāṇunā || 7.42 ||
tatra sthitvā laye bhūyaḥ sṛṣṭikāla upāgate |
bhuvānunnaḥ tamobhāgaṃ viśanti śrīharīcchayā || 7.43 ||
ekaśuddhastāmasāṇuḥ pūrvaistāmasasātvikaiḥ |
daśābhiścaikatamasā tathā rājasatāmasaṃ || 7.44 ||
prāpyasaṃmilitāḥsarve tamo vaiṣamyanāmakaṃ |
sahasrasātvikāṇubhyaḥ daśarājasakāṇubhiḥ || 7.45 ||
śuddhena rajasaikena rajasāyukta tāmasaḥ |
aṇuryadaikībhavati rajo vaiṣamya ucyate || 7.46 ||
rajo vaiṣamyataḥ sṛṣṭiḥ tamo vaiṣamyato layaḥ || 7.47 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede saptamo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.8>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
vyatīte layakāle tu sṛṣṭikāla upāgate |
parakālādimādau tu puruṣaḥ sarvacetanān || 8.1 ||
vāsudeverpayalliṅgabaddhānjīvān(sṛjyān)vilakṣaṇān |
tāratamyayutānsvayogyatāṃ kalitāṃśca saḥ || 8.2 ||
svasvocitamahāyatnābhimukhānsvasvakarmaṇā |
janmalābhocitānvāsudevasthānvīkṣya cintayan || 8.3 ||
sṛṣṭikālādyanimiṣasūkṣmadehocitaṃ hyajāṃ |
liṅgabaddhantu tatpūrvakṣaṇavaiṣamyasaṃgataḥ || 8.4 ||
ekaṃ gṛhītvānyajīvānprādātsaṅkarṣaṇo vibhuḥ |
sa vāyumekaṃ liṅge taṃ gṛhītvānyānsucetanān || 8.5 ||
pradyumro'dāttayorbhārye sa gṛhītvā saliṅgake |
tadanyāntātvikānsarvapadasthānbhāvitātvikān || 8.6 ||
vyaktāparokṣiṇaḥ saṃkhyā niyamātsūkṣmayogataḥ |
sraṣṭuṃ prādātsacarame aniruddho'khileśvaraḥ || 8.7 ||
vāsudevo viriñcasya liṅgadehaṃ vyacālayat |
tattaliṅgadehasthatriguṇebhyastribindavaḥ || 8.8 ||
nyapatantatra yobinduḥ sātvikaḥ sa tu nirmalaḥ | |
rajo vaiṣamyasaṃyukta rajobindustathāpatath || 8.9 ||
tamo vaiṣamyakalitastamobindurabhūttataḥ |
rajobindugato viṣṇūrajovaiṣamyamūrjitaṃ || 8.10 ||
pṛthaksthāpya rajobinduṃ śuddhaṃ cakre jagadguruḥ |
tamobindugatodevaḥ tamovaiṣamyamutkaṭaṃ || 8.11 ||
samudhṛtya pṛthaksthāpya taṃ binduṃ śuddhamātanot |
tato māyā prārthitaḥ sanvāsudevastadādimaḥ || 8.12 ||
tadrajoguṇavaiṣamyaliṅgabaddhamajaṃ vibhuḥ |
ajasya sūkṣmadehāptyai rajovaiṣamyamūrjitaṃ || 8.13 ||
nikṣipya ca nigīryeśomāyāyāṃ mahadāhvayaṃ |
sūkṣmarūpaṃ tu tadvīryaṃ prākṛtaṃ prākṣipaddhariḥ || 8.14 ||
tatra sā suṣuve sūkṣmadehavantamajaṃ vibhuṃ |
guṇatrayāṇavaḥ pūrvaṃ sāṃkhyā niyamataḥ sthitāḥ || 8.15 ||
yāvantaste sadā nityāḥ teṣāṃ nāśo na vidyate |
tatra satvāṇavaḥ sarve daśadhopacitāḥ sadā || 8.16 ||
bhavanti rājasāstāmasāṇavaḥ pādamātrataḥ |
bhavantyupacitā vāyusarasvatyādi sūkṣmagāḥ || 8.17 ||
satvādayastu daśadhā ūrjitāḥsambhavanti hi |
trṛṇādīnāṃ sūkṣmagatānadhikāḥ prabhavanti hi || 8.18 ||
satvāṇavo rājasāśca tāmasāhyaṇavaśca ye |
ṛjūnāṃ sūkṣmagāḥ pādopacitāḥ prabhavanti hi || 8.19 ||
sāṃśāḥsarveṣu sāṃśeṣu sūkṣmadeheṣu kṛtsnaśaḥ |
satvāṇavo daśādhikyaṃ yāntyevopacitau satāṃ || 8.20 ||
rajastamāṇavastūcceṣvalpanīceṣu cādhikāḥ |
tadrajoguṇavaiṣamyamādāyādyānujaḥ svayaṃ || 8.21 ||
sarveṣāṃ sūkṣmadehānāṃ ante vasati nāśakṛt |
satvaṃrajastamaḥ sṛṣṭaṃ viṣṇubrahmaśivātmakaṃ || 8.22 ||
śrībhūdurgāpatirviśvataijasaprājñanāmakāḥ |
śuklaraktātmikānīlā ādāyānantarūpakaḥ || 8.23 ||
tattatsūkṣmaśarīrasthasthānatrayagato hariḥ |
tadā hyavasthā tritayaṃ tadviśeṣādime sukhaṃ || 8.24 ||
ekonaviṃśatimukhau viśvataijasanāmakau |
savyāpasavyagānyevaṃ puruṣāsyānimadhyagaṃ || 8.25 ||
gajābhaṃ tu tayorasyāḥ mukhānāṃ kṛtyamapyuta |
dakṣiṇākṣisthito viśvaḥ satvasthaḥ satvabhāgataḥ || 8.26 ||
uttamānāṃ madhyamānāṃ nīcānāṃ tatvamānināṃ |
tribhirmukhaiḥ satvagataiḥ avasthāṃ jāgradāhvayāṃ || 8.27 ||
karotyayaṃ madhyagākhya tritayena sa eva hi |
uccamadhyāvarāmartyadāsānāṃ jāgradāhvayāṃ || 8.28 ||
karotyavasthāmantasthaiḥ tribhirāsyaiḥ sa eva hi |
niraṃśānāṃ ca jīvānāṃ jāgratāṃ sampravartakaḥ || 8.29 ||
puṃsāmevaṃ vāmagāsyaiḥ strīṇāmevaṃ kariṣyati |
anantānantajanmoru martyadehaiḥ kṛtāni tu || 8.30 ||
jāgraddhaśāyāṃ manasā vācakā yena cendriyaiḥ |
kṛtāni sātvikādīni karmāṇyucyāvacāni ca || 8.31 ||
yāni yānyapi sarvāṇi saṃcintānyapi teṣvalaṃ |
naṣṭeṣu tadadṛṣṭāni liṅgāni nivasantyalaṃ || 8.32 ||
teṣu bhogāya dattāni bhuktvā nāśaṃ prayānti hi |
bhogāya ca pradattāni ardhabhuktāni sarvaśaḥ || 8.33 ||
prārabdhāni ca teṣveva kānicidbhogataḥ kṣayaṃ |
yānti prārabdhaśiṣṭāni aparokṣe satisphuṭaṃ || 8.34 ||
na nayānti ca sarvāṇi bhogayogyānya saṃśayaḥ |
tatra duṣprārabdhajātaṃ bahvalpaṃ karotyajaḥ || 8.35 ||
bhagavadbhakti yogena harericchā hi tādṛśī |
hariṇā jīvabhogāya na dattāni hyanantaśaḥ || 8.36 ||
rāśīkṛtāni liṅgasthakarmādṛṣṭāni koṭiśaḥ |
sañcitānyapi tānīha aparokṣe sati sphuṭaṃ || 8.37 ||
dagdhānyapi vinaṣṭāni bhavantyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
martyo jīvanbhāratesminnityaṃ koṭisahasraśaḥ || 8.38 ||
karoti vivaśo baddhaḥ prakṛtyātānyanantaśaḥ |
ananta janmanānantānantāyuḥ parimāṇataḥ || 8.39 ||
kṛtānyabhuṅktasvakṛtakarmaṇāṃ bhogato layaḥ |
sañcitānāmanantoru brahmakālairapi kvacith || 8.40 ||
kvāpi no ghaṭate bhoktuṃ kintu śrīviṣṇudarśane |
naiṣāṃ phalaṃ hi jīvānāṃ bhavedityājñayā hareḥ || 8.41 ||
likhitānyapi patreṣu citraguptaiḥ punaḥ punaḥ |
ramābrahmādayo devāḥ sarvakarmābhimāninaḥ || 8.42 ||
yacchanti na phalaṃ puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ vā tasya karhicit |
cchitvā likhitapatrāṇi yamadūtairhariḥ priyaiḥ || 8.43 ||
bodhayantyapi he jīva na te sañcitato bhayaṃ |
iti kāraṇato nāśaṃ sañcitasya vadanti hi || 8.44 ||
ghaṭādināśavannāśaḥ karmaṇāṃ naiva śasyate |
suprārabdhasya sarvasya bhogādeva parikṣayaḥ || 8.45 ||
aparokṣānantarantu kṛtaṃ karma śubhāśubhaṃ |
āgāmītyuditaḥ sadbhirnatalopo bhaviṣyati || 8.46 ||
na likhanti paṭe citraguptāstāni surottamāḥ |
yacchanti na phalaṃ teṣāmato na śliṣyate janaḥ || 8.47 ||
āgāmikarmabhistaccāniṣṭaṃ kāmyaṃ hi rāja(kṣa)sāḥ |
gṛhṇantīṣṭaṃ kāmyapuṇyaṃ tadiṣṭāḥ prāpnuvanti hi || 8.48 ||
āgāmi viṣṇudveṣādi kalirgṛhṇāti netaraḥ |
āgāmi bhaktito mukto prāptavyānandamaśnute || 8.49 ||
vedoktamapyavedoktaṃ jñānājñānādibhiḥ kṛtaṃ |
bālyayauvanavārdhikaiḥ kṛtaṃ vākkāyamānasaiḥ || 8.50 ||
bhītyā prītyā āveśato vā kṛtaṃ karma śubhāśubhaṃ |
jñāyanneva karotyeṣaḥ bhuṅkte jāgṛddhi tatphalaṃ || 8.51 ||
samasta bhūtale vāpi vivareṣvantarikṣake |
svargādi sarvalokeṣu brahmāṇḍādbahirantare || 8.52 ||
niraye vā tamasi vā vaikuṇṭhādiṣu vā janaḥ |
jāgradbhuṅkte karmaphalaṃ viśvādhīnaḥ sadaiva hi || 8.53 ||
ānantāsanavaikuṇṭhaśvetadvīpeṣu saṃsthitāḥ |
maharādiṣu satyāntalokeṣvaṇḍādbahistathā || 8.54 ||
tātvikāḥ svādhikāreṣu niyatā netare janāḥ |
svaprārabdhaphalaṃ yānti te sarve hyaparokṣiṇaḥ || 8.55 ||
atātvikā niraṃśānāṃ yogyānāṃ kvāpi karhicit |
nādhikārastvayogyānāṃ kiṃ vācyaṃ duḥkhabhogināṃ || 8.56 ||
riyuḥ trīnkarmaṇā lokānjñānenaiva taduttarān |
tatra makhyā hariṃ yānti tadanye vāyumeva tu || 8.57 ||
apakvā ye na te yānti vāyuṃ vā harimeva vā |
sthānamātrāśritāste tu punarjani vivarjitāḥ || 8.58 ||
jñānagamyeṣu lokeṣu aṇḍāntarmaharādiṣu |
atātvikaniraṃśānāṃ muktāntānāṃ kramonnatāṃ || 8.59 ||
vāsaḥ prārabdhapuṇyena niṣkāmo na bhaviṣyati |
ātātvikaniraṃśānāṃ sargamartyādhameṣvapi || 8.60 ||
sa tātvikānāṃ prarabdhātpuṇyātpāpācca saṃcitāt |
bhogadattātpuṇyapāpātsukhaduḥkhādi śasyate || 8.61 ||
tiryagyoniṣu garbheṣu vāsebhyeṣāṃ prakalpyate |
niraṃśanīcasāṃśānāṃ narake vāsa iṣyate || 8.62 ||
prārabdhataḥ sañcitādvā na yogyānāṃ tamogatiḥ |
ayogyānāṃ dveṣapākāttamaḥ prāptirbhaviṣyati || 8.63 ||
devagandharvaparyantāḥ devaśabdoditāḥ kramāt |
devānāṃ nirayo naiva tamaścāpi kathañcana || 8.64 ||
nā surāṇāṃ tathā muktiḥ kadācitkenacitkvacit |
bhāratādyaṣṭavarṣeṣu saptadvīpeṣu cādriṣu || 8.65 ||
sarveṣāṃ pāpapuṇyaiśca mukhyaprārabdhasañcayaiḥ |
tamo mithyājñānayogātjñānenaiva hareḥ padaṃ || 8.66 ||
teṣāṃ prāgukta karmottha phalaṃ vai jāgratā bhavet |
jāgratpravartako viśvaḥ so'ja dakṣākṣigo bhaveth || 8.67 ||
liṅgottha satvamādāya tatsūkṣmastha tathā caran |
na dhātuḥ sañcitaṃ kāmyaṃ na dṛṣṭaṃ pātakādikaṃ || 8.68 ||
na dainyāveśaduḥkhādi na saṃśayaviparyayau |
na duricchānānyaratiḥ na bhogecchā kusaṅgatiḥ || 8.69 ||
na dehābhimatirnāsatpathe dehendriyodgatiḥ |
niṣkāmyakarmasajñānasadbhaktiṣu sadāratiḥ || 8.70 ||
brahmaṇo hi parasyāsya prasādātsarvadāsukhaṃ |
svapnanidrāvyādhipūrvaṃ tacca duḥkhaphalaṃ tyajeth || 8.71 ||
sontaraṅgo hareḥ sākṣātsarvotkṛṣṭa priyo mataḥ |
pakvaśeṣādibhirdṛśyamāparokṣeṇa keśavaṃ || 8.72 ||
anādikālamārabhya ajajīvagaṇāḥ sadā |
paśyantyātmocitahareraparokṣāya janmabhiḥ || 8.73 ||
yatanteśatakalpāntyaiḥ dṛṣṭveśaṃ śatakalpakaiḥ |
kṛtayānantavedokta guṇopāsanayā padaṃ || 8.74 ||
prāpnoti tasya sūkṣmāpirvāsudevāttadābhavat |
taddhehaliṅgajaṃ śuddhaṃ raja ādāya tejasaḥ || 8.75 ||
tatsūkṣmakaṇṭhe vasati svapnakṛdbhagavānhariḥ |
ekādaśendriyaiḥ karmakartavyamiti vāsanā || 8.76 ||
manasyutpadyate tacca na karoti prayatnataḥ |
nidrājñānonmādabhayarogāyāsā vidunmataiḥ || 8.77 ||
anukūlā bhāvatāva(bhavatyeva) janmajanmāntareṣvapi |
akṛtānāṃ karmaṇāntu svapnaḥ(pna)syeśaphalapradaḥ || 8.78 ||
svapnadṛṣṭā anantārthāstatkāla sukhaduḥkhadāḥ |
na jāgradṛṣṭipathagāḥ kintu tatkāla kalpitāḥ || 8.79 ||
svapne svasthasya jīvasya vāsanānantyamīśvaraḥ |
upādānīkṛtyamukhyaiḥ pūrvoktaireva taijasaḥ || 8.80 ||
kalpayitvā bahūnarthāndarśayatyakhileśvaraḥ |
svapnaḥ satyastato yasmātbhāvīnaphalasūcakaḥ || 8.81 ||
śeṣādisarvajīvānāṃ yogyakāle tathā caran |
taijasaḥ svapnakṛcceti janasya svapnakṛdbhaveth || 8.82 ||
tattamoguṇamādāya prājñastasūkṣmahṛdgataḥ |
jīvasya liṅgabaddhasya pādasthasya jagadguroḥ || 8.83 ||
dhyānāvasthāṃ dadātyevamekāsyo dehvayonitaḥ |
liṅgabaddhaṃ grasannardhaṃ mūrchāvasthāṃ prayacchati || 8.84 ||
liṅgabaddhaṃ mukhesthāpya prājñotyalponyarūpataḥ |
yadāśliṣyati taṃ devaḥ tadā nidrāṃ prayacchati || 8.85 ||
prājño jīveśvarastaṃ vai vinā jīvokṣamaḥ smṛtaḥ |
prājñaḥ kaṇṭhasthitaṃ prāpya jīvānāṃ svapnado bhaveth || 8.86 ||
jīvaṃ gṛhītvā dakṣākṣigatosau jaga(jāgṛ)dīśvaraḥ |
na prājñatejasau dhātuḥ svapnanidrā pravartakau(kā ?) || 8.87 ||
dhyānāvasthāṃ vinā tasya tattadduṣkarma varjanāt |
prājñaṃ jīveśvaraṃ prāhuḥ sa sarvahṛdisaṅgataḥ || 8.88 ||
sāṃśajīvaṃ sa vai prājño rūpaiṇyaikena pādayoḥ |
hṛdisaṃsthāpya sūkṣmānyarūpeṇādāya cetanaṃ || 8.89 ||
saliṅgaṃ sa yadā viśve naikībhūto bhaviṣyati |
soṇuḥ prājñohṛdisthena ekībhūto bhavedyadi || 8.90 ||
dhyānāvasthāṃ sañjanayatyapi jīvapatiprabhuḥ |
viśvādayo niraṃśānāṃ avasthā tritayapradāḥ || 8.91 ||
vinā ṛjūnsarvasurānbhuvijātānjagadguruḥ |
avasthātrayasaṃyuktānkurvanti prājñapūrvakāḥ || 8.92 ||
ūrdhvalokasthitānāntu sadā jāgṛtpravartakaḥ |
evaṃ sūkṣmaśarīreṇa brahmāṇamasṛjadvibhuḥ || 8.93 ||
evaṃ saṅkarṣaṇo vāyoḥ jayāyāṃ sūkṣmadehakṛt |
vāsudevaḥ sarasvatyāḥ brahmāṇamakarotpatiṃ || 8.94 ||
saṅkarṣaṇastu bhāratyāḥ mukhyaprāṇaṃ vyadhātpatiṃ |
sarasvatyāśca bhāratyāḥ pradyumnaḥ sūkṣmadehakṛth || 8.95 ||
tayostābhyāṃ sūkṣmatanū vipaśeṣau babhūvatuḥ |
sūtranāmā tadāvāyuḥ brahmā puruṣanāmakaḥ || 8.96 ||
vedādi sarvanityānāṃ arthānāṃ jīvasantateḥ |
nityānāmapi sṛṣṭau hi tāvubhāvābhimāninau || 8.97 ||
te bhārye tau vinākaścanārthopi sthitimarhati |
brahmā nirabhimānitvātśarīryapyaśarīravān || 8.98 ||
aśarīro vāyurabhraṃ vidyudityādi nāmabhiḥ |
paraśuklatrayaṃ vācyaṃ yogātte sarvamāninaḥ || 8.99 ||
athāniruddhaḥ kālena brahmavāyvoḥ sabhāryayoḥ |
tricatustvekaviṃśatyavāraṃ sūkṣmaśarīrakṛth || 8.100 ||
trivāramekaviṃśatsavāraṃ sūkṣma(tanupradaḥ)tanuṃ pradāt |
aniruddho vipādīnāṃ nīlādīnāṃ tathaiva ca || 8.101 ||
sauparṇyādi trayāṇāñca kāladeśakrameṇa ca |
indrādi puṣkarāntānāṃ tātvikānāṃ aśeṣataḥ || 8.102 ||
ekaviṃśativārantu aniruddhastu sūkṣmakṛt |
yadā yasya ca sūkṣmāptistatpūrvakṣaṇa eva ca || 8.103 ||
tattajjīvagaṇasyāsu liṅgavaiṣamyamityapi |
pañcasaptabhirmāsairbāhyamānena sarvaśaḥ || 8.104 ||
tātvikānāṃ tu devānāṃ sūkṣmasṛṣṭirabhūddhareḥ |
tato sa satvatamasaḥ vaiṣamyāvasthayāyutāṃ || 8.105 ||
jaḍākhyāṃ prakṛtiṃ samyakceṣṭayāmāsa viśvasṛṭ |
tato bindutrayaṃ jātaṃ satvaṃ ceti rajastamaḥ || 8.106 ||
ūrdhvadeśe satvabinduḥ rajobindustatopyadhaḥ |
tadadhobhūttamo binduḥ sthūlaścādyāstribindavaḥ || 8.107 ||
śrībhūdurgeti rūpā mā kramātriguṇamāninī |
viṣṇubrahmāśivaśceti bindukramaniyāmakāḥ || 8.108 ||
brahmavāyū sabhāryau dvau trirūpāvabhimāninau |
satvādibinduṣu śrīśca śritā naśvareṣu vai || 8.109 ||
rajastamo bindugatavaiṣamye jagadīśvaraḥ |
brahmā saṅkarṣaṇātmā tu samudhṛtya rarakṣatuḥ || 8.110 ||
tato viṣṇuḥ satvabindugataḥ saṃvardhayatyajaḥ |
satvāvaraṇamādyaṃ ca cakre tatra vṛtodyamaṃ || 8.111 ||
rajovindugato brahmanāmakastadadhastane |
deśe dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ rājasasyaiva nāmakaṃ || 8.112 ||
tamobindusthito rudrastāmasāvaraṇaṃ vyadhāt |
taddhi dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ rajasasyaiva garbhagaṃ || 8.113 ||
viṣṇubrahmaśivau dvau dvau brahmavāyū sabhāryakau |
śrībhūdurgāsvādyamādya tṛtīyāvaraṇeṣu hi || 8.114 ||
tattadātmasthūladehatriguṇeṣvabhimāninau |
ādyaṃ śuklaṃ dvitīyaṃ tu raktaṃ nīlaṃ tṛtīyakaṃ || 8.115 ||
rajoguṇasyavaiṣamyā triguṇāvaraṇāntare |
mahadāvaraṇaṃ cakre brahmanāmā sa bhūpatiḥ || 8.116 ||
caturthāvaraṇaṃ tatra viṣṇubrahmaśivātmakaḥ |
jātābhyāṃ brahmavāyubhyāṃ jatau dvau dvau trirūpakau || 8.117 ||
sabhāryau brahmavāyū ca mahattatvābhimāninau |
mahattatvaniyanteśaḥ ānandākhyo hariḥ svayaṃ || 8.118 ||
ānandanāmnastanayau brahmavāyū mahadgatau |
rajoguṇasya vaiṣamyātsarvāṃśena mataḥ kalāḥ || 8.119 ||
rājasāṃśenendriyāṇāṃ daśānāṃ ca kalāstathā |
tāmasāṃśena tanmātrāḥ kalāḥ pañcamahadgatāḥ || 8.120 ||
abhavantāḥ kalā nityaṃ ṣoḍaśāpi sadātanaḥ |
mahattatvādvikurvāṇādbrahmaṇaḥ sthūladehataḥ || 8.121 ||
ānandavaśagājjātastvahaṅkārastrivṛnmataḥ |
vaikārikastaijasaśca tāmasaścetyahaṃ tridhā || 8.122 ||
udyadraviprabhāvatvānmahatāhaṃ trivarṇakaḥ |
vaikārikastu satvāṃśajātaḥ śukla udahṛtaḥ || 8.123 ||
taijaso rājasāṃśotthaḥ raktavarṇaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
mano hṛṣīkāṇi mātrāḥ pūrvaṃ sūkṣmasvarūpataḥ || 8.124 ||
mahattatvagatāhyetā ahaṅkārāśritāḥ kramāt |
gāyatrīśo vidhiḥ satve viriñcastu rajoguṇe || 8.125 ||
sāvitrīśo bhavatyaddhā tamasi brahmanāmakaḥ |
sarasvatīśo vasati tādṛgrūpatrayotthitau || 8.126 ||
trirūpau vai brahmavāyū tannāmānau mahadgatau |
vidyādibhistribhistāsu gāyatryādiṣu tisṛṣu || 8.127 ||
kramādbrahmā ca vāyuśca śeṣaścāsanharīcchayā |
vipaśeṣau sabhāryā ca mahadrāje rajotridhār || 8.128 ||
haryājñayaivatrividhāhaṃreṣvabhimāninaḥ |
vidyādyutthairbrahmapūrvaistribhiḥ rudrastrirūpakaḥ || 8.129 ||
sabhāryastrividhāhaṅkṛnmānītusthūladehakaḥ |
mahattatvasya garbhasthamahadāvaraṇaṃ mataṃ || 8.130 ||
lakṣmīnārāyaṇo brahmavāyū bhāryāsamanvitau |
ukteṣu vakṣyamāṇeṣu sarvatatveṣu sarvadā || 8.131 ||
vasanti tānvinā śeṣavīśādyā nakṣamā kvacit |
vaikārikāddhi kurvāṇānmanastatvaṃ jagadguruḥ || 8.132 ||
sṛṣṭvāhaṅkāragarbhasthaṃ cakāra puruṣarṣabhaḥ |
taijasāttu vikurvāṇāthariṇā cendriyāṇi ca || 8.133 ||
jñānaśaktīni pañcāpi karmaśaktīni pañca ca |
sṛṣṭā bhāvīni kāle tu sṛjyaṃ vai bhūtapañcakaṃ || 8.134 ||
yatra yatra sthāpyamīśastatratāni kramādvyadhāt |
ākāśaṃ tu vikurvāṇātsparśamātramabhūddhareḥ || 8.135 ||
tato vāyuṃ samutpādya vāyvāvaraṇamātanot |
vāyoścaiva vikurvāṇādrūpamātraṃ hyabhūttataḥ || 8.136 ||
tato vahniṃ samutpādya vahnyāvaraṇamātanot |
vahnerapi vikurvāṇājjalamātraṃ vyadhāddhariḥ || 8.137 ||
tataḥ salilamutpādya jalāvaraṇamācarat |
jalāvṛtte vikurvāṇāttu gandhamātrābhavadvibhoḥ || 8.138 ||
tataḥ pṛthvīṃ sanirmāya cakre bhūmyāvṛtiṃ hariḥ || 8.139 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.9>
śrībhagavānuvāca
bhūmyāvaraṇamadhye tu brahmāṇḍotpādanocitaṃ |
yojanānāṃ pañcaviṃśatkoṭidviguṇasaṃmitaṃ || 9.1 ||
deśamurvaritaṃ cakre bhagavāndeśakālavit |
asaṃsṛṣṭāni tatvāni brahmāṇḍordhvagatāni ca || 9.2 ||
aṇḍaṃ tadantarnikhilaṃ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ parikīrtitaṃ |
vijñānanāmako viṣṇuḥ ahaṅkārasya rakṣakaḥ || 9.3 ||
mano hṛṣīkāmātrāsu tattannāmā ramāpatiḥ |
ākāśavāyutejastu prāṇanāmākhileśvaraḥ || 9.4 ||
annanāmā pṛthivyāṃ saḥ jalāvaraṇagopi ca |
viśeṣapṛthivī cāṇḍadviguṇāvṛttirūpikā || 9.5 ||
abagnīranabhohaṅkṛnmahattatva guṇā ime |
satvādyāvṛttayoṇḍordhvaṃ sthitāḥ kṣmāvaraṇāḥ kramāth || 9.6 ||
daśottarāḥ pravijñeyā tamaso dviguṇaṃ rajaḥ |
rajaso dviguṇaṃ satvaṃ tato na prākṛtaṃ kvacith || 9.7 ||
paścādūrdhvapradeśe tu aniruddhātmako hariḥ |
tasmāddhaśādhikatanuḥ sopyāvaraṇarūpakaḥ || 9.8 ||
tato daśādhikau pradyumnasaṅkarṣaṇanāmakau |
vāsudevastadūrdhvasthastaddhaśāṃśādhiko mataḥ || 9.9 ||
tatopyanantaguṇikānantānanta pradeśake |
lakṣmīnārāyaṇau vyāptau maryādā na tayoḥ kvacith || 9.10 ||
śrīrdeśakālatonantāguṇānantā na hi kvacit |
aniruddhātprathamato ṛjubhistvaparokṣibhiḥ || 9.11 ||
saha pūrvoktakālena jātau dvau bhāryayāyutau |
trirūpakau brahmavāyū triguṇāvṛttigau kramath || 9.12 ||
teṣāṃ satvādyā vṛttayaḥ sthūladehāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
tatonirūddhasūkṣmadehājjātau vidhīraṇau || 9.13 ||
sa ṛjūkau caturvāraṃ mahattatvagatau matau |
teṣāṃ mahattatvameva sthūladehaṃ vadanti hi || 9.14 ||
tata strirūpau sa ṛjū tau dvau śāntīśajau kramāt |
trividhāhaṅkṛtigatau sa bhāryau sa ṛjū ubhau || 9.15 ||
ekaviṃśadvārajātau manastatvāditatvagau |
aparokṣiṇāṃ ṛjūnāṃ ca śatasyāṇḍādbahirjaniḥ || 9.16 ||
evaṃ rudrādi tatveśāḥ svasaṃkhyā niyamocitāḥ |
aṇḍādbahiḥ sṛṣṭiyogyāḥ dṛṣṭeśāḥ netare kvacith || 9.17 ||
evaṃ trirūpato jātāḥ prākśāntīśena kālataḥ |
sūkṣmeṇa ṛjujanmānantaraṃ śeṣādayastrayaḥ || 9.18 ||
ṣaṇṇīlādyāḥ kālayogātsāparṇyādyāḥ krameṇa tu |
ajeśājeraśeṣyaistu ajerābhyāṃ stribhiḥ stribhiḥ || 9.19 ||
rūpairahaṅkāratatvātmakasthūlaśarīriṇaḥ |
babhūvuritareṣāṃ tu padayogyāparokṣiṇaḥ || 9.20 ||
te tathaiva mahattatvādyekaviṃśati tatvake |
tāvadrūpaiḥsūkṣmatatvājātau prāganirūddhataḥ || 9.21 ||
mana ādīni tatvāni sthūladehāni bhejire |
indrādi puṣkarāntāśca sūkṣmatatvāniruddhajāḥ || 9.22 ||
ekaviṃśadvārataste āsancittādi tatvagāḥ (āsa śritādita tatvagāḥ) |
tāni sthūlādi tatvāni(dehāni) teṣāṃ āsankrameṇa hi || 9.23 ||
prākśāntīśotthitendrādyāḥ manasthūlaśarīriṇaḥ |
dvitryabdipañcaṣaḍvāraṃ jātāḥ śrotrāditatvagāḥ || 9.24 ||
saptāṣaṭanavadigrudravārajātāḥ krameṇa tu |
indrādayaḥ puṣkarāntāḥ vāgādi sthūladehakāḥ || 9.25 ||
tataśca pañcavārotthāḥ prākśāntīśena kālayogataḥ |
śabdādi sthūladehetāḥ tato jātāstu dvādigāḥ || 9.26 ||
atroktānāṃ surāṇāntu yādṛśasthūladehataḥ |
bhāvīnāṃ ca padasthānāṃ tadvaśe sthitiriṣyate || 9.27 ||
aniruddhaḥ sūkṣmakartā prāgeṣāmucitasthalaṃ |
prāpyaiva sṛṣṭimakaroddheśakālārthatatvavith || 9.28 ||
sarvatatvasthitānāntu ṛjūnāṃ sūkṣmadehagāḥ |
satvāṇavo daśaguṇādhikā yānti ca sarvaśaḥ || 9.29 ||
rajastamoṇavaḥ pādamātrato yānti connatiṃ |
bahuvārajātarudrendrādi sūkṣmaśarīragāḥ || 9.30 ||
satvāṇavaḥsamāścopāścopacitāstathā |
rajastamoṇavo rudraśakrādisurasūkṣmagāḥ || 9.31 ||
dvitryabdhipañcaṣaṭsaptāṣṭa dharmaśivārkakaiḥ |
bhāgatoviṃśadantairvṛddhiṃ yāsyanti vai kramāth || 9.32 ||
tenaiva tatkāraṇena pūrvapūrvokta kāraṇaiḥ |
uttarottararūpeṣu buddhimāndyādi ceṣyate || 9.33 ||
mahadādiṣu cāṇḍordhvaṃ daityāveśādi neṣyate |
yathā sūkṣmaśarīreṣu abhivṛddhaṃ rajastamaḥ || 9.34 ||
bhavatteṣāṃ tathā proktaṃ sthūladeheṣvapi sphuṭaṃ |
rajastamoṇavo vyaktiṃ yāsyantyeva tataḥ kramāth || 9.35 ||
tatvāni pūrvarūpāṇi santyanantāni tāni tu |
ṛjavaḥ sarvatatveṣu pratibhātaparāvarāḥ || 9.36 ||
sabhāryā nīcoccasarvatatveṣu saṃsthitāḥ |
rajastamoṇūpacayādhikyādbuddhiviparyayaṃ || 9.37 ||
naiva yāsyanti te sarve hareḥ priyatamāḥsadā |
brahmāsarasvatīviṣṇoḥ sarvāṅgaiḥ sambhavanti hi || 9.38 ||
vāmanāḍyutthitau śeṣavipau viṣṇoḥ śivastathā |
nīlādyāvāruṇī pūrvāḥ indrakāmau manobhavau || 9.39 ||
sabhāryau ca gururbuddheḥ manurādyohyupasthataḥ |
dakṣoṅguṣṭātkāmaputraḥ manavaḥ kāmapūrvakāḥ || 9.40 ||
marutaḥ śvāsataḥ sūryaḥ cakṣuṣaḥ candramā śruteḥ |
dharmo hṛdaḥ sabhāryaśca varuṇo rasanotthitaḥ || 9.41 ||
bhṛgustvacognirmukhataḥ jātānmeḍhrānmanurgateḥ |
viśvāmitramarīcyādyāḥ mitropānāccanairutaḥ || 9.42 ||
skandādbhavagaṇapatiḥ śambhujayantau nābhipādayoḥ |
nāsatyau nāsikābhyantu viśvakseno mukhādabhūth || 9.43 ||
padbhyāntu ṛbhavaḥ(rutavaḥ)(rubhavaḥ) kaṇṭhāddhigdevāstu karmajāḥ |
rudrāḥ kapolāllalāṭācca vasavatsvicchayā vibhoḥ || 9.44 ||
ādityāstu prakāśotthā dantapaṅktisthitā api |
sanakādyā manojātā jayādyānābhi pārśvataḥ || 9.45 ||
gaṅgādi nadyo nāḍibhyaḥ pādāṅguṣṭādibhiḥ prabhoḥ |
anuktatātvikāḥsarve kaṭākṣotthā budhāmatāḥ || 9.46 ||
śaniḥ śarīrataḥ puṣkarādyā karmendriyodbhavāḥ |
anuktadevatābhāryāḥ bhartṛbhiḥ sahasambhavāḥ || 9.47 ||
ete hyaśeṣatatveṣu tatvasthita harīrayoḥ |
proktyairaṅgaiḥ kramājjātaḥ vasanti hyabhimāninaḥ || 9.48 ||
sṛṣṭāvevaṃ bahiścāṇḍānmuktāvevaṃ harervaśaḥ |
jātau jātau hi yastasmātkāmastudgatiḥsutaḥ || 9.49 ||
caṇḍo jayādyā dvārasthāḥ śaśāṅko dhanapastathā |
gandharvapatayaḥsarve jātiṣvapi ca tātvikāḥ || 9.50 ||
puṣkaroccā budhoccāstu anuktāste prakīrtitāḥ |
evaṃ śeṣasya bhāgotthā(mārgottha) bahiraṇḍātsamastaśaḥ || 9.51 ||
navakoṭyo hi devānāṃ satvatatvābhimāninaḥ |
ajo vipastato nīlādviṣaṭkavipavallabhā || 9.52 ||
tata indraḥśaśī tasmātprāṇādyā mārutokhilāḥ |
indrāddakṣastato devarṣayo vai nāradādayaḥ || 9.53 ||
indrādgurustato sūryaḥ jayau vighnaprabhustataḥ |
abhavaṃśca smṛtāsūryādagniragnestu rudrakāḥ || 9.54 ||
ādityāvasavo viśvāpyāśvanau rakṣasādhipaḥ |
parjanya budhaśanyādyāḥ puṣkarāntāḥ samutthitāḥ || 9.55 ||
vasanti sarvatatveṣu garutmānmārgadevatāḥ |
uttamānāṃ ca guravaḥ nīcā uttamasevakāḥ || 9.56 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede navamo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.10>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
sarvatra bhāviḥṛjavo viṣṇorvāyorbhavanti hi |
aṇḍāntarbahisthā vā nānairjanma labhanti te || 10.1 ||
vyāmohāyāsurāṇāntu anyajā iva bhānti te |
brahmāparokṣiṇaḥsarve rudrādyāḥ bhāvitātvikāḥ || 10.2 ||
ahaṅkāreśvarairvāyujātairjatā bhavanti hi |
uttamaiḥsarvanīcānāṃ sṛṣṭiraṅgerdhadeśataḥ || 10.3 ||
evaṃ kramātsṛṣṭiruktā vyutkrameṇa layaḥ smṛtaḥ |
aṇḍordhvadeśatatveśā atra pūrvoktarītitaḥ || 10.4 ||
suprārabdhā bahuvidhā doṣasambandhavarjitāḥ |
ye ye yathā yathā jātāḥ sṛṣṭikāle bhavanti hi || 10.5 ||
guṇasṛṣṭyuttaraṃ jātāḥ sarvesthūlaśarīriṇaḥ |
aṃśānandabalajñānadeśakālāparokṣakāḥ || 10.6 ||
layasṛṣṭipadāveśa guṇalakṣaṇakāntayaḥ |
evaṃ caturdaśamahātāratamyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 10.7 ||
vedeṣu deśakāleṣu liṅgadeheṣu cākṣare |
prakṛtau sarvajīvānāṃ mahattatvaguṇatraye || 10.8 ||
aṃśā ṛjūnāṃ santyeva vipādyaṃśā na teṣu hi |
ahaṅkāre vipādyaṃśā na tatrendrādikāṃśakāḥ || 10.9 ||
nīcanīcāmaramano dehādiṣu surottamāḥ |
vasantyaṃśairnanīcāstu svottameṣu viśanti hi || 10.10 ||
aṇḍādantarbahistvevamaṃśotthā tāratamyadhīḥ |
vidheḥ sarvāṅgasāyujyaṃ vāṇyāḥ vācimanasyapi || 10.11 ||
svotpatyaṅgeṣu devānāṃ sāyujyaṃ diśatīśvaraḥ |
uttamānāṃ mahānandā nīcanīceṣu saṅgatāḥ || 10.12 ||
śatāṃśādavarā nīcānandāduttamagāmi tat |
śatāṃśāduttamā muktau śvetadvīpādiṣu sphuṭaṃ || 10.13 ||
triṣu sthāneṣu śrībhāge cinmātrānandamūrjitaṃ |
bhūdurgābhāgayoḥ prāptavyānanda(daṃ śuddha) sātvikaiḥ || 10.14 ||
dehairbhogocitānanta śuddhasātvikavastubhiḥ |
bhūdurgābhyāṃ kalpitaistairbhagavānbhaktasantateḥ || 10.15 ||
uttamānāṃ sa bahulaṃ nīcānāmalpameva ca |
tattadyogya nijānandaṃ dadāti puruṣaḥ svarāṭh || 10.16 ||
muktau svottama bhogyoru mudapekṣālpikasya tu |
na vidyate hi svānandapūrṇasya na punarjaniḥ || 10.17 ||
duḥkhājñānānādikaṃ bhūyo bhagnaliṅgasya neṣyate |
śataśabdo daśasahasraparaṃ prāptaṃ vadanti hi || 10.18 ||
uttamānāṃ balaṃ bhūrinīcānāmalpameva tu |
garbhodakāgataḥ śeṣaḥ mūrdhnaikenākhilaṃ bhuvaṃ || 10.19 ||
sa dvīpābdhisaricchailāṃ dhatte sarṣapavatsadā |
taṃ vai śeṣaṃ sahasrāsyaṃ vāyukūrmo mahābalaḥ || 10.20 ||
dhatte pucchāgrataḥ śakrapūrvāḥ śeṣādbalāvarāḥ |
bahavo gomahiṣyādyā eka kuñjara bhojane || 10.21 ||
yāvattāvatkūrmahastitopyete hi koṭiśaḥ |
militā api nāgena yoddhumarhati na kvacitu || 10.22 ||
tathottamasurairnīcasurāḥ koṭisahasraśaḥ |
militāḥ prathaneneśāḥ kimumartyāstu rākṣasāḥ || 10.23 ||
ato balena sarveṣāṃ tāratamyaṃ vivecitaṃ |
anālocyānantakalpeṣvaṇḍāntarbahirāsthitaṃ || 10.24 ||
atītaṃ vartamānaṃ cānāgataṃ vastu sañcayaṃ |
sadā karatalāmalakavadvetti viṣṇvanugrahāth || 10.25 ||
anādi nityānadhikānnirviśeṣādanantarān |
sopi nārāyaṇaguṇānālocyāpyatikālataḥ || 10.26 ||
anantakalyāṇaguṇapūrṇasyānantabhāgataḥ |
vibhaktānantabhāgaikabhāga jñaptau na hi kṣamaḥ || 10.27 ||
vineśvaraguṇajñānejñānaṃ ṛjugaṇasya hi |
atītānāgatajagadviṣayīkaraṇe kṣamaṃ || 10.28 ||
ālocya vaiṣṇavajñānepyadhikaṃ sarvajīvataḥ |
vipaśeṣādikajñānaṃ sarvadālocanādapi || 10.29 ||
svottamairbhinnajagataḥ viṣayīkaraṇe kṣamaṃ |
na bhavedekadeśena jānantyete na cānyathā || 10.30 ||
jaḍañcācetanaṃ vāpi śivādyāḥ puṣkarāntimāḥ |
liṅgabaddhāḥ padasthāśca bhāvinaścāparokṣiṇaḥ || 10.31 ||
aṇḍāntare bahiścāpi tātvikā bahurūpataḥ |
sthitā api na jānanti sākalyena jagadgataṃ || 10.32 ||
anādisarvajīvānāṃ jñānaṃ tadvividhaṃ mataṃ |
svārūpikaṃ ca bāhyañca nityaṃ svārūpikaṃ mataṃ || 10.33 ||
ajanyaṃ śāśvataṃ viṣṇoḥ bandhasya vaśagaṃ sadā |
jīvaṅgeśādhīnamāyāvaśagaṃ kramayogataḥ || 10.34 ||
sādhanena vyañjyamānamāmuktyantaṃ tataḥ paraṃ |
ekaprakāramajñānaṃ rahitaṃ svottamāśritāth || 10.35 ||
jñānācchataguṇanyūnaraṃ iśvare vā jagatyalaṃ |
anantavedoktaguṇānviṣṇerjānāti padmabhūḥ || 10.36 ||
padastho vā vimukto vā tadanye tadguṇasthitāḥ |
krameṇaiva śataṃnyūnajñānaṃ viṣṇorupāsakāḥ || 10.37 ||
prākṛtoprākṛtaśarīrāśritasya ṛjoḥsadā |
manasyanityaṃ yajjanyaṃ punaḥ punarapi sphuṭaṃ || 10.38 ||
tadbāhyevṛttirupaṃ ca yathārthaṃ sarvadāpi ca |
teṣāṃ pañcavidhajñānaṃ kvacideva na sarvadā || 10.39 ||
daityāveśādi śūnyāste sadā durviṣayojjhitāḥ |
sākṣīndriyaiḥ sahāpyetalliṅgasūkṣmendriyāvṛtaiḥ || 10.40 ||
sthūlendriyaiḥ suviṣayāsaktaiḥ prātyakṣakī matiḥ |
yadārthaiva tayā doṣaśūnyā sadyuktijā matiḥ || 10.41 ||
yuktijanyaṃ tu yajñānamānumānikamīritaḥ |
pratyakṣamanumānañca āgamastasya sādhanaṃ || 10.42 ||
yathārthaiva tathā vedaśāstrārthaśravaṇādapi |
manojātaṃ svāgamajaṃ yathārthajñānameva hi || 10.43 ||
deśādavaradeśetu kramāttatveṣu saṃśritāḥ |
tatvavyāpārakāle tu svarūpocitaśaktayaḥ || 10.44 ||
yathāgamasamudbhūtaṃ trividhaṃ parikīrtitaṃ |
jñānaṃ manaḥsamudbhūtaṃ bāhyakāryamitīritaṃ || 10.45 ||
yatpratyakṣānumānaṃ ca āgamastasya sādhanaṃ |
jīvaḥ pramātāviṣayā arthāste kāraṇāmatāḥ || 10.46 ||
rūdrādīnāntu yajjhānaṃ bāhyaṃ manasi sambhavaṃ |
pratyakṣādyaistrabhiḥ sadbhiḥ sādhanairapyanityakaṃ || 10.47 ||
kvacidyathārthaṃ tajñānamayathārthaṃ kvacidbhavet |
karmadevājānajādyāḥ devadāsāḥ kramāvarāḥ || 10.48 ||
devagandharvaparyantāḥ sāṃśāḥ sarve prakīrtitāḥ |
niraṃśāste sadāmartyagandharvādyā stṛṇāntimāḥ || 10.49 ||
jīvā eṣāṃ svarūpantu jñanaṃ nityaṃ yathārthakaṃ |
uttamottamato nyūnaṃ ayathārthādikaṃ kramāth || 10.50 ||
bāhyaṃ janyamanityaṃ ca yathārthaṃ prāyaśaḥ sadā |
riśvare vā jagati vā kramādalpāvagāhi ca || 10.51 ||
iti jñāne tāratamyaṃ deveṣūccāvaceṣvalaṃ |
mahadgaṇatrayeṣvaṃśāḥ ṛjūnāṃ santi connate || 10.52 ||
deśe tatra na rudrādi devāṃśāstadadhastane |
deśehaṅkṛtirudrādi devāṃśāḥsanti sarvadā || 10.53 ||
tatrendrādyā na tiṣṭhanti tatopyavaradeśagāḥ |
bhūtāvyatīnāṃ maryādā yaddeśe yādṛśīritā || 10.54 ||
kalāṣoḍaśatadgāśca tattadā vṛttisantatāḥ |
manaḥ śabdaḥ śrotravācāṃ kalāstvākāśasantatāḥ || 10.55 ||
tvaksparśapāṇisaṃjñāstu kalāvāyusamāśritāḥ |
netrarūpāṅghrisaṃjñāstu kalāstejaḥ śarīragāḥ || 10.56 ||
rasanārasaguhyānāṃ kalāvārisamāśritāḥ |
gandhaghrāṇopasthakalā pṛthivyāvaraṇāśritā || 10.57 ||
manomātrendriyakalā bhūteṣu kramayogataḥ |
tadādhikārā hariṇā śakrādyāḥ puṣkarāntimāḥ || 10.58 ||
sūkṣmasthūlotpattikāle uttamāstūrdhvadeśagāḥ |
nīcanīcāstūccadeśasthitoccoccasurāśrayāth || 10.59 ||
deśādavaradeśe tu kramāttatveṣu saṃsthitāḥ |
tatvavyāpārakāle tu svarūpocitaśaktayaḥ || 10.60 ||
iti deśocita mahātāratamyaṃ vadanti hi |
devamānenābdaśataṃ paurvāparyavyavasthayā || 10.61 ||
ṛjūnāṃ ca janiḥ proktā sahasrābdāntarāmatāḥ |
vipādyāstu tathendrādyā daśasāhasravatsaraiḥ || 10.62 ||
kramātpaurvāparyajātāḥ kālānnīcoccabhāvinaḥ |
atha sarvasyāparokṣatāratamyaṃ vivicyate || 10.63 ||
tṛṇādisarvajīvānāṃ svarūpoddhāra āgate |
kāle tattadyogyajīvasādhanocitakālake || 10.64 ||
hariśca śrībrahmavāyū sabhāryau ca tadājñayā |
etadanyadvīpavarṣajāyāmānānhi sarvaśaḥ || 10.65 ||
svakarmavaśagānsvargāntarikṣadharāncaran |
nānāyonigatānjīvānatalādyādharasthitān || 10.66 ||
uddhartukāmo bhagavānbhārate janayiṣyati |
tatra mānuṣayonyutthāṃstatrāpi brāhmaṇottamān || 10.67 ||
tatra sarvasvarūporuvyaktikārakakarmaṇā |
saṃyojya sarvasadyogyajñānadānagurūttamān || 10.68 ||
bhinnadeśe bhinnakāle yojayatyakhilaṃ hariḥ |
tṛṇādimartyaparyantagandharvāste hyanantaśaḥ || 10.69 ||
niraṃśāste pratīkālambanāste karmayoginaḥ |
devagandharvapūrvāstvājānajāntā samastaśaḥ || 10.70 ||
apratīkālambanā sāṃśāḥ kramocca jñānayoginaḥ |
pratīkasthaṃ śarīrasthaṃ paśyantīti tathāhvayāḥ || 10.71 ||
pratīkādbahirīśasya darśānātkarmadevatāḥ |
apratīkālambanāśca prāyovijñānayoginaḥ || 10.72 ||
eṣāmaṇḍāntare janmāparokṣādi praśasyate |
niraṃśāḥ pakvakāle tu utkrāmyaiva maharādigāḥ || 10.73 ||
eṣāṃ laye svottameṣu praveśo naiva vidyate |
devagandharvapūrvāṇāṃ bhūmāvutkramaṇāllayaḥ || 10.74 ||
mahālaye svottameṣu praveśaḥ lūkṣmadehataḥ |
utkramānutkramānyoginohyatatveśvaravaśānjaguḥ || 10.75 ||
puṣkarāntā tātvikāśca kvāpi notkramaṇāllayāḥ |
yānti sthūlaśarīraiste svottameṣu viśanti hi || 10.76 ||
tānanukramaṇānyāhuḥ yogino bhaktiyoginaḥ |
satkarmajñānabhaktī ca sarveṣāṃ sarvadāpi hi || 10.77 ||
avaśyakāste sarvepi tattatprācuryatastathā |
niraṃśāḥsatsādhanāyocitajanmasahasrataḥ || 10.78 ||
jñānantu daśabhiḥ prāpya sadbhaktiṃ tribhirāpya ca |
dṛṣṭvā hṛdisthaṃ mucyante hyataste karmayoginaḥ || 10.79 ||
satkarmakṛtvā niṣkāmyaṃ labhanti gurusantatī |
atātvikā devadāsāḥ satkarmaśatajanmabhiḥ || 10.80 ||
kṛtvā daśasahasrorujanmabhirjñānamāpya ca |
bhaktiṃ triśatakairāpya mucyante jñānayoginaḥ || 10.81 ||
ataste karmadevatāstadūrdhvaṃ bhaktiyoginaḥ |
tṛṇādyāḥ svahṛdisthaṃ taṃ paśyantyucitajanmabhiḥ || 10.82 ||
dīpakāntiviśiṣṭeśaṃ na devānnaiva martyagaṃ |
ghaṭikārdhaṃ śarīre vā muktau śaktistu tādṛśī || 10.83 ||
cinmātropāsanāśaktiḥ naivādhikaguṇottare |
teṣāraṃ idṛśa evaṃ syātapararokṣo hi nādhikaḥ || 10.84 ||
rāmakṛṣṇādi rūpāṇi bahiḥ paśyantyapi kvacit |
mānuṣatvena paśyanti aparokṣena saṃsmṛtaḥ || 10.85 ||
sthūlahṛdgāparokṣāstu bāhyastādaparokṣakāḥ |
svasvarūpasthajīvābhasvatantrasya ca darśanaṃ || 10.86 ||
svarūpābhyāparokṣastu sarveṣāmevameva hi |
pādonaghaṭikāmātraṃ svarūpe prākṛte hṛdi || 10.87 ||
paśyanti krimikīṭādyāḥ pradīpaprabhayā hariṃ |
svarūpacakṣuṣādṛṣṭiḥ aparokṣa itīryate || 10.88 ||
veda deśākṣarokālo jīveśau prākṛtendriyaiḥ |
na vedyā hi svarūpeṇa vedyā eva prakīrtitāḥ || 10.89 ||
bhaktyā hariprasādena naṣṭetvajñānapañcake |
ratnavadbhāsure liṅge sūkṣmadehesthitasya tu || 10.90 ||
jīvasya cakṣuṣī vyakto bhavataḥ svena paśyati |
tejomayasvarūpasthaṃ sthūlahṛdgaṃ krameṇa tu || 10.91 ||
yavamātraṃ harintārṇāḥ kīṭādyāḥ badarīmitaṃ |
aṅguṣṭāgramitaṃ vṛṣāḥ pradīpadaśasannibhaṃ || 10.92 ||
hariṃ paśyanti ghaṭikāmitaṃ kālaṃ ramāpatiṃ |
samaṃ dīpaṃ cidānandaṃ cidānandātmakaṃ mṛgāḥ || 10.93 ||
khagāśca paśavo gāvaḥ hṛdgaṃ jīvagataṃ hariṃ |
pradīpaśatabhāyuktaṃ ghaṭikārdhatadardhakaṃ || 10.94 ||
saccidānda ātmeti mānuṣyairdhyaiyariśvaraḥ |
vidyudvanmānuṣā vidyurdaśavidyunmitaṃ nṛpāḥ || 10.95 ||
rājānastadviguṇābhaṃ sūryamaṇḍalavatsurāḥ |
gandharvāḥ pitaraḥsūryaśatasannibhamīśvaraṃ || 10.96 ||
gopyaḥ kṛṣṇāṅgasaṅgārhāḥ pañcasāhasrasūryabhaṃ |
ājānajādaśasāhasasrārkābhaṃ tvagnijā api || 10.97 ||
caturlakṣādhikāśītilakṣajīvagaṇeṣvapi |
daśaiva puruṣāḥ proktāstātvikāsteṣu pañca ca || 10.98 ||
parjanyādavarāḥ pañca puṣkarāduttamā matāḥ |
sastrīkāḥsomahīnā aṇḍādantarbahisthitāḥ || 10.99 ||
triṃśaccāraṇarakṣāṃsi saptatiḥ sarvajātiṣu |
antarāletu padmotthā brahmāyāvaddhitiṣṭhati || 10.100 ||
tāvatsaṃvatsaragata pratipratidineṣvapi |
sāṃśā atātvikāḥ sarvesaṃkhyayā vakṣyamāṇayā || 10.101 ||
mitāstu pratikalpepi padasthā hyaparokṣiṇaḥ |
urvaśyādyāhyapsarasaḥ śataṃśā sva(svaṣṭa)saṃkhyayā || 10.102 ||
mitā ajānajaistulyāḥ karmadevasamāparāḥ |
śataṃ pitṝṇāṃ saptaiva teṣūrvaśyādibhiḥ samāḥ || 10.103 ||
anye ājānajebhyastu nyūnāstebhyovarāḥ kramāt |
gandharvāstu śataṃ teṣu aṣṭau tulyāśca karmajaiḥ || 10.104 ||
śatakoṭimitāḥ sarve ṛṣayo viṃśaduttamāḥ |
teṣveva tāvatviṣṭābhiḥ śatakaṃ karmajaiḥ samaṃ || 10.105 ||
tadanye ājānajebhyastulyā agnisutā api |
dvyaṣṭausahasrāṇi śatantriṃśaccāraṇarakṣasāṃ || 10.106 ||
sādhyasiddhāstathānyāstu saptatiḥ sarvajātiṣu |
śataṃ siddhājānajākhyā paśyanti hṛdi jīvagaṃ || 10.107 ||
koṭisūryaprabhaṃ karmadevā hṛdgaṃ bahisthitaṃ |
dvisaptalokasamitaṃ te pratīkāvalambanāḥ || 10.108 ||
martyāḥ dvighaṭikāmātraṃ trināḍyantaṃ nṛpottamāḥ |
tathā mānuṣagandharvāścaturnāḍyantamīśvaraṃ || 10.109 ||
ṣaṇṇāḍyantaṃ sugandharvā gopyāyāmāntamañjasā |
śatonaśatakoṭyastu ṛṣayognisutā api || 10.110 ||
sājānajādaśaghaṭikāmitaṃ kālaṃ jagadguruṃ |
paśyanti karmadevāstu madhyāhnāntaṃ pareśvaraṃ || 10.111 ||
svottamaspardhayāhīnāḥ duṣprārabdhavivarjitāḥ |
bhāvarūpājñānaśūnyāḥ daityāveśavivarjitāḥ || 10.112 ||
pūrvoktakāntyākāle taṃ paśyanti jagadīśvaraṃ |
pūrvoktadoṣarahitāstātvikāḥsarvadā hariṃ || 10.113 ||
paśyantidivaparyantā doṣā ete hi nīcakaiḥ |
uttameṣūttameṣvalpāstathā cānyatarāmatāḥ || 10.114 ||
brahmaṇo doṣa sambandho na kvāpi kva caddṛśyate |
rājāno martyagandharvapūrvā ṣaṭdaśapūrvakān || 10.115 ||
śataṃ daśasahasrāṃśca lakṣakoṭimitānguṇān |
viṣṇorguṇānarbudāṃśca sarvānapi tatodhikān || 10.116 ||
anantavedoktaguṇānbrahmopāsyati netaraḥ |
martyoccā hṛdi devāṃśca padasthā pīṭhadevatāḥ || 10.117 ||
tathāvaraṇagāndevānpaśyantyapi ca maṇḍalaṃ |
suṣumnāṃ saptakamalānyapi tadgaharīśvare || 10.118 ||
sthāneśaṃ caiva mūleśaṃ rājāno martyagāyakāḥ |
iḍāpiṅgalanāḍisthānkramoccānsarvadehagān || 10.119 ||
tatveśāṃ stadgatahareḥ rūpāṇi a(ni)khilānyapi |
dhyātvopāsya vimucyante devā ājānajāhvayāḥ || 10.120 ||
ime te ca pratīkastha haripādāvalambinaḥ |
karmadevā atatveśā api rūpāṇi sarvaśaḥ || 10.121 ||
upāsyāpi virāḍrūpaṃ mucyante samupāsya ca |
apratīkālambanāste tu karmadevāḥ surā api || 10.122 ||
tyaktaliṅgasya muktasya tṛṇakīṭādikasya ca |
svarūpadeha te sarve daśayojanasantatā || 10.123 ||
vṛkṣādīnāṃ mṛgādīnāṃ tato dviguṇarugbhavet |
vimuktānāntu martyānāṃ śūdrādīnāmataḥ paraṃ || 10.124 ||
ekadvitricatuṣpādādhikaruksantatāḥ sadā |
rājanyāmartyagandharvāstatodvitryādhikaprabhāḥ || 10.125 ||
parito merutulyātviṭdevagandharvasantateḥ |
cirānto pitaro muktā bhuvarlokāntakāntayaḥ || 10.126 ||
gopyaḥ kṛṣṇāṅghrisaṅgārhāḥ upasantati dīptayaḥ |
ājanājātatsamāśca svargāntā tatprakāśinaḥ || 10.127 ||
yasya yasya ca jīvasya prakāśo yatra ceritaḥ |
daivādbahirdṛṣṭigato bhāti martyoccavaddhariḥ || 10.128 ||
na saccidānandatanurbāhyadṛggocaro bhavet |
dṛṣṭvopāsya vimucyante karmadevā virāṭtanuṃ || 10.129 ||
tādṛjjotistāvadantaṃ viṣṇuṃ paśyati cakṣuṣā |
svārūpikeṇa bādhyena naiva paśyanti ceśvaraṃ || 10.130 ||
paśyanti naivopāsyanti brahmāṇḍe vyāptamīśvaraṃ |
puṣkarādyāstu śakrāntāḥ pūrvoktākhilarūpiṇaṃ || 10.131 ||
aṇḍādbahiḥ pṛthivyādi manastatvādi santateḥ |
dṛṣṭvādhyātvopāsya sarve muktiṃ yāsyanti kālataḥ || 10.132 ||
ahaṅkāragataṃ draṣṭumīśānopāsane kṣamāḥ |
puṣkarādyā mahendrāntāḥ kramādadhikakāntayaḥ || 10.133 ||
koṭyarbudārkābhaṃ devaṃ vāmadevādayo hariṃ |
upāsyāhaṅkāratatvavyāptasattanumīśvaraṃ || 10.134 ||
mucyate ca mahattatve tamasyapi tataṃ hariṃ |
paśyantīvāruṇīpūrvā neṣyanti tamasisthitaṃ || 10.135 ||
anantārkarucā satvāvṛtyantaṃ vyāptamavyayaṃ |
upāsya gīrmuktimeti tatopyadhikaruktataḥ || 10.136 ||
vāsudevāvaraṇagaṃ brahmā paśyati netaraḥ |
yāvatpaśyanti taṃ jīvaṃ tāvatsāmānyaruksa hi || 10.137 ||
yāvanupāsyo vai viṣṇustāvatteṣāṃ viśeṣabhā |
brahmaṇo naiva sāmānyarugviśeṣaprabho hi saḥ || 10.138 ||
atyalpasukharūpāṃśā jīvānāṃ yogyatāvasan |
yādṛśī bhāramāyāstu vyāptāyāḥ sarvadeśagā || 10.139 ||
bhānuprabhāgatā dīpaprabhā yadvanna rocate |
tathā viṣṇuprabhānunnāmāprabhā naiva rocate || 10.140 ||
evaṃ nīcaprabhāsvoccaprabhāsu pravilīyate |
tathāpi ca yathārthajñā muktāptai tatvavettamāḥ || 10.141 ||
yo yāvatīṃ harau kāntiṃ vetyasau tacchatāṃśabhāk |
prabhātyeva prakāśasya tāratamyaṃ viduttamāḥ || 10.142 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede daśamo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.11>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
puruṣeṣu brahmavāyū vipādiṣu ramāpatiḥ |
puṃ rūpeṇa viśeṣāveśādikaṃ sarvadā hariḥ || 11.1 ||
karoti strīṣu mā vāgīrvāyustrī(ṣu)svarūpataḥ |
viśeṣāveśakṛdviṣṇuḥ yogyatāyānuyogataḥ || 11.2 ||
āviṣṭastūttameṣvekaḥ viśeṣātmā tu taiḥ suraiḥ(svakaiḥ) |
jīvairasādhya sumahatkarmakāra yatīśvaraḥ || 11.3 ||
uttamottamajīveṣu āve(de)śo yādṛgiṣyate |
na tathā nīcajīveṣu tasmāttehyalpaśaktayaḥ || 11.4 ||
padastheṣu sureṣvevamāveśo naiva muktigaḥ |
na niraṃśeṣu cāveśāḥ sāṃśeṣvājānajeṣvapi || 11.5 ||
vāyoḥ sarvāvatāreṣu viśeṣāveśa eva hi |
svarūpaliṅgasūkṣmeṣu sthūladeheṣu sarvadā || 11.6 ||
rakṣārthaṃ sarvatatveṣu sarvadā vasatīśvaraḥ |
