āveśo(tha) na tathā viṣṇorviśeṣākhyaḥ sureṣvapi || 11.7 ||
kvacidvyaktiṃ kvacidrāsaṃ saptāveśoṇḍajeṣvapi |
aṇḍādūrdhvaṃ sadāde(se)veṣvantaḥ ṛjuṣu ceṣyate || 11.8 ||
nīceṣūttamadevānāṃ āveśaḥ kvacidiṣyate |
balārthamasureṣveva(ṣvīraḥ) āveśaṃ kurvati prabhuḥ || 11.9 ||
ādādājñā tena dṛptā surairnaṣṭā harīcchayā |
bhavanti nityadā viṣṇuḥ sureṣvantarhito hi saḥ || 11.10 ||
jñānānandabalādyāye guṇāḥ sādhāraṇā matāḥ |
tṛṇādi brahmaparyanteṣvapi māyā harāvapi || 11.11 ||
tathāpi nīcanīceṣu guṇādīpaupamāśritāḥ |
sādharaṇāḥ sajātīyāḥ vijātīyā vilakṣaṇāḥ || 11.12 ||
svantantratvaṃ svaramaṇatvaṃ suguṇavyāptitā tathā |
parāpekṣādi śūnyatvaṃ(rāhityaṃ) niyantṛtvaṃ jagadgurau || 11.13 ||
sṛṣṭyādi kartṛtādyāstu vijātīyā harau matāḥ |
akṣa(ra)tvādayo māyāṃ naiva te brahmaṇi sphuṭaṃ || 11.14 ||
ṛjutvaṃ prākṛtoccatvaṃ nityārtheṣvabhimānitā |
sarvādhipatyatā liṅgadehe kāryavaśītvatā || 11.15 ||
niṣkāmyayogyatāpūrvāḥ vijātīyā itismṛtāḥ |
ete harīrayoḥ proktāḥ naiva te vipaśeṣayoḥ || 11.16 ||
evameṣūttameṣūktāḥ vijātīyā na nīcagāḥ |
satyanantatvatastveṣāmuktirnaiva hi śakyate || 11.17 ||
karmadevānsamārabhya brahmā teṣu śarīriṣu |
cakraśaṅkhādayorekhāḥ śaraśaktyaṅkuśādayaḥ || 11.18 ||
prāsatomaratiryaṅkahalabhāgyomṛtādayaḥ |
govindyātilagodhūmachatracāmaratoraṇāḥ || 11.19 ||
dhvajordhvavyajanākārā āndolāgatavāninaḥ |
macchakacchapasiṃhādyāḥ vṛṣaratnadharādayaḥ || 11.20 ||
garutmaccheṣamukharā rekhā dakṣake pade |
puṃsāntu dakṣiṇepāde strīṇāṃ vāme vasanti hi || 11.21 ||
dhenuśaṅkhagadārekhāḥ vāme puṃsāṃ śubhā matāḥ |
nīceṣvaṅganyūnarūpāḥ uttameṣūccarūpakāḥ || 11.22 ||
sampūrṇāḥ sarvadārekhāḥ prāṇe māyāṃ harāvapi |
tatrāpyuccakramoccāsti rekhāḥ coccatvahetavaḥ || 11.23 ||
ṣaṇṇavatyaṅgulo yastu nyagrodhaparimaṇḍalaḥ |
saptapāda caturhasto dvātriṃśallakṣaṇairyutaḥ || 11.24 ||
śarīrāṅgeṣu sādhūnāṃ pañcasthāneṣu dīrghatāṃ |
hṛsvatvaṃ pañcasu tathā saptasthāneṣu raktimā || 11.25 ||
ṣaḍaunnatyaṃ yasya samyakpṛthutā triṣu yasya tu |
gambhīratā ca laghutā triṣu sthāneṣu bhāti vai || 11.26 ||
sa sulakṣaṇasampannaḥ sa tu jñānaprado gurūḥ |
martyottameṣu vai viṃśallakṣaṇāni bhavanti hi || 11.27 ||
krameṇaikaikaśo vṛddhānyapi salakṣaṇāni ca |
sarvāṇi paripūrṇāni lakṣaṇāni vidhīrayoḥ || 11.28 ||
śarīre ca svarūpe ca sarvasatsādhaneṣvalaṃ |
ati(bhi)vyaktāni (co)vocceṣu avyaktānītareṣu ca || 11.29 ||
atha sṛṣṭau tāratamyaṃ śṛṇuṣva prāgathottare |
ātmasūkṣmaguṇasthūlasṛṣṭayaḥ prāgudīritāḥ || 11.30 ||
pañcamātrāvyaktabhūtā vṛttipañcakataḥ kramāt |
prāgukta bhāvarūpājñānapañcakamabhūttataḥ || 11.31 ||
bhrāntirbhautikadehānāṃ vyaktāyoniṣvapi kvacit |
aṇḍādūrdhvamiyaṃ sṛṣṭi rasaṃ sṛṣṭeti kīrtyate || 11.32 ||
mahadādyaṇḍaparyantaḥ sarga ityabhidhīyate |
saṃsṛṣṭamaṇḍaṃ tadgañca samastaṃ samprakīrtitaṃ || 11.33 ||
anusargaṃ janāḥ prāhuḥ aṇḍāntaryadajotthitaṃ |
mahadādyāḥ surāḥ sarvepyaṇḍasṛṣṭāvaśaktayaḥ || 11.34 ||
astuvanpuruṣaṃ sārdhaṃ saptatriṃśaccharanmayaṃ |
kālaṃ tato hariḥ sarvatatvajātaṃ tadantakaiḥ || 11.35 ||
bhāgyairādāya saṃyojyairekībhūtaṃ karotyajaḥ |
tacca tredhā vibhajyeśaḥ teṣvādyaṃ bhāgamīśvaraḥ || 11.36 ||
gṛhītvā viṣṇunāmeśastannigīrya śriyaṃ svayaṃ |
vīryaṃ tadātmakaṃ tasyāmādhatta puruṣeśvaraḥ || 11.37 ||
pṛthivyāvṛttimadhye tu suṣuveṇḍaṃ vidhestanuṃ |
pañcāśatkoṭivistāramaṇḍaṃsṛṣṭvā jagadguruḥ || 11.38 ||
sarvaciccaityasahito vīrāṇāmaṇḍamāviśat |
brahmāṇḍe jalasampūrṇe sarvalokāśrayo bhaveth || 11.39 ||
sa viṣṇuḥ padmanābhākhyo bhūdaṇḍasalile śubhe |
prākṛteḥ svasvarūpeṇātyabhinno hīśvaro hariḥ || 11.40 ||
sarvābharaṇasampannaḥ sahasrāṅghryakṣibāhukaḥ |
prāk(pratyak)śirāstūrdhvamukhaḥ śiśye pītāmbaraḥ samaḥ || 11.41 ||
aṇḍasṛṣṭyādimekāle tatvajātāttridhā kṛtāt |
adyādaṇḍamabhūdanyaṃ padmanābhograsadvibhuḥ || 11.42 ||
tatsṛṣṭaṃ bhūpradānaṃ nābhau sthāpya hiraṇmayaṃ |
padmaṃ vyajanayattasya kandātbhūmirathobhavath || 11.43 ||
saptadvīpasamudrādi tamoghanajalānvitā |
pañcāśatkoṭivistīrṇāpyaṇḍamadhyapradeśagā || 11.44 ||
lakṣayojanasaṃkhyākapṛthulātidhṛḍhāśrayā |
pañcaviṃśatkoṭimitalakṣonaṃ garbhavāryadhaḥ || 11.45 ||
yojanānāṃ tadupari sthāpitābhūrabhūddhareḥ |
bhūnāmā bhūmigo viṣṇugarbhavāryestadāhvayaḥ || 11.46 ||
vakṣyamāṇeṣu cokteṣu jaḍacitsu tadāhvayaḥ |
ādhāro rakṣakaḥ sarvanāmā sarvabhṛdīśvaraḥ || 11.47 ||
merustanmadhyago bhūmeścaturasro hiraṇmayaḥ |
yojanānāṃ lakṣamitaḥ ucchrāyādratnasānukaḥ || 11.48 ||
atha ṣoḍaśasāhasrayojanāyāmariritaḥ |
tāvatā bhūrimānaḥsahamekaḥsarvasurālayaḥ || 11.49 ||
ūrdhaṃ tadviguṇāyāmaḥ sahasrāśītiyojanaiḥ |
caturbhiradhikairbhūmerucchritaḥ sarvataḥ śubhaṃ || 11.50 ||
meroḥ pūrvādyaṣṭadikṣu lokapāla sadāṃsyalaṃ |
santyevasarvabhadrāṇi sārdhatādvisahasrakaiḥ || 11.51 ||
caturasrāṇi tanmadhye taccaturguṇavistaraṃ |
brahmaṇastu sadasyāni ratnahemamayāni ca || 11.52 ||
teṣvindrogniryamaḥ kravyātvaruṇāni layakṣapaḥ |
śivaśceti sabhāryāste sāṅgāḥ saparivārakāḥ || 11.53 ||
tanmadadhye maṇṭapastasya vidherājñākarāstathā |
tatra vāṇīśvaronanta bhujagasya suśiraskakaḥ || 11.54 ||
lokapālasado madhye vindhyodikṣu catusruṣu |
samanāḥ sarvatobhadrā tārasārdhāṣṭasahasrakāḥ || 11.55 ||
mandārapārijātādyāḥ divyapuṣpaphalaścadāḥ |
saritsarovarāṇyatra kañjādisumavantyapi || 11.56 ||
nānānārīsevitāni haṃsādi dvijavantyapi |
ājānajasamānaikajātīyaparicārakaiḥ || 11.57 ||
sevitodrivarobhāsvaddīpya divyadrigocaraḥ |
divyābharaṇavastrādyaiḥ alaṅkṛtasurāda(la)yaḥ || 11.58 ||
tasya pūrvādyaṣṭadikṣu mandaro merumandaraḥ |
supārśvaḥ kumudaśceti avaṣaṭambhanagāḥ kramāth || 11.59 ||
te sahasrastrikā yāmāḥ sahasradaśakocchritāḥ |
cūtajambūkadambāścanyagrodhasteṣu bhūruhāḥ || 11.60 ||
ekādaśaśatoccāste gajopamaphalānvitāḥ |
suvarṇaratnasaṃkāśāḥ śataśākhā samāvṛtāḥ || 11.61 ||
tatra nānā pakṣigaṇāḥ sagandhakusumachadāḥ |
teṣāṃ tūparitaḥ svarṇaratnamandārabhūmiṣu || 11.62 ||
nandanaṃ caitrarathakaṃ vaibhrājakamitīritaṃ |
vanānyavarṇanīyāni sarvatobhadrasaṃjñakaṃ || 11.63 ||
teṣu vai kāmikāḥ siddhāḥ caranti lalanānvitāḥ |
teṣu sañcaratāṃ saukhyaṃ adhikaṃ na śramādayaḥ || 11.64 ||
eṣāṃ phalairnipatitaiḥ viśīrṇaiḥ śubhrasadrasaiḥ |
niryadrasamudbhūtāścatasraśca catasṛṣu || 11.65 ||
dvilakṣavṛtta dīrghasthāḥ jarā durgandha duḥsahāḥ |
nīlaḥ śvetaḥ śṛṅgavāṃśca meroruttarato nagāḥ || 11.66 ||
yojanānāṃ navasahasrāyāmākṣmātalasya ca |
maryādāgirayaḥsarve pratyaksāgaradīrghakāḥ || 11.67 ||
uścrayāddhaśasāhasrāḥ pṛthulā dvisahasrakāḥ |
niṣadho hemakūṭaśca himavāniti dakṣiṇe || 11.68 ||
pūrvamānenoktabhuvaḥ maryādā girayomatāḥ |
vistārāducchrayāddhairghyānnīlādīnāṃ samāḥ kramāth || 11.69 ||
nānāsiddhagaṇaiḥ sevyāḥ sarvaratna samākulāḥ |
pūrvasyāṃ diśi merostu gandhamādanaparvataḥ || 11.70 ||
pratīcyāṃ diśi merostu mālyavānnāma bhūdharaḥ |
ānīlaniṣadhāyāmau parvatau devapūjitau || 11.71 ||
tau pūrvasamitau divau sarvasadvṛkṣaśobhitau |
caturdikṣvapi merostu navasāhasrayojanaiḥ || 11.72 ||
vistṛtaṃ bhūtalaṃ celāvṛtavarṣaṃ vadanti hi |
ramyako hiraṇmayaścaiva kuruvarṣādayastrayaḥ || 11.73 ||
varṣānīlādyadribhiste vibhaktā svarṇabhūmayaḥ |
gandhamādanataḥ pūrvabhūmau bhadrāścavarṣake || 11.74 ||
mālyavatpaścimeketumālyavarṣaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
harikiṃpuruṣa bhāratavarṣāstu kramayogataḥ || 11.75 ||
dakṣiṇe niṣadhādyadrivibhaktabhūmayaḥ kramāt |
vaivasvato manurmartyopāsako ramyakeśvaraḥ || 11.76 ||
aryamāḥ pitṛpaḥ kūrmopāsakastu hiraṇmaye |
bhūmirādivarāhasya bhāryā vai kuruvarṣagā || 11.77 ||
hayāsyopāsako bhadraśravāḥ bhadrāśca varṣake |
ratyākāmenārcitau dvau kṛtipradyumnasaṃjñakau || 11.78 ||
ketumālye strījanārcyo na jīvaṃ bhadrake khalu |
prahlādopāsito nārasiṃhmopi harivarṣagaḥ || 11.79 ||
hanumatpūjito rāmaḥ nāthaḥ kiṃpuruṣasya tu |
bhadrāśvāddakṣiṇe varṣe bhāratākhyo guṇādhikaḥ || 11.80 ||
narābhimāni śeṣāṃśa bhāratasyāsya cānujaḥ |
nārāyaṇo dakṣaputryāḥ(tryāṃ) mūrtyādharmasamutthitau || 11.81 ||
tau nāradasya ca ṛṣeḥ jñānadau lokapūjitaḥ |
ilāvṛtte merupūrvadiśi savyāpasavyagau || 11.82 ||
jaṭharo devakūṭaśca merupṛṣṭhe ca pūrvataḥ |
gandhamādana saṃspṛṣṭau svarṇaratnamayau samau || 11.83 ||
vistārāttūcchrayācca yojanā dvisahasrakau |
navasāhasrasadbhūmimadhye jaṭharanīlayoḥ || 11.84 ||
bhūrdevakūṭaniṣadhamadhyasthāttāvatī matā |
ṣaṭsahasrāntarabhuvau gandhamādanamandarau || 11.85 ||
tiryaktadviguṇā bhūmiḥ merupūrvasthagotrayoḥ |
kailāsakaravīrau dvāvevaṃ merostu dakṣiṇe || 11.86 ||
pavanaḥ pariyātraśca dvau meroḥ paścimāśrayau |
śṛṅgaśca makaraścaiva meroruttaratastathā || 11.87 ||
niṣadhāntau dakṣiṇavuttarau nīlasaṃmitau |
pāścātyau mālyavatsṛṣṭau pūrvagādrīvamānataḥ || 11.88 ||
etairakhilāvṛtovarṣo vibhaktaścāṣvadhāmalaḥ |
sukumāraṃ vanaṃ ramyaṃ sarvatryaiṇyaiva teṣvalaṃ || 11.89 ||
teṣu cevāstriyaḥ pṛṣṭāpūrvādhyaṣṭaṃ digunmukhāḥ |
aṣaṭarūpairādiśeṣaḥ sahasravadanaḥ prabhuḥ || 11.90 ||
vasatyasya mahārudraḥ sāṃbaścāṣṭatanuḥ svayaṃ |
sadopāsta iti śeṣaṃ tadgaṃ saṅkarṣaṇaṃ paraṃ || 11.91 ||
panaraṣṭatanuḥ śambhuḥ bhavānyābhasmago bhavaḥ |
teṣvaṣṭabhavaneṣveva sadāramati śaṅkaraḥ || 11.92 ||
prārabdhakarmaṇā sāpi patiṃ ramayati sphuṭaṃ || 11.93 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.12>
śrībhagavānuvāca
trivikramanakhodbhinnājāṇḍakharpararandhrataḥ |
antaḥ praviṣaṭabāhyāmbu vaikuṇṭhaśikharāttataḥ(dadhaḥ) || 12.1 ||
patatkamaṇaḍule dhātā dadhāreśa padāmbuje |
prakṣālyataccheṣajalādgaṅāgānāmanadī vyadhāth || 12.2 ||
aranāmnīṇyanāmnī sā bhūdurgābhāgayordvayoḥ |
pṛthaksaptāvarṇagā parighā rūpiṇī matā || 12.3 ||
nandinī satyaloke sā nalinī ca tapomaye |
janoloke mālinīti maharloke tu bhoginī || 12.4 ||
svardhunī sā svargaloke viyadgaṅgā dhruvālaye |
madāpagā sāntarikṣe merorbrahmasadordhvataḥ || 12.5 ||
nipatantī ca pūrvādi caturdikṣu krameṇa sā |
sītācā(?a)lakālakanandā ca cakṣurbhadrāhvayā śubhā || 12.6 ||
sītākhyātiravodvegātsarvatrelāvṛtādbahiḥ |
nipātyamānā hariṇā pūrvamabdhiṅgamiṣyati || 12.7 ||
saivadakṣiṇabhāgasthā kālena mahatā kila |
sagarātmajaśuddhyarthaṃ bhagīrathasamarcitā || 12.8 ||
bhāgīrathīti gaditā sarvalokaikapāvinī |
jahnunā rājaḥṛṣiṇā pītātyaktā ca vākyataḥ || 12.9 ||
sā jāhnavī merunagasyottarādhaḥsthalasthitāt |
mārgādatalapūrveṣu saptalokeṣu saṃsthitā || 12.10 ||
saiva tripathagā devī bhāti nārāyaṇāśrayā |
saiṣā ramyaka(?ta)pūrveṣu harivarṣādiṣu triṣu || 12.11 ||
bhadrāśvaketumāle tu carantī sāgarapriyā |
bhāratesminmahāvarṣe nadyaḥ santi sahasraśaḥ || 12.12 ||
śrīraṅgo veṅkaṭeśākhyo totādriḥ puṣkarāhvayaḥ |
śrīmaṣṇanaimiṣāraṇye gaṇḍakī badarīsthalaṃ || 12.13 ||
aṣṭāvete svayaṃvyaktāḥ bhārate sarvasiddhidāḥ |
svāmipuṣkaraṇīpūrvāḥ puṣkariṇyaḥ sahasraśaḥ || 12.14 ||
manavaḥ pitaraḥ siddhāḥ sarvasādhanatatparāḥ |
surāsurā bhāratesminsvasādhanaparāyaṇāḥ || 12.15 ||
atra satkarmaṇā svargaṃ nirayaṃ pāpakarmaṇā |
miththā jñānena ca tamo jñānenaiva padaṃ hareḥ || 12.16 ||
prāpnoti puruṣo hyatra padasthāstatvadevatāḥ |
kecitsarvāvatāreṣu bhūmau bhūtvā bhavantyalaṃ || 12.17 ||
sarvesurāḥ padasthāśca rāmakṛṣṇāvatārayoḥ |
jātā bhūmāvupāsyeśaṃ punarmūlasvarūpakaiḥ || 12.18 ||
ekī bhavantyapi surāḥ bhāvinaḥ prāyaśotra tu |
svasādhanaikaniratāḥ devadāsādayopi ca || 12.19 ||
sāṃśāḥ niraṃśāśca tathā svasvasādhanatatparāḥ |
vidhayaśca niṣedhādyāḥ atratyānāṃ bhavanti hi || 12.20 ||
lakṣayojanavistīrṇalavaṇāmbudhiveṣṭitaḥ |
dvīpoyaṃ jambvākhyo hi merustasyaiva madhyamaḥ || 12.21 ||
sumeroparitaścelāvṛtaṃ tadgavanaṃ śubhaṃ |
sukumārābhidhaṃ rudraśāpāttatragataḥ pumān || 12.22 ||
nārībhavati pārvatyārantukāmastathākarot |
etasmādviguṇaḥ plakṣastvikṣūdāmbudhiveṣṭhitaḥ || 12.23 ||
sthūlāśca parvatāḥ santi tatra śrīharisaṃmitāḥ |
tāste kṣīrābdhimārabhya ikṣusāgarasaṃspṛśāḥ || 12.24 ||
bhāratādyaṣṭavarṣeṣu ṣaḍdvīpā aparā api |
madhyastatkāmyapuṇyānāṃ bhogārthaṃ harinirmitāḥ || 12.25 ||
pāpādatra janīnaiva puṇyādeva hi kāmyataḥ |
na tatra cāyurnayamo yāvatpuṇyantu jīvanaṃ || 12.26 ||
narānāryaḥ sundarāṅgāḥ santi bhogocitārthadāḥ |
vṛkṣāśca puṇyaratnāni gṛhāśca svarṇabhūmayaḥ || 12.27 ||
vinaiva kālamaraṇaṃ tatra duḥkhādikaṃ na hi |
icchāmātreṇa sarvāthasiddhistatreśanirmitaḥ || 12.28 ||
sūryātmakaṃ hariṃ tatra pūjayanti vicakṣaṇāḥ |
tato hi dviguṇodvīpaḥ śālmalaḥ sarvavartulaḥ || 12.29 ||
yojanānāṃ caturlakṣaṃ madhvabdhi samaveṣṭitaḥ |
jāmbavaṃ puṣkaraṃ tyaktvā madhyadvīpeṣu pañcasu || 12.30 ||
sarveṣu saptanadyaḥ syuḥ parvatāḥ saptakīrtitāḥ |
saptadvīpagatā nadyaḥ dadbāhyāmbudhigāminaḥ || 12.31 ||
bhogopayuktā nikhilāḥ padārthajanasaṅkulāḥ |
tatratyāḥ parvatāḥ pūrvottarasāgarasaṃspṛśaḥ || 12.32 ||
tairvibhaktāḥsaptavarṣāstattanmanusutrāśrayāḥ |
candrāntaryāmiṇaṃ viṣṇuṃ śālmalasthā bhajanti hi || 12.33 ||
krauñcadvīpastadviguṇodvyaṣṭalakṣārṇave na tu |
pavyambunā veṣṭitolaṃ sūryakāntādi sanmaṇiḥ || 12.34 ||
varuṇāntargataṃ viṣṇuṃ bhajanti hi tatotra hi |
tato hi dviguṇāḥ śākadvīpaḥ kṣīrābdhiveṣṭhitaḥ || 12.35 ||
dvātriṃśallakṣavistāraḥ samīropāsakoditaḥ |
śākadvīpasya madhyetu anantarūpī harisvayaṃ || 12.36 ||
ardhalakṣasthalatanuḥ saptalakṣonnataḥ prabhuḥ |
merulokālokanagābhimukhānantalasanmukhaḥ || 12.37 ||
cakraprākārarūpātmā tadvīpe paritaḥ sadā |
lakṣonnatastheṣu jagaccakṣuḥsūryaścaratyasau || 12.38 ||
meruṃ pradakṣiṇī kurvanrūkṣacakronuvegataḥ |
dinedinetvekavāraṃ sahasrāṃśuḥ prakāśakṛth || 12.39 ||
sūryasyākṣottarāgrantu tailayantramivolitaḥ |
luptamanyaccakrayutaṃ mānasottaraparvate || 12.40 ||
paribhramanti tanmadhye'nasi bhūmeḥ samonnate |
catvāriṃśadyojanordhvapradeśaspraśimadhyataḥ || 12.41 ||
tāvatparimitānyakṣasambandho tāvadantarāḥ |
stambhāḥ ṣaṣṭhisahasrāśca catvāraḥ santi sadṛḍhāḥ || 12.42 ||
śatayojanakā sthūlā dīpyadhvajramayāḥ śubhāḥ |
tadūrdhvamaṇṭape pādalakṣoccamaṇi dīpite || 12.43 ||
yojanāyuta dedīpyamaṇḍale bhānurājatiḥ(rūrjitaḥ) |
ahaḥkaraḥ sarva satsādhanakṛtphaladoryamā || 12.44 ||
meroḥ pūrvasthito bhāratasthānāmudayaṅkaraḥ |
merordakṣiṇagomadhya(dhyaṃ)dinakṛtsaptasandhayaḥ || 12.45 ||
meroḥ paścimago bhāratatsthānāmastamānakṛt |
meroruttaragaścārdharātrikṛdbhārate nṝṇāṃ || 12.46 ||
evameva caturdikṣuvidyamānajanasya tu |
prātarmadhyandinaniśīthārdharātrapravartakaḥ || 12.47 ||
nārāyaṇa viśeṣāveśaidhitātmagatiḥ svayaṃ |
ekameva pṛthaṅnāmabhedena śrīharīcchayā || 12.48 ||
saptadvīpeṣu yobhānuvaripohastabādhipaḥ |
savyena bhramatāṃ bhānāṃ trilakṣonnatavartināṃ || 12.49 ||
tiryagūrdhvaṃ ca ṣaṭpañcāśallakṣādhikakoṭitaḥ |
dīrghābhiśca śalākābhiḥ nirmite rūkṣacakrake || 12.50 ||
śatayojanavistīrṇaṃ śalākāgreṣu bhāntyalaṃ |
vasanti tanmārgagatāḥ sañcaranti navagrahāḥ || 12.51 ||
te śalākā vajramayāḥ aṣṭottaraśatātmakaiḥ |
chidraiśca kalitāpāśaiḥ dhravādhīnaiśca vāruṇaiḥ || 12.52 ||
sambandhāgrābhiraṃśena klpyaikaikenabhāśrayāḥ |
catuścidrayutāḥ sarvaśalākāsteṣu vāruṇāḥ || 12.53 ||
pāśāścāntaḥ praviṣṭāśca bhūmerlakṣonnatāntare |
sthitasya sūryarathāgretāḥ akṣakoṭyastathāgrataḥ || 12.54 ||
dhurāgrākṣāgrayorbaddhapāśāgreṇasamanvitāḥ |
pāśādhruvasya nakṣatrapādairevāpasavyataḥ || 12.55 ||
saṃvatsareṇaikavāraṃ bhramantaṃ bhrāmayatyalaṃ |
dhruvastasya hayāḥsaptaharidvarṇā mahājavāḥ || 12.56 ||
anuktastātvikaistulyāstadyantā garuḍāgrajaḥ |
so'nūrurustvaruṇaḥsarpapāśaistānyojayatyalaṃ || 12.57 ||
mānasottarameroḥ tu saṃspraṣṭākṣa sumadhyage |
aneharmyakalitavajrasambhāvyagramaṇṭape || 12.58 ||
carato bhāskarasyāsyatvādhāraḥ śākamadhyagaḥ |
harirmeroḥsārdhasaptottarasaptatilakṣakaḥ || 12.59 ||
tāvattaduttaragata mānasottaraparvate |
yojanānāṃ ṣaṣṭhisahasronnataṃ cakramūrjitaṃ || 12.60 ||
madhyachidrānvitaṃ vajramayaṃ bhramati viṣṇunā |
dvādaśāraṃ hi ṣaṇṇemi trināḍyantarbahisthale || 12.61 ||
yugakūbarākṣatrayasya rekhāsu tithivārapāḥ |
apareṣu māsāstiṣṭhanti ṛtavastasya nemigāḥ || 12.62 ||
caturmāsyāstrinemisthāḥ dvipariścedageyane |
vatsaraḥ sarvacakrasthastvekacakrorathasya tu || 12.63 ||
sa karkaṭādiṣu caranekohnā(hnāṃ)kṣayakṛdraviḥ |
ṣaṭsurātradīrghyamānaḥ kārayandakṣiṇāyanaṃ || 12.64 ||
karoti nakrādiṣu ṣaṭsucaranrātrikṣaya(yaṃ)karaḥ |
ahnānyabhyedhayannekaścottarāyaṇakārakaḥ || 12.65 ||
vināḍikānāṃ pañcāśanmāsimāsyedheyetyasau |
ahānyuttarāṇenyatra rātrikālardhikṛttathā || 12.66 ||
yadā tu meṣatulayoḥ caratyeṣa samāniśā |
dyasrāśca vṛścikādyeṣu pañcasvapi niśārdhikṛth || 12.67 ||
vṛṣādi pañcasu carandivākālasamardhikṛt |
trilakṣonnatanakṣatraśalākāgrāṣṭaviṃśake || 12.68 ||
udagdakṣiṇakoṇādyāḥ kṛttikādyāḥ samāśritāḥ |
tiryagūrdhvaṃ saptasaptaśalakābhirvinirmitaṃ || 12.69 ||
dvābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ koṇayostu rekhābhyāmupakalpitaṃ |
nakṣatramaṇḍalaṃ bhūmerūrdhvaṃ lakṣatrayeṇa hi || 12.70 ||
ekaikasyāṃ śalākāyāṃ chidrāṇi ca pṛthakpṛthak |
catvāri tāni daṃsrakṣādyādikāni vanāni ca || 12.71 ||
meṣāṅkāni vṛṣāṅkāni nṛyugmakāni vai nava |
karkasiṃhāṅgakanyāni tulakā vṛścikāni vai || 12.72 ||
dhanurnakṣatraśaṅkhāni mīnāṅkāni ca vai nava |
teṣu teṣu praviṣṭāśca pādadyaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ || 12.73 ||
pādairdhuvaḥsasūryastu apasavyakrameṇa vai |
sarvanakṣatrapādeṣu kramādvṛddhau bhaviṣyati || 12.74 ||
dhruvāntarhariṇā viṃśadghaṭikāntadinatrayaṃ |
ekaika nakṣatrapāde baddho bhavati vai kramāth || 12.75 ||
sārdhatrayodaśādinairekanakṣatrago raviḥ |
māsena navapādāni bhuñjandvādaśa rāśiṣu || 12.76 ||
saṃvatsareṇa carati hyapradakṣiṇato raviḥ |
meṣādhyaṅkitarandhrāṇi meṣādyāḥ rāśayasmṛtāḥ || 12.77 ||
lakṣonnatapradeśestha dhruvapādāgrago raviḥ |
dvilakṣonnatadeśastha dhruvapādāgrataḥ śaśī || 12.78 ||
candrasūryau ṛkṣacakrādadhau deśagatau matau |
kujādayaśca pañcāpi ṛkṣacakrordhvavartinaḥ || 12.79 ||
dhruvapāśaiḥ sadābaddhāḥ sañcaratyapasavyataḥ |
jñaḥ pañcalakṣonnatagataḥ saptalakṣonnataḥ kujaḥ || 12.80 ||
navalakṣonnataḥ śukraḥ lakṣaikādaśako guruḥ |
trayodaśaṃ tu sūryasyamaṇḍalaṃ parikīrtitaṃ || 12.81 ||
lakṣapañcādaśādūrdhvaṃ śanimaṇḍalamasti hi |
ṣaḍviṃśati sulakṣāṇāṃ ūrdhvaṃ saptarṣimaṇḍalaṃ || 12.82 ||
lakṣaikādaśatastatmātdhruvaḥsaṃstho haripriyaḥ |
sa viṣṇoḥ śiṃśumārasya mahācakrapadāsthitāḥ || 12.83 ||
dhruvādīngrahānviṣṇuḥ cakre sarvānsuvāruṇaiḥ |
pārśaiyorjayati śrīmānśalākārandhrasaṅgatāḥ || 12.84 ||
sarvehyete bhānusamāpradeśeṣūttarottaraṃ |
caranti ṛkṣapādasthapāśairevatu tanmitaiḥ || 12.85 ||
eteṣāṃ rāśisañcāraiḥ kālaṃ yojayatīśvaraḥ |
candrorāśidvādaśakaṃ māsenaikena vartate || 12.86 ||
kujo māsārdhato rāśiṃ bhuktvā rāśiṃ sameṣyati |
gururdvādaśabhirmāsaiḥ triṃśanmāsaiḥśanaiścaraḥ || 12.87 ||
māsenaiva budhaḥ śukraḥ pratirāśiṃ bhajatyalaṃ |
sadā samagatī vakraśūnyau dvau śaśibhāskarau || 12.88 ||
bhānumaṇḍalagaścandro darśecāstaṅgato bhavet |
evaṃ kujādayaḥ pañca astaṃ yāsyanti bhānugāḥ || 12.89 ||
prākdiśyastaṅgatau śukragurū dvau paścimodayau |
pratyagastaṅgatau tau dvau tu pūrvadiśyuditau matau || 12.90 ||
evamanyepi sūryasya nāstamānaṃ svabhāvataḥ |
meruchāyāgataṃ cāstaṅgataṃ sūryaṃ vadanti hi || 12.91 ||
candrārka kavyastamaye laukike tu śubhaṃ tyajet |
pūrvasyāṃ diśi śukrostaṅgato māsadvayotthitaḥ || 12.92 ||
pratyagastaṅgataḥ kāvyaḥ saptāhobhirudetyalaṃ |
gururmāsenobhayatra hyudito bhavati dhruvaṃ || 12.93 ||
kujādīnāṃ vakramādyāstambha ṛjvoti śīghrataḥ |
gatayo vividhāḥsanti kālasyāśubhasūcakāḥ || 12.94 ||
na samā hi gatisteṣāṃ kvacideva samāgatiḥ |
lakṣadīrghayugadvandva purasthāṃ rajayojitāḥ || 12.95 ||
tadvādaśāṃśadhuragāḥ saptāśvā bhaskarasya hi |
mānasottarasyaparito navakoṭisuvistaraṃ || 12.96 ||
sārdhaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kartuṃ yojayanti mahājavāḥ |
itthaṃ navagrahaiḥ kālacakraṃ paramaḥ pumān || 12.97 ||
sūryasya gatyā meṣādīnmāsānsaureṇa mānataḥ |
kalpayatyapi candrasyagatyā caitrādīkānhariḥ || 12.98 ||
cāndramāsānkalpayanti nakṣatraṃ ṛkṣasantateḥ |
āvṛtyaṃ caturorāptiṃ cārānmānaṃ bṛhaspateḥ || 12.99 ||
sārasvatantu gāndharvāḥ pakṣāhorātrayo matāḥ |
sārasvatantu gāndharvamānamāhurvicakṣaṇāḥ || 12.100 ||
māsena syādahorātraṃ maitraṃ mānaṃ vidurbudhāḥ |
daivamānaṃ vatsarāhorātrikālena tāvatā || 12.101 ||
manurmānaṃ mānavākhyaṃ brāhmamānamajasya hi |
mānāśca navamānānāṃ viṣṇurekaḥ pravartakaḥ || 12.102 ||
dvātriṃśallakṣavistīrṇakṣīrābdhermadhyadeśataḥ |
tadardhavistaroccakramaprākārarūpakaḥ || 12.103 ||
daśalakṣonnato bhūmeḥ śvetadvīpo ramātmakaḥ |
vedāvarṇā deśakālau nityavyāptā jaḍāmatāḥ || 12.104 ||
vāyvākāśaḥsauradīptiranityāstu jaḍāmatāḥ |
dravyānyapratighātāni tenyonyasyāśrayāṇi ca || 12.105 ||
lakṣmīnārāyaṇau śuddhacidrūpau vyāptavigrahau |
sarvārthapratighātā ca śvetadvīpādayastrayaḥ || 12.106 ||
lokāstvapratighātāśca stambhādhyairlokarūpakāḥ |
durgābhāgastadupari bhūbhāgastu tadūrdhvataḥ || 12.107 ||
śrībhāgastu tatastūrdhvaṃ śikharantu tadūrdhvagaḥ |
bhāgatrayepi bhūkandāḥ pañcalakṣonnatāyutāḥ || 12.108 ||
triṣubhāgeṣvapi ramā deśaharmyasvarūpiṇī |
harmyaṃ pradeśa bhūbhāgāḥ lakṣayojanakandakāḥ || 12.109 ||
uparyāvarttayo bhāgastrayo bhūkandasaṃmitāḥ |
bhāgatrayepi ca stambhāmaṇimadbhūmitalāśrayāḥ || 12.110 ||
sārdhadvilakṣasamitā nānāmaṇivirājitā |
śrībhāgamadhyavedisthaḥ lakṣmīśonantabhānuruk || 12.111 ||
cinmātrānantamukteḍyaḥ svargādupari vartate |
sthānaṃ traye tripādviṣṇoḥ divisvargonnatasthale || 12.112 ||
śvetadvīpodvyaṣṭalakṣa durgābhāgastu tanmitaḥ |
bhūbhāgastu tadardhetaḥ śrībhāgastu tadardhataḥ || 12.113 ||
maṇisvarṇojvalatsatvaprākārāntātmabhāgakāḥ |
anekatoraṇāṭṭālavedīcatvāraśobhitāḥ || 12.114 ||
maṇimatnanadyadrisarovāpyādisaṃkulāḥ |
nānāyādogaṇopetasvarṇakañjābu bhāsvarāḥ || 12.115 ||
sarvartukālapuṣpāḍhya pallavasvarṇabhūruhāḥ |
haṃsakokilabarhādi śukasvanasamākulāḥ || 12.116 ||
atisundara siṃhādi nānāmṛgasamākulāḥ |
avarṇanīyaratneru racitānekamaṇṭapāḥ || 12.117 ||
nānāsvādusvanopeta naranārīgaṇauryutāḥ |
siddhacāraṇagandharvāpsaro gānamaṇitāḥ || 12.118 ||
nānāvādyamahāvīṇāśaṅkhadundubhinisvanāḥ |
gajāśvarathapādātidhvajachatrādi lāñchanāḥ || 12.119 ||
sarvābharaṇavastrāḍhyā vimānāvalisaṅkulāḥ |
nānākrīḍāsādhanetāḥ nānāḥ gandhopabṛṃhitāḥ || 12.120 ||
anekahaṃsatūle tu maṇiśayyā samāvṛtāḥ |
bhogastrībhiḥ samākīrṇāḥ maṇivyajanadarpaṇāḥ || 12.121 ||
maṇisvarṇātmakāneka bhittibhāṇḍādi sādhanaiḥ |
anuktānantasarvopaskaraṇairupaśobhitāḥ || 12.122 ||
cāmarāndolikāḍolā pādapīṭhādi saṃyutāḥ |
tulasīpuṣpasadratnamuktāmālopaśobhitāḥ || 12.123 ||
bhānukoṭiprabhādīptā pātranaivedyarūpiṇī |
ghṛtadadhyamburūpā sā pūrvoktārthasvarūpiṇī || 12.124 ||
dāsīdāsasamākīrṇabhāryārūpākhileśvarī |
durgābhūḥśrīsvabhāgeṣu anantānantarūpiṇī || 12.125 ||
anantarūpiṇaṃ viṣṇuṃ sadārādhayatīśvaraṃ |
svabhāvādeva nāsyārthaḥ prāptavyaḥ kaścideṣyate || 12.126 ||
pūrṇakāmo hariḥ prītyā tatpūjāṃ svīkarotyajaḥ |
sthānatrayepi sā devī hyanantoktārtharūpiṇī || 12.127 ||
sarvadeśeṣu kāleṣu sarvarūpāśritā ramā || 12.128 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.13>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
ye proktā devagandharvakarmajñānāstvatātvikāḥ |
pakvādhikāriṇo bhūmerutkrāntā maharādiṣu || 13.1 ||
sthitā vipaśeṣoktamārgeṇa svottameṣu te |
bhogadehapratiṣaṭhāstu aṇḍāntadvirajātale || 13.2 ||
(yu)tyaktaliṅgāsturyavaśā aṇḍanāśa upāgate |
parāṣṭamāṃśāvaśiṣṭakāle śraharigarbhagāḥ || 13.3 ||
muktā api na muktākhyāḥ punarbrahmāṇḍasambhave |
viṣṇorbahirgatāḥ kukṣeḥ śuddhasātvakadehakaiḥ || 13.4 ||
ramādattairbhogayogyapūrvoktānantasātvikaiḥ |
arthairabhyunnatāśvetadvīpasthā harimañjasā || 13.5 ||
dṛṣṭvā natvā prasādyeśaṃ muktākhyāṃ prāpyacājñayā |
avyāhateṣṭagatayo vaikuṇṭhe śuddhasātvikaiḥ || 13.6 ||
dehairmodanti bhūdurgābhāgayoḥ śrīśalālitāḥ |
cinmātradehaiḥ śrībhāge na cātra jaḍasaṃsthitāḥ || 13.7 ||
vṛkṣapakṣimṛgā hyabdhinadyopi navakoṭayaḥ |
sarāṃsi nānāvidhabaddhā api lokeṣu vai hareḥ || 13.8 ||
śrībhūdurgābhāga ete sevante harimañjasā |
svādhikyasya sidhyarthaṃ svānandādhikyakāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 13.9 ||
kevalaṃ liṅgadehena bhogabhāge vasanti hi |
(bhūbhāge saṃvasanti hi) niraṃśānāṃ sūkṣmadehāḥ aṇḍāntaḥsatyalokagā tu || 13.10 ||
aniruddhādalpakālavyatyayātprabhavanti hi |
layeṇḍanāśātpurataḥ natatsū(nāśaḥ sū)kṣmatanorbhaveth || 13.11 ||
tṛṇādārabhya sanmartyagandharvāsta(nta)sya kṛtsnaśaḥ |
eṣāṃ sūkṣmatanuḥ sṛṣṭiḥ kramādyuktamatolayaḥ || 13.12 ||
sraṣṭvaiṣvevāmartyagandharvādikarmyante(nta) devatāḥ |
durgābhāgasthāniruddhātsūkṣmayuktā bhavanti hi || 13.13 ||
sṛṣṭikramādvyutkrameṇa nāśastatragatāddhareḥ |
aṇḍanāśātpuraivete kevalaṃ liṅgamātragāḥ || 13.14 ||
tṛṇādyāśacātātvikāśca bhūbhāge saṃvasanti hi |
eṣāṃ śrībhāgaparikhābhūta śrīvirajā jale || 13.15 ||
aṇḍanāśātpurābhagnaliṅgānāṃ turyasaṃśrayaṃ |
prāptānāṃ śrīharergabhe tadāvāso bhaviṣyati || 13.16 ||
bhūmāvutkramamāṇāstu dehantyaktvā maharjanāḥ |
tapaḥ satyeṣu durgākhyabhāge nīcoccakāḥ kramāth || 13.17 ||
vasanti dehabhogaiste(bhogadehaiḥ) niraṃśānāntu bhogataḥ |
śīrṇā bhavanti hi aniruddhena ye cate || 13.18 ||
praviṣṭāni bhavanti hi aniruddhena ye cate |
aniruddhe praviṣṭāni saṅkarṣaṇajayāya te || 13.19 ||
sṛṣṭānyato liṅgamuktāḥ prabhavanti hyatātvikāḥ |
bhogedehaiḥ svottameṣu praviṣṭāḥ prabhavanti hi || 13.20 ||
gandharvāṇāṃ kuberantu pitṝṇāṃ yama eva hi |
kṛṣṭāṅgasaṅga gopīnāṃ sarvajātiṣu saptateḥ || 13.21 ||
mitānāmājānajānāṃ sastrīkāṇāṃ dhanādhipe |
tatsamānāstu ṛṣayaḥ śatonaśatakoṭayaḥ || 13.22 ||
eteṣāṃ tulayodakṣe hyagniputragaṇasya tu |
vahnau karmajadevānāṃ layaḥ svāyambhuve mataḥ || 13.23 ||
tasminnekādaśamitāmanavovidhyatātvikāḥ |
tātvikau dvau saptamādyau yau tau manupadeśvarau || 13.24 ||
nārāyaṇaḥ svayaṃ viṣṇuḥ tāpasomanurīritaḥ |
vinā tānstrīnmanūnsvāyambhuve laya udīritaḥ || 13.25 ||
ādya indro hariryajñaḥ vāyudharmośvinau kramāt |
ṣaṣṭastu mandradyumnanāmā saptamastu purandaraḥ || 13.26 ||
dvau śacīramaṇāṃśau tau ṣaḍaite tātvikāḥ smṛtāḥ |
bhāvīndrāḥ karmajābhāste teṣāmādyamanau layaḥ || 13.27 ||
saptakānāṃ ṛṣīṇāntu śatasya ca layaḥ smṛtaḥ |
dakṣe dharme ca tattulyapitṝṇāṃ saptakasya tu || 13.28 ||
tatsamāstvaṣṭagandharvāstvaṣṭona śatayoṣitaḥ |
urvaśyādyāstatsamāhi kuberaṃ praviśanti hi || 13.29 ||
ato niraṃśāstūtkrānti yoginastu ta(nasta)duttamāḥ |
atātvikāstu bhūlokādutkrānta maharādigāḥ || 13.30 ||
bhogadehairuttamaistu bhakṣitāḥ prabhavanti hi |
utkramāṇutkrameṇa gāyanyoginastānatho jaguḥ || 13.31 ||
nityasaṃsāriṇaḥ pṛthvyāṃ tyaktvā sthūlantu sūkṣmataḥ |
dharme praviśyasantyaktānādyavidyā bhavanti hi || 13.32 ||
kecidatraiva mucyante kecidvaimerumūrdhani |
vaiśvānare dyunadyāṃ vā sūrye vā deha eva vā || 13.33 ||
tyaktvāvidyāṃ miśrapākālaye nābhau harergatāḥ |
svargabhūmyādiṣu sṛṣṭau bhramanti śrīharīcchayā || 13.34 ||
laye'yogyā garbhodagatasaṅkarṣaṇāgninā |
pātāle nityanarake bhagnasūkṣmakalevarāḥ || 13.35 ||
vāyorgadāprahāreṇa bhagnaliṅgāstamonugāḥ |
laye sṛṣṭau tamasyandhe patanti śrī harīcchayā || 13.36 ||
tatvābhimānino devāḥ hyaṇaḍāntarbahirūpataḥ |
anutkramāḥ svottameṣu vipaśeṣādi mārgataḥ || 13.37 ||
tyaktadehāḥ sūkṣmadehaiḥ satyaloke vasanti hi |
naṣṭe satye brahmadehe naṣṭe taiḥ saha padmajāḥ || 13.38 ||
aṇḍanāśa upāvṛtte viśeṣaṃ yātitaiḥ saha |
atrasthitaiḥsūkṣmadaihaiḥ sarvatatrasthasūkṣmakaiḥ || 13.39 ||
ekībhavantyevamekaviṃśattatvagaṇā(tā) api |
vyutkrameṇa tu bhūmyādi tatvopādāna sañcaye || 13.40 ||
sthale tathā nābhimānivaśage sati viṣṇunā |
annādi nāmnādevaistadūrdhvamūrdhvagatakramāth || 13.41 ||
tyaktānyāvaraṇānyete pūrvamānena nāśanaṃ |
yanti pūrvoktamānena aniruddhādivatkramāth || 13.42 ||
saṅkarṣaṇādvinaṣṭe(ṣu) tu sūkṣmadeheṣu kṛtsnaśaḥ |
tadā satvārdha śrībhāgasthalakṣmyālakṣmyātmake jale || 43 ||
(pradhānavirajā nadyāmityarthaḥ)
nīcakramātbhagnaliṅgāḥ viśanti jaṭhare hareḥ |
punaraṇḍe samutpanne śvetadvīpe samāpya ca || 13.44 ||
muktau bhavatyasau lokaḥ kṣīrasāgaramadhyagaḥ |
lakṣmyaiścatuṣaṣṭhimitaiḥ vistṛtaṃ(visṛjaḥ) puṣkarāhvayaḥ || 13.45 ||
dvīpaḥ kṣīrābdhiparito maṇivajramayadyutiḥ |
tanmadhyadeśaparito vartulo mānasottaraḥ || 13.46 ||
mahīdharoyutonnahaḥ dvisahasrasuvistaraḥ |
dhṛḍho vajramayaḥ saptarathacakraṃ raveḥ sthitaṃ || 13.47 ||
tadantarmartyasaṃsāraḥ tadvāhyomartyagocaraḥ |
dvīpāyāmasamaśuddhavārisāgaraveṣṭitaḥ || 13.48 ||
tasyāpi parito vajralepikāsvarṇabhūḥ śubhā |
caturlakṣanyūnakoṭivistarāttadgato nagaḥ || 13.49 ||
lokālokābhidhovartulākāro parito giriḥ |
maṇisvarṇamayaḥ prākārākāro nirmalo mahān || 13.50 ||
sa pañcāśatsahasretatpañcāśallakṣavistṛtiḥ |
tāvataivocchritastasmātardhalakṣādhikonnataḥ || 13.51 ||
nānāvṛkṣāśrayo yatra bhāti pūrvoditaṃ jagat |
tadaṣṭadikṣu śikharāṇyapi chidrānvitāni ca || 13.52 ||
śatonnatāni digdantigaṇāstaduparisthitāḥ |
tacchidraveṣṭitaḥ śuṇḍādaṇḍairūttaṃbhayanti te(taiḥ) || 13.53 ||
gotrante cāntarikṣasthāḥ haryāveśayutāḥ śubhāḥ |
te ca pūrvādyaṣṭadikṣu garbhodasthāgataṃ(tā) bhuvaṃ || 13.54 ||
ūrdhvościtrairātmahastairdharanti śrīharīcchayā |
te mānasottare merau svarge rūpāntarasthitāḥ || 13.55 ||
kurvanti śrī harerājñāṃ dikpālānāṃ ca vāhanāḥ |
anantarūpairbhagavānuparistaṃnnagordhvagaḥ || 13.56 ||
ūrdhvādhaḥ śikharaspṛṣṭānantamūrdhvoti yo hariḥ |
lokālokāntarāle tu sūryendvṛkṣagrahaprabhāḥ || 13.57 ||
vasiṣṭhanāradātryādyānantagaṃ svarṇabhūmigaṃ |
vadanti pūrvadvīpeṣu yathā sūryo(pūrvo)dayāntare || 13.58 ||
yathā tathaiva giriśṛṅge maitreyapūrvakāḥ |
svarṇabhūmimatītyaivasthitamāhurnagottamaṃ || 13.59 ||
taduktamāhuḥ paiṅgyādyāḥ tannetyāhurmṛ(ka)kuṇḍajaḥ |
merulokālokamadhyapradeśastu trikoṭikaḥ || 13.60 ||
yojanānāṃ sārthakoṭisaṃmitaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
lokālokadha(ra)bhūmyāsahitaḥ parimāṇataḥ || 13.61 ||
andhantamastannagasya paritaḥ saptakoṭikaḥ |
yatra mithyājñānayuktāḥ patanti śatakoṭiśaḥ || 13.61 ||
harīrasuragurvāyarviṣṇuvaiṣṇavavidviṣaḥ |
niṣkāraṇaṃ sādhuyoṣidvedaśāstra vidūṣakāḥ || 13.62 ||
bhaktileśavihīnāśca nityanaimittikojjitāḥ |
anantapāpakaraṇe bhayaleśavivarjitāḥ || 13.63 ||
jīvābhedo nirguṇatvamapūrṇaguṇatā tathā |
sāmyādhikye tadanyeṣāṃ bhedastadgata eva ca || 13.64 ||
prādurbhāvaviparyāsaḥ tadbhaktadveṣa eva ca |
tatpramāṇasya nindā ca dveṣā etekhilā matāḥ || 13.65 ||
samprāpya bhārate janma viṣṇudveṣādi pākataḥ |
yathā yogyaṃ vaiparītyā parokṣaguruvidviṣaḥ || 13.66 ||
pakvasvarūpavidveṣādvyudasyātmīya sañcitaṃ |
bhuktvā duṣprārabdhaphalaṃ pūrṇaṃ pramādikā gataṃ || 13.67 ||
suprārabdhaphalaṃ viṣṇudveṣādalpīkṛtaṃ hareḥ |
bhuktvāgāmi vyudasyaiva hyāgāmi dveṣayākṛtaḥ(pākataḥ) || 13.68 ||
prāptavyaduḥkhamicchanti sthūladehānviṣāgnibhiḥ |
śastratyaktvā yātanādyaiḥ nityatāmiśranāmakaḥ(rakāḥ) || 13.69 ||
aṇḍanāśātpurā sūkṣmadehāntatra visṛjya ca |
vāyorgadāghātataśca liṅgabhaṅgo bhaviṣyati || 13.70 ||
ciraṃ śrīharyūrudeśe klptaduḥkhabhujaḥ khalāḥ |
punaraṇḍe samutpanne tamasyandhe patatyalaṃ || 13.71 ||
martyādhamā niraṃśāśca piśācāḥ svalpatāmasāḥ |
teṣu kecittatvadevābhāsarūpāstvadhastane || 13.72 ||
svargādiṣu carantaste pāpakarmābhimāninaḥ |
daityāścarākṣasāścaiva sāṃśāste bahurūpiṇaḥ || 13.73 ||
rasātalasthāniruddhātprāksūkṣmaṃ dehamāśritāḥ |
karmānuvaśataḥ sthūladehānprāpya mahāsuraiḥ || 13.74 ||
svayaṃ naṣṭā parānsādhūnnāśayanti kusādhanaiḥ |
tamasyandhe lakṣanimnodhyuparyasthale sthitāḥ || 13.75 ||
prāptavyaduḥkhaṃ bhuñjanti śuddhaṃ tāmasavigrahaiḥ |
pūyāsṛṅmūtraviṣṭādyaiḥ taptatailāstraśastrakaiḥ || 13.76 ||
adṛṣṭāśrutaduḥkhāpti sādhanocitavastubhiḥ |
durgayākalpitaiḥ ghoraiḥ nirghātādyairanekaśaḥ || 13.77 ||
siṃhavyāghrādirūpiṇyā saha tādṛksvarūpavān |
avarṇanīyaduḥkhaughaṃ vāyurdāsyati tānprati || 13.78 ||
tatprāptavyamiti prāhuḥ klptaṃ duḥkhaṃ tatopyadhaḥ |
bhuñjanti svasvarūpasthaṃ sarvarogavraṇādikaṃ || 13.79 ||
sarvakṣatādikaṃ nityaṃ pūrṇaduḥkhaṃ vrajantyalaṃ |
tato dvisaptakoṭibhyāṃ(kyāṃ) ghanodaḥ paritaḥ sthitaṃ || 13.80 ||
yatra nārāyaṇonantatanurūpātmakaśriyā |
sadā viharati śrīśo brahmādyairapi sevitaḥ || 13.81 ||
tatordhakoṭisamitamanantāsanamīśituḥ |
dhāmaṇḍakharparaspṛṣṭa viṃśallakṣocca vedimath || 13.82 ||
catvāriṃśallakṣāmito bhūbhāgaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
triṃśallakṣamitastūccaḥ śrībhāgaḥ sarvataḥ śubhaḥ || 13.83 ||
tadanyānantaguṇitaḥ śvetadvīpasamaḥ smṛtaḥ || 13.84 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.14>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
bhūmeradhastādatalo vitalaḥ sutalastataḥ |
ayutonnāhavistārastalātalamahātalau || 14.1 ||
rasātalaśca pātālahyetasmādyuttarohyadhaḥ |
dvisahasroru bhūkandāstvantarantarbhilopamāḥ || 14.2 ||
mahāmaṇiprakāśetā sūryādigrahavarjitāḥ |
meruruttarasteṣāṃ mārgaḥ sarvatra saṃmataḥ || 14.3 ||
teṣu sarveṣu kalparūhatulyāśca pādapāḥ |
kāmadhenusamāgāvaḥ cintāmaṇi samāḥ śilāḥ || 14.4 ||
tatratyānāṃ yathāyogyaṃ satāmapyasatāṃ phalaṃ |
yacchanti svacchasalilaṃ cāpi tattadgataṃ śubhaṃ || 14.5 ||
na tatrāyāsataḥ saukhyaṃ anāyāsātsukhāvahaḥ |
bhujyopayuktānantoru vastujālasamākulaḥ || 14.6 ||
yāvatkāmya śubhantāvadāyuṣaṣyamasatāmadhaḥ |
bhūmau bhāratagā ye ca kṛte lakṣāyuṣaḥ smṛtāḥ || 14.7 ||
tretādiṣu triṣu daśāṃśanyūnāyurmitānarāḥ |
ādhivyādhijarāvārdhikyāyāsetyabhidhādayaḥ || 14.8 ||
yugāyurniyamātkālamṛtyavastatra naiva hi |
tatrasthayoḥ sadasatorbhaktidveṣau vinā kvacith || 14.9 ||
tathā vidhiniṣedhau ca na ca varṇāśramādayaḥ |
na tatra karmajñānādiḥ pūrvakarmabhujo'(jau)khilāḥ || 14.10 ||
prāyasyāṃśāstadutpannāḥ bhūmau jātā vidhīśayoḥ |
varaiḥsuradviṣo viṣṇuvāyubhyāmavatārakaiḥ || 14.11 ||
tayormṛtāḥ punaḥ pūrvasthāne prārabdhakāmyake |
śubhaṃ bhuñjanti niṣkāmyaśubhaṃ teṣāṃ na vai kvacith || 14.12 ||
kāmyapāpānnārakino niṣkāmāśubhatastanūḥ |
icchantontaścarādaityāniraṃśābhāratārjitān || 14.13 ||
kāmyātteṣu vasanti sma dānavāśrayabhūtayaḥ |
sadā śeṣādikastatra vasanti sati kāraṇe || 14.14 ||
harīcchayā surāstatra vasantaḥ karmato na hi |
ete hi lakṣabhūkandamadhye lokāstatohyadhaḥ || 14.15 ||
pātāle narakāścāṣṭāviṃśatsaṃkhyā hareḥ kṛtāḥ |
tadadhastātpañcaviṃśatkoṭigarbhāmbunirmalaṃ || 14.16 ||
lakṣonaṃ yatra lakṣmīśo yādorūpī virājate |
sahasramūrdhnaḥ phaṇinastvekasminmastake tadā || 14.17 ||
sarṣapāyati vai viśvaṃ nikhilaṃ vāruṇīpateḥ |
dharākhileyaṃ sadvīpā sa sāgarakulācalā || 14.18 ||
tasyāśrayovāyukūrmaḥ tasya kūrmākṛtirhariḥ ||
taṃ dhatte jalamadhyasthā śaktirādhārarūpiṇī || 14.19 ||
saṅkarṣaṇanṛsiṃhātmā sahasrā(saṃhartā)nanta nāmakaḥ |
tadadhastāccamaṇḍūko varāhastadadhastaṭī || 14.20 ||
tamaṇḍakharparaspṛṣṭā pādamāhuradhastale |
teṣāmupari bhūlokastanmadhye merubhūdharaḥ || 14.21 ||
sa kañjanābhanābhyuttha padmanābhabisākṛtiḥ |
merumadhyotthanāloyamūrdhvaṣaṭkañjasaṃśrayaḥ || 14.22 ||
śatayojanavistīrṇaṃ satyalokāntadīrghavān |
vajrātisudhṛḍho garbhachidraṃ tene prakāśavān || 14.23 ||
lakṣonnatasthalo bhūmyāḥ bhuvarlokastadāśrayaḥ |
maṇihemāmayo lakṣabhūkanando lokasundaraḥ || 14.24 ||
saptaprākārakalitaḥ nānā bhogoruyogibhiḥ |
jaḍairartheḥsatvaguṇaprācuryaiḥ viśadālayaiḥ || 14.25 ||
bhūmeḥ koṭyunnatasvargalokoramyo'tivartulaḥ |
koṭiyojanavistāre bhuvarlokavadeva saḥ || 14.26 ||
dvilakṣabhūkandayukto merunālamupāgataḥ |
maṇihemamayottuṅga saptaprākārabhāsuraḥ || 14.27 ||
daśayojanavistārāḥ caturasrāsuvīthayaḥ |
yatrānekapatākāśca darpaṇānekasaṅkulāḥ || 14.28 ||
maṇiśayyāsanānantasātvikocitasādhanaiḥ |
pūrvapuṇyasuvarṇaśrīrāja(nma)mandiramaṇḍitāḥ || 14.29 ||
nānodyānāṣṭadikpālanilayāṣṭakabhāsurāḥ |
madhyemaṇigaṇabhrājadviṣṇumandiramaṇḍapaḥ || 14.30 ||
sarvāyudharathākīrṇacintāmaṇyādi saṅkulaiḥ |
muktāmaṇisṛjopeta sarvābharaṇasantatāḥ || 14.31 ||
divyavastrānnadānekapārijātādi saṅkulāḥ |
aṣṭāviṃśatpuradvāratoraṇāṭṭālagopurāḥ || 14.32 ||
harmyāyutavṛtānekagṛhadvaryāśritāpagāḥ | |
mandākinī maṇimayā(tkalpa)tulyarūpāmbujāśrayā || 14.33 ||
parighārūpato yatra bhāti yādogaṇānvitā |
suśabdasparśarūpetaḥ suraso(sye)ti sugandhavān || 14.34 ||
martyadevāsurairbhogya pṛthagartha samākulāḥ |
bhuvarlokastvadholokaḥ guṇaiḥśatagaṇottaraḥ || 14.35 ||
tataḥ daśaguṇodriktāḥ lokāḥ svargādayaḥ kramāt |
tathā ca tattalokasthaṃ suropasurasantataḥ || 14.36 ||
adhikārikasanmartyagandharvādyalpacetasāṃ |
yathāyogya phalaṃ teṣāṃ ādhārā(mādhāsyadvi)viṣṇuravyayaḥ || 14.37 ||
tataśca sārdhakoṭyucco maharlokastvakarmakaḥ |
tataḥ koṭidvayoccastha janolokaḥ supūjitaḥ || 14.38 ||
tato sārdhadvikoṭyuccastapolokaḥ sucidgamaḥ |
tataḥ koṭyunnatoyaṃ satyalokoti bhāsuraḥ || 14.39 ||
tricatuḥ pañcaṣallakṣabhūkandājñānagocaraḥ |
nābhyutthita mahānālasyāgrasthe satyalokage(ke) || 14.40 ||
brahmā caturmukho jāto viṣṇunā sarvalokasṛṭ |
svarlokasaviśeṣārthāḥ maharādiṣu santatāḥ || 14.41 ||
ajarotpatsamānoru nānāmartyagaṇāśrayāḥ |
etādṛśaṃ lokapadamādhyājārdhāyuṣontime || 14.42 ||
dine sṛjaddharistatra prākṛtenābhimāninī |
loko'jasya bhuvaḥ koṭitrayodaśamito mataḥ || 14.43 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ
<1.15>
śrīhaṃsaḥ
satyātkoṭirdvādaśordhvaḥ loko vaikuṇṭhanāmakaḥ |
durgābhāgastvadhodeśaḥ bhūkando daśalakṣakaḥ || 15.1 ||
tasyopariṣṭādbhūbhāgaḥ śrībhāgastu tadūrdhvataḥ |
bhūkandena trayastulyā triṃśallakṣonnataṃ mitaṃ || 15.2 ||
śikharaṃ tatribhāgeṣu mahāharmyāṇyanekaśaḥ |
maṇyātmakāni vaidvyaṣṭasaṃkhyāni ca śubhāni ca || 15.3 ||
madhye vajramayāneko paryāvaraṇamāni ca |
maharādīni catvāri hayajataṃ vistarāṇi ca || 15.4 ||
bhuvanāni yathā bhāgatraye vaikuṇṭhabhūmayaḥ |
ajāṇḍakharparaspṛṣṭāḥ madhye prākārasaptakāḥ || 15.5 ||
yuktā pūrvādidikdvārā saptasapta hiraṇmayaḥ |
puṣkarāgāvarṇayuktapadmarāgakavāṭakāḥ || 15.6 ||
indranīlaniroḍhoruvajrapaṭṭasabhāsurāḥ |
savyāpasavyo dedipyanmaṇi vedī taṭonnatāḥ || 15.7 ||
dvāri dvāri kṛtodāra ratnakañjāsanā sabhāḥ |
anantasūryabhādīptalokamaṇḍanabhūṣaṇāḥ || 15.8 ||
teṣu teṣu tribhāgeṣu ramānantānantādhirūpiṇī |
vipeśānāṃ samastānāṃ brahmaṇā saha karhicith || 15.9 ||
vaikuṇṭhādi tribhāgānāṃ darśanaṃ bhavati dhruvaṃ |
na tatra saṃsthitisteṣāṃ maharādiṣu saṃsthitiḥ || 15.10 ||
tṛṇādayo maharloke mānuṣādyāstadūrdhvataḥ |
rājānastu tapoloke satyaloke nṛgāyakāḥ || 15.11 ||
muktānāmeva baddhānāmutkrāntā(muktā)nāmihasthitiḥ |
nṛgandharvājānajāntā utkrāntāstu tapasthitāḥ || 15.12 ||
karmadevāḥsamutkramya satyaloke vasanti hi |
atātvikā liṅgamuktā sthānatrayagatā api || 15.13 ||
bhāgatrayeṣvapi na hi prākārasaṃsthitā bhavat |
tasyopariṣṭātprākārāḥ śrībhāgasya bahisthitāḥ || 15.14 ||
catuṣkoṭyātmakastasyaparitaḥ parighātmikā |
dviṣaṭkoṭyā yāmayuktā bāhyaprākārariritaḥ || 15.15 ||
ṣaṭprākārāstadantasthā koṭyantaravakāśakāḥ |
aṣṭakoṭyātmakastasya (u)pariṣṭādbahirāvṛtiḥ || 15.16 ||
śrīḥ koṭiyojanamitā virajākhyā nadīśubhā |
yadambhaḥsparśanālliṅgamuktāḥ sadyo bhavanti hi || 15.17 ||
janāstatra harirnityaṃ jalakrīḍārataḥ śritāḥ |
bhūbhārordhvāvaraṇagā bhūrevāvarāṇātmikā || 15.18 ||
tanmadhye tu tato lakṣonnata śrībhāgabhūtale |
virajā tatra dvau bhāgau muktāmuktāśrayau śubhau || 15.19 ||
evaṃ durgābhāgagato paryāvaraṇasaṅgatā |
bhūbhāgadurgā bhāgau dvau madhyo bhūrunnatākṛti || 15.20 ||
tayorūrdhvāvaraṇatatsandhigeraṇyanāmake |
nadyau durgābhūmayāmbusamite śrīharipriye || 15.21 ||
na tatra liṅgabhaṅgādi hariṇā cintitaṃ kvacit |
pradhānaparamovyomnorantarā virajā nadī || 15.22 ||
pradhānātmakadehau tau brahmavāyū sabhāryakau |
bhūbhāgordhvāvaraṇago laye sarvasuraiḥ saha || 15.23 ||
surānvināvatārāṇāṃ ārambhe ca samāptike |
kāle bhūbhāgordhvagatau syātāṃ kvāpi harīcchayā || 15.24 ||
ato bhūbhāgordhvadeśaḥ pradhānamiti kīrtitaṃ |
yadadhīnā yasya sattā tattadityeva bhaṇyate || 15.25 ||
tādṛkpradhāna saṃsṛṣṭaḥ śrībhāgaḥ paramāmbaraṃ |
ataḥ pradhānaparamo vyomagetyabhidhīyate || 15.26 ||
niraṃśatātvikānāṃ ca hyaṇḍāntastannimajjanaṃ |
tātvikānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ aniruddhāvṛti sthite || 15.27 ||
satvāraṇatastūcca pradeśe saṃsthite male |
śvetadvīpasvadhāmabhyo ekībhūteti(tvānti)(tvānte) śobhane || 15.28 ||
śrīdurgābhyāṃ ca bhūmyā ca ekībhūte vilakṣake |
śrībhāge tatrage tasya parighā virajā nadī || 15.29 ||
tajjale tu tataḥ snātvā liṅgabhaṅgaḥ pradṛśyate |
laye pṛthivyāvaraṇaṃ vyutkrāmadgacchatāṃ satāṃ || 15.30 ||
prathamāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ satvāvaraṇamantimaṃ |
taduccāvyākṛtākāśo vyomaśabdena bhaṇyate || 15.31 ||
anantā(antimā)varaṇavyomnorantarā virajetyataḥ |
sotpatyaṅgeṣu devānāṃ aṅgasāyujyamiṣyate || 15.32 ||
śrībhāgavaikuṇṭhatadgavāsudevasya cakriṇaḥ |
anantarūpe sarvāṅgasāyujyaṃ brahmaṇo bhaveth || 15.33 ||
muktasya ca svarūpeṇa hyaṇorvṛddhirna yujyate |
nohāniḥ punarāvṛttiduḥkhājñānabhayādayaḥ || 15.34 ||
nīcanīcālpamuktānāṃ pūrtiḥ svānandamātrataḥ |
naivottamamudākāṅkṣā sevādhyānādikaṃ(ka) phalaṃ || 15.35 ||
na harernaparasyāpi mudbhujaste svamudbhujaḥ |
pralayatvaṃ kvacitsṛṣṭiḥ kāle śrībhāga eva hi || 15.36 ||
brahmā cinmātradehena hyantaprākārago mataḥ |
rudrādayaḥ ṣaṣṭamagāḥ indrādyāḥ pañcamasthitāḥ || 15.37 ||
caturthasthāḥ sūryādyā tṛtīyasthāḥ marudgaṇāḥ |
dvitīyaprākārasaṃsthāḥ parjanyādyāḥ surottamāḥ || 15.38 ||
cinmātradehaiḥ śrībhāge ādye karmajapūrvakāḥ |
ete muktāstu bhūbhāge durgābhāge tathaiva ca || 15.39 ||
śuddhasātvikadehaistu prāptāstu sukhino hareḥ |
caturlakṣādhikāśītilakṣajīvagaṇasya tu || 15.40 ||
bimbātmā tattādākāro sadā tadgaḥ prarakṣati |
sarvajīvagaṇāmuktāḥ santi vaikuṇṭhamandire || 15.41 ||
jīvā(na)nāṃ pātya(pāyo)pi hariḥ sa hi rūpāntaraivibhuḥ |
sarvajīvasamātyalparūpaiḥ sthūlaiścatādṛśaiḥ || 15.42 ||
anantakoṭikalpīyamuktopāsyaḥ tribhāgagaḥ |
tādṛgrūpānantayuktaśriyā saha caratyasau || 15.43 ||
tau tatra pūjopayogyānantānantārtharū(pikā)pakau |
tatra svārūpikakirīṭādi śaṅkhacakrādi sādhanaiḥ || 15.44 ||
sudarśanādyāyudhyaiśca gandhamālyādikārhaṇaiḥ |
naivedyavastrā(saṅgādyaiḥ)śayyādyaiḥ rūpaiḥ svaiḥ svayamarcitaḥ || 15.45 ||
śriyāsadaikyamāpannaḥ modarūpyanumodate |
śrīḥ deva(vi)makhilākārā tairviṣṇuṃ pūjayatyalaṃ || 15.46 ||
tairnnimālyaiḥ viṣṇudattaiḥ ramaikī bhavati sphuṭaṃ |
bhūdurgabhāgayorevaṃ śuddhasātvikasādhanaiḥ || 15.47 ||
tanmāninī śrīḥsampūjya tannirmālyairajādikān |
muktāstoṣayati prājñāḥ gandhamālyādibhiḥ śubhaiḥ || 15.48 ||
te vimānarathāṇḍolā ḍolālola viśeṣakaiḥ |
śayyābharaṇavastrādyaiḥ kariharyādibhiḥ kvacith || 15.49 ||
samānairuttamajanaiḥ phalapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ |
svasvyeṣṭataruṇībhiśca jalakrīḍādibhistathā || 15.50 ||
avyāhṛteṣṭagatayaḥ pūjāyā satkathādibhiḥ |
punarāvṛttirahitā namanti nikhilā api || 15.51 ||
navakoṭyo hi devānāṃ padasthā devatāgaṇāḥ |
liṅgasūkṣmasthūladehayuktāḥsarve sabhāryakāḥ || 15.52 ||
sevārthaṃ śrīharerdurgābhāge samyagvasanti hi |
te kāmadhukkalpavṛkṣacintāmaṇisamutthitaiḥ || 15.53 ||
pūjyopa(yuktā)yogānantārthaiḥ jalena śrīkarārpitaiḥ |
tayā sampūjitaharinirmālyaiḥ punarārpitaiḥ || 15.54 ||
pavitritāṅgāparitaḥ sañcarantyapi modinaḥ |
sāyujyamapi sālokyaṃ sāmīpyaṃ sarvadā hareḥ || 15.55 ||
sārūpyamapi muktirhi caturthā kila bhaṇyate |
viṣṇurmuktānsvadehasthānsvasvānandaikabhojinaḥ || 15.56 ||
karoti taddhi sāyujyaṃ tatsurāṇāṃ ca nīcagaṃ |
sāmīpyādi trayaṃ karmadevānāṃ yujyate kvacith || 15.57 ||
tadbhinnadevadāsānāṃ sārūpyādi dvayaṃ mataṃ |
ekalokanivāsākhyaṃ sālokyamitarasya tu || 15.58 ||
śuddhasātvikadeheṣu caturbhujasamanvitāḥ |
darāripītāmbariṇo yadi sārūpyamīritaṃ || 15.59 ||
jayaśca vijayaścaiva balaśca prabalastathā |
caṇḍaḥ pracaṇḍaśca tathā nandaścāpi sunandakaḥ || 15.60 ||
kumudaḥ kumudākṣaśca tathā bhadrasubhadrakau |
sudhāmadāmanāmānau viṣvaksenavaśe sthitau || 15.61 ||
rūpaiścaturbhisarvepi caturdikṣvapi saptasu |
antaḥ prākāragadvāramārabhyadvārapālakāḥ || 15.62 ||
nandinī haripādotthā durgābhāgaśubhodakā |
parighā sa tu vaikuṇṭhaścaturasraḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 15.63 ||
bāhyaṇḍavyāpino viṣṇorloke vaikuṇṭhanāmakaḥ |
koṭyucchritānantamūrdhnaḥ mukhe mūrdhni sucityate || 15.64 ||
garbhodakāntardehasthakharparāspṛṣṭapādukaḥ |
svaratasyānantāsanākhyaḥ kaṭiḥ pītāmbarātmakaḥ || 15.65 ||
ghanodakaṃ kaṭitaṭī merustannābhimadhyage |
yojanānāṃ pañcaviṃśatkoṭigaṃ meruparvatāth || 15.66 ||
ajāṇḍakharparaṃ dikṣuvidikṣūrdhvamadhaḥ smṛtaṃ |
mahyādibhirāvaraṇyairdaśabhistu daśādikaiḥ || 15.67 ||
dattaṃ cāvaraṇānyetyanyūrdhvaśiṣṭāni sarvaśaḥ |
adhaḥ pādāni tadviṣṇoḥ sthalānyāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ || 15.68 ||
śilāvatpratimā viṣṇorajāderabhimānataḥ |
aṇḍaṃlokāśca devānāṃ kramāddhehāstu māninaḥ || 15.69 ||
evaṃ vidhājāṇḍakoṭyanantātmakarūpakaiḥ |
sāvakāśaṃ sañcaradbhiḥ vilāsadromakūpataḥ || 15.70 ||
harerevaṃ jagattasya vaśejñātvā viśeṣataḥ |
virāḍrūpasya ca hareḥ satyalokaḥ śirasyalaṃ || 15.71 ||
lokālokastasya hareḥ kaṭisūtre vicintyate |
pītāmbaraḥ svarṇabhūmiḥ śuddhodābdhiḥ kaṭistale || 15.72 ||
kaṭipradhānasthigaṇe cintayenmānasottaraṃ |
mūlādhāre jāmbavākhyaṃ kesareṣu ilāvṛtaṃ || 15.73 ||
jaṭharādyāstu tadrekhā merustannābhimadhyagaḥ |
merūparisthalokeśa bhavanāni ca sarvaśaḥ || 15.74 ||
bījakośeṣu sañcintya mucyate puruṣarṣabhaḥ |
bhuvarlokaṃ tasya nābhau svarlokaṃ hṛdi cintayeth || 15.75 ||
maharlokamuraḥsthāne janalokaṃ gale hareḥ |
tapolokaṃ mukhe dhyātvā satyalokaṃ tu mastake || 15.76 ||
ṣaḍaṣṭadvādaśādvyaṣṭadvisahasradalānyapi |
nābhyādikāni padmāni bhuvarādīni vai hareḥ || 15.77 ||
pātālaṃ tasya pāde tu prapadeṣu rasātalaṃ |
mahātalantu jaghane jaṅghayośca talātalaṃ || 15.78 ||
sutalaṃ jānudeśe tu ūrvo(pi tu)stu vitalātale |
virāḍrūpasya cāṅgeṣu lokāścintyā sucetanaiḥ || 15.79 ||
padmanābhasya nābhyutthaṃ prākṛtaṃ padmamūrjitaṃ |
saṃsṛṣṭatatvasañjātaṃ bhūtatvādikasaṅgataṃ || 15.80 ||
sarvalokātmakaṃ tasya śikhāgre satyanāmake |
ādyaḥ brahmāṃśato jāto viṣṇunaiva caturmukhaḥ || 15.81 ||
sṛṣṭaḥ svayaṃ draṣṭumicchancaturdikṣuvidhirhariṃ |
mukhāni lebhe catvāri nāpaśyaddhikṣu vai svayaṃ || 15.82 ||
tatastannālamadhyaṃ tacchidrādantargataṃ hariṃ |
paśyāmīti tapaścakre na tatrāvidadīśvaraṃ || 15.83 ||
satyabhāgadhosaṃspṛṣṭa saṃspṛṣṭāmbusthayā śriyā |
vāyurūpasthayā dhātā bhītā viṣṇorvibhīrabhūth || 15.84 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ prathamaparicchede pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ sampūrṇaḥ
<2>
<2.1>
dvitīyaparicchedaḥ
atha prathamo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
kañjasthitaḥ svayaṃ brahmā nāpaśyatsarvagaṃ hariṃ |
yatnavānapi tatpūrvamanasītthamacintayath || 1.1 ||
bhāvarūpājñānamāninyapi durgājagadguroḥ |
nityānugrahapātratvātputra(ucca)tvācca ramā mama || 1.2 ||
svopāsanāratatvācca nājñānaṃ bhāvarūpakaṃ |
icchatisma saliṅgasyānādyavidyāyutasya ca || 1.3 ||
svakīyasyendireṣanme hyabhiprāyaṃ śivādayaḥ |
aviditvānyathābrūyuḥ durgājñānapradaiva sā || 1.4 ||
na samarthājñānadāne vidherityasurāśrayāḥ |
ityajñāvākyairmā(turme'yaśasaḥ kāraṇaṃ vacaḥ || 1.5 ||
mehyayaśaḥ kāraṇaṃ na ca
tatrāhaṃ saṃmato mātuḥ kīrtyarthaṃ prārthayāmi tāṃ |
durgāmiti tathā cakre tenādātsasya saṃśayaṃ || 1.6 ||
datvājñānaṃ kṣaṇārdhantu svabharturbhaktimātanot |
bhayaśokau ca tenāsāvajñobhīto babhūva hā || 1.7 ||
tadajñanaṃ bhāvarūpaṃ caturvāraṃ harerbhayaṃ |
śoko dvivāraṃ ramayā prārthito jagadīśvaraḥ || 1.8 ||
dadāvalocanābhāva daśāyāṃ niyamaḥ kṛtaḥ |
vṛtyajñānasya bahuśaḥ sambhavādbrahmaṇopi tu || 1.9 ||
saṃkhyāviśeṣasyoktatvātbhāvājñānaṃ vadanti tat |
tatastvitthaṃ saṃśayobhūttrivāraṃ padmajanmanaḥ || 1.10 ||
svataḥ padmamidaṃ jātaṃ kiṃpuṃsānyena kenacit |
iti sañcintya puṃsyaiva jātaṃ naiva jaḍasya tath || 1.11 ||
svataḥ pravṛttirghaṭate jñānecchāderabhāvataḥ |
ahaṃ padmena vā jātaḥ matsṛṣṭiḥ puruṣeṇa vā || 1.12 ||
iti saṃścitya matsṛṣṭiḥ puruṣeṇai no vā |
iti niścitya sa pumānsvatantro vāsvatantrakaḥ || 1.13 ||
iti sañcintyāsvatantraḥ kathaṃ māṃ sraṣṭumarhati |
tasmātsvatantrājjātohaṃ taṃ pasyāṃ punarutthitaḥ (paśyeyaṃ) || 1.14 ||
iti nālaṃ praviśyāntaḥ nāpaśyatpunarutthitaḥ |
kañjastha āsa tajjñānaṃ viparītamajasya hi || 1.15 ||
bahirnadṛṣṭaḥ sa kathamantardṛṣṭi pa(ka)thaṃ vrajet |
tataḥ pracaṇḍavātotthādbhūmirmajjalakampitāth || 1.16 ||
padmātpatanniva mahābhayagrastho babhūva ha |
tadā kṣaṇārdhaṃ śokobhūdbrahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ || 1.17 ||
sṛṣṭvā kālaṃ mahākālaṃ māninaṃ vidhimātanot |
kālāvāsāne tvāsanne taṅgraniṣyati cādadath || 1.18 ||
āsyaṃ nṛsihmastu tadā bhayaśokānvito bhavat |
atraiva tatkāraṇena bhāvājñānayuto bhaveth || 1.19 ||
nānyatra duḥkhājñānādyaṃ ya(ma)duktaṃ tanmṛṣaiva hi |
tatastapatapa iti viṣṇoreva vaco śṛṇoth || 1.20 ||
tato dhyānaṃ jagaddhātuḥ kṛtvā paśyatsasarvagaṃ |
brahmā dadarśa vaikuṇṭhaṃ harerlokaṃ mahādbhutaṃ || 1.21 ||
pūrvoktāśeṣasumahāviśeṣārthaguṇonnataṃ |
dṛṣṭvā tuṣṭāva natveśaṃ kṛtārthobhūtsakañjajaḥ || 1.22 ||
sarvādhipatyaṃ tasyādātsarvajñatvaṃ vikuṇṭhapaḥ |
tadvaśobhūtsvatantropi bhagavānbhaktavatsalaḥ || 1.23 ||
dvisaptamanubhogārhakālaṃ dhāturaho vyadhāt |
yathā tathā rātrikālaṃ vyabhajadbhagavānvibhuḥ || 1.24 ||
tatrādyarātribhāge tu sāṅgaṃbrahmāṇḍamātanot |
tatrāṇḍanāmako viṣṇuḥ praviśyāntaḥ samedhayath || 1.25 ||
rātridvitīyabhāge tu padmanābhasya nābhitaḥ |
saṃsṛṣṭasyānyabhāgena lokapadmaṃ vyadhāddhariḥ || 1.26 ||
padmāgragaṃ ca brahmāṇaṃ tadajñānabhayādikaṃ |
tacchāntiṃ tasya tadbhaktyudrekaṃ tasya tapasthitiṃ || 1.27 ||
tasminpūrṇānugrahaṃ ca kṛtavānharirūrjitaṃ |
rātri tṛtīyabhāge tu vaikuṇṭhamatisundaraṃ || 1.28 ||
sasarja tallokapatirajasyādarśayaddhariḥ |
sa dṛṣṭvā taṃ vidadhyau muktāmukteśvaraṃ hariṃ || 1.29 ||
caturthāṃśe niśāyāṃśca virāṭsṛṣṭiṃ dadarśa ha |
dṛṣṭvā dvisaptalokeṣu virāṭdehasthitānsurān || 1.30 ||
sūkṣmadehasthānpūrvamanirūddhātsasūkṣmakān |
tathājastatra tatraiva sthūladehayutānvyadhāth || 1.31 ||
aṇḍanirmāṇakāle tu hyasaṃsṛṣṭāni sarvaśaḥ |
tatvānyādāya saṃyojya tatridhāvyabhajaddhariḥ || 1.32 ||
aṇḍaṃ tadāditaścakre dvītīyenābjamātanot |
tṛtīyaṃ tu tridhābhitvā punarekaikaśāstridhā || 1.33 ||
devopadevamartyānāṃ sthūlānyādaistribhirvyadhāt |
dvitīyāṃśaistribhisthūlānyāsurāṇāṃ śrī harīcchayā || 1.34 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ dvatīyaparicchede prathamo'dhyāyaḥ
<2.2>
śrīhaṃsaḥ
cakresthūlaśarīrāṇi yaddheśesthāpyayānvirāṭ |
teṣāṃ tadupayuktārthānjaḍāni vividhāni ca || 2.1 ||
sarvasthūlaśarīrāṇi upādānānyajaḥ svayaṃ |
virāṭdattāni saṅgṛhya kramātkālānusārataḥ || 2.2 ||
tattadguṇakriyājātirūpalakṣaṇasampadaḥ |
vyavahārāśrayācāraveṣabhāṣāsvarūpakān || 2.3 ||
dānamānā(nnapānārha)śanāyāśanārhapūrvānantārthasañcayān |
caturlakṣādhīkāśītilakṣajīvaguṇocitān || 2.4 ||
virāḍuktānvirāḍḍantānstadbhāvajñastathākarot |
yathottaratra sthūlopi na dṛṣṭastena tacchrutaṃ || 2.5 ||
tatpūrvaṃ padmanābhottha trivikramapadāmbujāt |
dakṣiṇādyudhṛtā gaṅgā tasyāṅguṣṭhanakhāgrataḥ || 2.6 ||
viśīrṇājāṇḍamadhyordhvarandhrātkṣmāvaruṇordhvagaṃ |
ambhaḥ prāgagnitatvasthā(sthaṃ) prākṛtāṅghrabhirakṣitaṃ || 2.7 ||
viṣṇoḥ pādātsamudbhūtaṃ tadvāryāṇḍāntarāviśat |
(e)tatmaṇḍulugaṃ kṛtvā brahmā viṣṇvaṅghrimajjanaṃ || 2.8 ||
cakāra tena tadvāri gaṅgākhyā susaradvarā |
ato viṣṇvaṅghrijāṃ brahmakamaṇḍalusamudbhavaṃ || 2.9 ||
vadanti sā suṣumnā hi brahmāṇḍavyāpino hareḥ |
niśāyāḥ pañcamāṃśe tu brahmeśādyākhilāmarān || 2.10 ||
sṛṣṭvā vaikuṇṭhalokasthadurgābhāgagatānvyadhāt |
sevārthaṃ tadgatānantarūpasya śrīhareḥ śriyaḥ || 2.11 ||
teṣāmucitavijñānaṃ hareḥ sevāṃ upādiśat |
ṣaṣṭamāṃśe niśāyāśca punaḥ sarvānsurottamān || 2.12 ||
ajo sṛjatsvasevārthaṃ tāṃśca(te ca) pūrvoktarītitaḥ |
saptaprākāra(ke)gānsaptalokeśaṃ toṣayatyalaṃ || 2.13 ||
tāvatsatyādhasthalaspṛgvāryaṇḍaparipūritaṃ |
mahogravāyunā śuṣyanmaharlokādadhoviśath || 2.14 ||
saptamāṃśe niśīthinyāḥ punardevānajosṛjat |
teṣu pradhānānsarvānmanaso dehato'sṛjath || 2.15 ||
teṣu pradhānānśeṣādīṃ tapolokevyadhādajaḥ |
tatra lokapatiḥ sākṣātsaṅkarṣaṇamanāmayaṃ || 2.16 ||
ārādhayato'nya surānmanasā dehato sṛjat |
indrakāmau janoloke tṛtīyotāvajo vyadhāth || 2.17 ||
pradyumnantāvabhajatāṃ sṛṣṭvā tau svāvarāmarān |
aniruddhādikāndhātā maharlokagatānvyadhāth || 2.18 ||
aniruddhaṃ bhajantastevṛsṛjansvāvarāmarān |
aṣṭamāṃśetriyāmāyā sanakādīnpitāmahaḥ || 2.19 ||
sṛṣṭvā sṛṣṭvaitanniyuktāstadājñānācarandrutaṃ |
tataḥ prakupitāddhātuḥ rudro ekādaśātmakaḥ || 2.20 ||
jātobhūtsapatnī(kṣī)kaḥ sasṛṣṭyarthamajoditaḥ |
tapastaptvāthakālena pīte svargāntavāriṇī(ṇā) || 2.21 ||
ajenāvāpyasasvargabhūtānpretāṃśca rākṣasān |
nandinaṃ mātṛkāḥsapta piśācānlokabhīkarān || 2.22 ||
bhaktānsurāṃśānapi durbhāvayuktāndigambarān |
krūrasṛṣṭiṃ tato jñātvā brahmā taṃ pratyaṣedhata || 2.23 ||
tatojaḥ svākaṃ deśena nāradaṃ tvasṛjadṛṣīṃ |
dakṣaṃ dakṣāṅguṣṭatojastanvā bhṛgumacīklpath || 2.24 ||
marīcīṃ manasā netreṇātrimāṅgirasaṃ mukhāt |
karṇarandhrātpulastyaṃ ca pulahaṃ nābhideśataḥ || 2.25 ||
karātkratuṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ ca prāṇātkāmāccagādhijaṃ |
chāyayā kardamaṃ budhyāruciṃ vyajanayadvibhuḥ || 2.26 ||
ṛgādi devānpadmotthaḥ pūrvādyāsyaiḥ kramādvyadhāt |
bhārataṃ pañcarātrāṇi purāṇāni smṛtistathā || 2.27 ||
pūrvādibhirvyadhādāsyaistathā varṇāśramādikān |
bhṛguṃ manuṃ budhaśanī caturvarṇapravartakān || 2.28 ||
cakāra(rā) kālāvayavāndiśastadabhimāninaḥ |
yajñānhṛdāpuṇyadharmo pṛṣṭenādharmavistṛtiṃ || 2.29 ||
tathā pañcavi(dhā)jñānaṃ durgāṃ tadabhimāninīṃ |
ahorātre pakṣayośca lajjāṃ mūrdhoṣṭatosṛjath || 2.30 ||
adharoṣṭāttato lobhaṃ nāḍibhyo vyasṛjannadīḥ |
asthibhyaḥ parvatānkukṣeḥ sarvasāgaramāninaḥ || 2.31 ||
meghānkeśairlomajātairvṛkṣā(ścaiva latādikān)ngulmānlatānvitān |
caturlakṣādhikāśītilakṣayoniṣu tātvikān || 2.32 ||
daśasaṃkhyāmitāndhātā vyasṛjaccānya cetanān |
karmadevādikāndevāḥ kramājjātāstu kālataḥ || 2.33 ||
śataṃ gandharvāpsarasāṃ triṃśaccāraṇarakṣasāṃ |
śataṃ siddhāstathānyāsu saptatiḥsarvajātiṣu || 2.34 ||
ājānajāḥ kuberotthāstebhya eva niraṃśakāḥ |
devadāsā niraṃśāśca padmakalpādanantaraṃ || 2.35 ||
svayambhuventare bhūmau jātāḥ sarvatra saṅgatāḥ |
kṣaṇadānavame bhāge mahīdāsahayānanau || 2.36 ||
sanatkumāramachākhyāvādiśetāṃ manuṃ vidheḥ |
avatīryākhilānvedānsāraṃ caiva(veda sāraṃ) purāṇakaṃ || 2.37 ||
pañcarātraṃ mahāśāstraṃ śrīmadbhāgavataṃ śubhaṃ |
daśame tu niśābhāge padmanābhasya karṇayoḥ || 2.38 ||
kalpitāttāmasādbhūri kīkasādāsatūripū |
tadā kusumanābhasya niśāṃśadaśame śubhe || 2.39 ||
kalpitāttamasādbhūri kīkasādāsatūripū |
madhuśca kaiṭabhaścaiva rākṣasau durmadoddhatau || 2.40 ||
kadāmerurūrdhvadeśasthaṃ pītvā tāvajjalaṃ sthitaṃ |
rūpāntareṇābjabhavaṃ prāpya yuddhāya rākṣasau || 2.41 ||
āhvayāmāsa tu brahmā tāvāhāhaṃ hi durbalaḥ |
ityuktvā sa hariṃ dadhyau hayagrīvāhvayaṃ hariḥ || 2.42 ||
vyakto bhūdbrahmahṛdāsatābhyāṃ ciramayodhanaṃ |
kṛtvā hatvācchādyakuntaṃ medhasā medinīṃ vyadhāth || 2.43 ||
kṣaṇadaikādaśāṃśe tu brahmā merusthitaḥ svayaṃ |
piśācāndānavāndaityānniraṃśānsāṃśarūpakān || 2.44 ||
etānviṣṇudviṣo yogyānrākṣasāṃśca kaliṃstathā |
vyasṛjattānadholokavāsinaḥ kālatokaroth || 2.45 ||
ajeyatvamavadhyatvavaraṃ teṣāmadādvidhiḥ |
kālena bhārate janma pratimanvantareṣvapi || 2.46 ||
yathāyogyaṃ janmalabdhvā tapastaptvā śivādvaraṃ |
prāpya viprāryagurvādigosuradveṣiṇaḥ sadā || 2.47 ||
bhaviṣyatheti teṣāṃ tu varaṃ prādādathābjajaḥ |
paraśuklatrayaṃ hitvā chinnabhāgavateṣvalaṃ || 2.48 ||
praviśya buddhivyāmohaṃ kalpayitvā kvacitkvacit |
yogyatātikramodbhūtapuṇyaṃ nāśayatheti ca || 2.49 ||
śarīratatvopādānasthitasarvottamānsurān |
sarver(va)tattvo(tvā)pacayagā bhavanto bhīṣayantyatha || 2.50 ||
pāpaduḥkhādi sambandho bhavatāṃ ca bhavediti |
viṣṇuvismṛtiyuktānāṃ śarīreṣu sātāmapi || 2.51 ||
asatāṃ miśrabuddhīnāṃ abhimānitvamastuvaḥ |
mithyābhimānino daityāḥ devāstatvābhimāninaḥ || 2.52 ||
sarvatatveṣvatha kalirjīvamānī durāgrahāt |
dehastha devatāḥ pūr(ṇar)vapuṇya(bhujaḥ)saukhyabhujaḥ kramāth || 2.53 ||
pāpaduḥkhabhujaścānye dehamānyubhayātmakāḥ |
antarbahisthajīvānāṃ kiñciduḥkhaṃ bhavatyapi || 2.54 ||
daityāveśānna vāyorhi daityāveśaḥ kadācana |
jīvamānyuttamo brahmā dehastho madhyamaḥ svayaṃ || 2.55 ||
adhamastu kalirjīvamānyeva hi surāsurāḥ |
iti tatvārthavitteṣāṃ brahmādāducitaṃ varaṃ || 2.56 ||
śuddhodasāgarabahirmaharloke tadūrdhvataḥ |
garbhodake tu(ca) daityānāṃ āveśo nāsti sarvathā || 2.57 ||
etadanyatra daityānāṃ sthityai prādādvaraṃ vibhuḥ(dhiḥ) |
dvādaśāṃśe tu(sa)tāmasyāḥ brahmāṇḍasthāmbunirgame || 2.58 ||
cintyatojasya vai ghrāṇātghroṇī rūpī babhūva ha |
ghroṇī rūpī varāhātmā haristvavatatāra ca || 2.59 ||
sa tu śvetavarāhākhyaḥ brahmaṇā saṃ(yu)stuto'sṛjat |
yajñābhimānino yajñānmantratantrārtvigoṣadhīḥ || 2.60 ||
tiladarbhāśvapaśvājyahavīṃṣisamidhaḥ kramāt |
satrāṇi somasadanaṃ rā(ra)mbhabhaiśyādikānvyadhāth || 2.61 ||
yupāgnīnspṛṣadājyaṃ ca phalānyapyasṛjatkiṭiḥ |
tadā hayagrīvanāmā rākṣaso ghoradarśanaḥ || 2.62 ||
ajasya mukha(khya)tovyaktaṃ guruṃ vedābhimāninaṃ |
nigṛhyāgātradā vedāḥ apapāṭhatirohitāḥ || 2.63 ||
tadā prārthito maccharūpī jalamupāviśat |
hatvā hayaśiraskantaṃ vedānuddharadīśvaraḥ || 2.64 ||
tadā dhātrārthitaścādisūkaroṇḍagataṃ jalaṃ |
aśāmayadbhuvamadholokāṃśca vimalānvyadhāth || 2.65 ||
sūryādayo grahāḥ sarve tadādyāḥ saprakāśakāḥ |
tadādi kalpaṃ taṃ prāhuḥ vārāhamiti sajjanāḥ || 2.66 ||
rātrau trayodaśāṃśe tu purīṃ barhiṣmatīṃ vyadhāt |
varāhātmāhyādimanorāvāsārthamaśeṣavith || 2.67 ||
tadaivāṣṭasvayaṃvyaktāḥ purāṇi vividhāni ca |
nadyaśca devakhā(ghā)tāśca vanānyupavanāni ca || 2.68 ||
tadā sarvopayuktāni sarvatra vyasṛjadvidhiḥ |
caturdaśāṃśe rātrestu dikpālānsarvalokagān || 2.69 ||
sarvatrājñāpayaddhikṣu vidikṣu ca yathākramaṃ |
tatprāgeva samutpannā brahmaṇā lokapālakāḥ || 2.70 ||
virāḍdehagatasturyo hyaniruddhā(kṣigo vibhuḥ)bhidho hariḥ |
atalādiṣvayogyānāṃ sūkṣmāṇya(pya)jamadarśayath || 2.71 ||
(saṃ)sa tānpadmajasambhūtaḥ(to) merumadhyasthakañjajaḥ |
ūrdhvamudhṛtya ca sthūladehayuktāṃścatānpunaḥ || 2.72 ||
tattallokeṣu saṃsthāpya mukhyaprāṇavaśānvyadhāt |
tadā brahmavarodbhūto hiraṇyākṣo hi rākṣasaḥ || 2.73 ||
pañcabhūtāvarāṃ bhūmiṃ gṛhītvātha rasātalaṃ |
viveśa tanmocanāya varāhātmāṇḍajārthitaḥ || 2.74 ||
rasātalaṃ praviśyaiva mocayitvā bhuvaṃ tvaran |
taṃ gadāpāṇinā(mā) kruddhaṃ prāpayitvaiva bhārata || 2.75 ||
yudhvā svaṃ daṃṣṭrāgrahataṃ kṛtvā taṃ narake vyadhāt |
tato merugato dhātā savyātsvāyaṃbhuvaṃ vyadhāth || 2.76 ||
dehāpasavyatastasya śatarūpāṃ satīṃ vyadhāt |
bhugudakṣamarīcyādīntapase(sa)manumādimaṃ || 2.77 ||
āhūyājaḥ prāhasṛṣṭiṃ bṛṃhaṇāya satāṃ gatiḥ |
śatarūpā patiḥ prāha tapastaptvā sudāruṇaṃ || 2.78 ||
varāhoddhāraṇāptaśca kiyatkālaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ |
varāddhareśca strīpuṃso yogātsyaiṣvividhīritāṃ || 2.79 ||
stripuṃsayogādbhāvyaṃ ca sṛṣṭimetāṃ vidhīritāṃ |
sṛtvā tasyāmajanayatputrau dvau lokaviśrutau || 2.80 ||
priyavratottānapādau tisraḥ kanyāśca sattamaḥ |
ākūtirdevahūtiśca prasūtiriti viśrutāḥ || 2.81 ||
tatrādyāyāmṛcerjajñe yajñanāmā ramāpatiḥ |
tenaiva saha sambhūtā dakṣiṇā nāmikā ramā || 2.82 ||
ajāvayonijau dvāvaprākṛtadehayutau parau |
putrikāputradharmeṇa yajñamātmasutaṃ manuḥ || 2.83 ||
cakāra dakṣiṇā yajñe dakṣiṇāmāninī ca sā |
tuṣitā nāma tatputrāḥ sambabhūvuḥ surottamāḥ || 2.84 ||
tattvābhimāninaḥ(tairnīcātātvikāḥ) sarve babhūvuḥ navakoṭayaḥ |
ākūterdevahūteśca prasūtiranujātuyā || 2.85 ||
dakṣasyā sasatī tasmātasyāṃ ṣoḍaśakanyakāḥ |
abhavannarkaputrasya dharmasyaiva trayodaśa || 2.86 ||
putryastvekāgnaye dattā tasyāṃ tretā(tatrā)gnayobhavan |
āhvanīyogarhapatyo dakṣiṇāgniriti trayaḥ || 2.87 ||
teṣāmekā dakṣasutā bhāryā(dattā) tasyāṃ tribhiḥ kramāt |
pṛthakpṛthakpañcadaśaputrā agnisvarūpakāḥ || 2.88 ||
āsannādyā yajñabhujo madhyamā havyabhojinaḥ |
antyāḥ kavyabhujaḥ pūrvai pañcāśatsaṃmitāgnayaḥ || 2.89 ||
ekanyūnā dakṣasutā jyeṣṭhā dattā śivasya tu |
dharmānvarūtvatī putrānpañcāśanmaruto'sṛjath || 2.90 ||
viśvedaśāsanviśvāyāṃ vasavauṣṭau vasūdbhavāḥ |
rudrā asaṃsmarudrāṇyāṃ bhūtāyāṃ bhūtanā(ya)makāḥ || 2.91 ||
upadevāśca sādhyāyāṃ anyāsvapi ca dharmataḥ |
upadevāśca devāśca babhūvurhi bahuprajāḥ || 2.92 ||
dharmasya te padasthāśca sarve(sarga)vāsocitā matāḥ |
tadutthitāśca bhāvīnāḥ prāyaśo hyaparokṣiṇaḥ || 2.93 ||
prāguktāstātvikāścaikarūpeṇa bhuvi bhārate |
uṣitvā bhāvisatkarmajñānabhaktyādi kāṅkṣiṇaḥ || 2.94 ||
śubhāśubhaṃ ca prārabdhaṃ bhoktuṃ yogyāstato bhavat |
te ca dvīpādri varṣeṣu bhārate(tā)nyeṣu sarvaśaḥ || 2.95 ||
suprārabdhyaika bhogārhāḥ dharmādharmotthasambhavāḥ |
āsandevāśca pūrvoktā hyaparokṣya naparokṣiṇaḥ || 2.96 ||
satsādhanaṃ bhāratesminkartumarhāḥ surottamaiḥ |
(tya)vyaktāparokṣibhirjātāḥ sadaivāsanharīcchayā || 2.97 ||
priyavratotthānapādau tayo(po) niṣṭhau babhūvatuḥ |
niśā caturdaśāṃśe tu sṛṣṭireṣā sumerugāḥ || 2.98 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ dvitīyaparicchede dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ
<2.3>
hariḥ oṃ
tadā merau tu sujyeṣṭhaḥ kāmadhukkalpavṛkṣakau |
cintāmaṇiṃ rūpabhedaiḥ svargopasvargagānvyadhāth || 3.1 ||
tadāvidhiḥ sarvajīvabimbarūpāṇyacintayat |
svayaṃ tathā tathābhūtvā sṛjattā(vacha)dṛkśarīriṇaḥ || 3.2 ||
svargasthānāṃ padasthānāṃ sarvamanvantareṣvapi |
bhūmau haryāvatāre tu tadājñāmakarodvidhiḥ || 3.3 ||
duṣprārabdhasya bhogāyāgāmi satsādhanāya ca |
surāṇāṃ harisevārthaṃ avatārāṇyanukramāth || 3.4 ||
punastattanmūlarūpairekībhāvaṃ sa sarvadā |
bhuvaṃ spṛśantviti tathā te ca kurvantyajājñayā || 3.5 ||
edhamānadviḍeṣāṃ hi sarvadā bhāratāsthitiṃ |
nājñāpayadharistena tathā cakre satāṃ(tān) vidhiḥ || 3.6 ||
tato dhātātmadehasthatatvānyuccāvacāni ca |
saṃsṛṣṭāni tadīśāṃśca merusthaḥ savyacintayath || 3.7 ||
dehamadhyetvadhonābhermūlādhāraṃ sa puṣkaraṃ |
caturdalaṃ(śaṃ) svarṇanibhaṃ māyādyādaladevatāḥ || 3.8 ||
aniruddhamudānaṃ ca suṣumnā (di)hi trikāśrayaṃ |
tanmadhyasambhavā nāḍī suṣumnā sūkṣmarūpiṇī || 3.9 ||
ekā śākhādirahitā svarṇavarṇā parāśrayā |
bhūmyā svāyambhavenāpi rakṣitebje samutthitā || 3.10 ||
cchidrānvitā ca sā saptaprāṇādi marudāśrayā |
madhye jvalantī tatpārśvajātayoḥ piṅgalela(ḍa)yoḥ || 3.11 ||
mūrdhāṃ tā nābhikañjādi ṣaṭpadmānāṃ samāśrayā |
ṣaṭdalaṃ nābhikamalaṃ nīlādyāstaddalādhipāḥ || 3.12 ||
iṣannīlopetabhāge gorikāraktimānvite |
sthitaṃ pradyumnanāmānaṃ nāgākhyaprāṇasevitaṃ || 3.r13 ||
marutaḥ pravahādīṃśca saptatadgānvyacintayet |
te dve ūrdhvamukhe hṛdgakañjamāharadhomukhaṃ || 3.14 ||
nābhe dvyaṣṭāṅgulordhvastha suṣumnāṣṭadigutthitaiḥ |
dalairadhomukhairudyadbhānuvarṇaisthāṣṭabhiḥ || 3.15 ||
daśāṅgulimitāntar(ga)dgar(bhā)bhāvakośāvahaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
parasparaṃ dṛḍhāśliṣṭasaṃyuktāgrāṅgulaṃ bhaveth || 3.16 ||
hṛtkajaṃ tatragīrdevī bhāratyā sahamāninī |
prāṇāḥ kūrmātmakastatra vasavo daladevatāḥ || 3.17 ||
sthāneśo hi tadantastho daśāṅgulamito hariḥ |
tadīya hṛdayākāśamaṇṭapaṃ śrīsvarūpakaṃ || 3.18 ||
durgābhūśrībhāgabhedairbhāgatrayasamanvitā |
durgākandātmikā caiva bhūrmadhye nālarūpiṇī || 3.19 ||
tadūrdhvage tu śrībhāge ramākañjasvarūpiṇī |
vedikā tu tribhāgasthā sārdhāṅguli dṛḍhā matā || 3.20 ||
tanmaṇṭapaṃ maṇimayaṃ caturdvāraṃ śubhāspadaṃ |
sugandhairmaṇidīpaiśca rambhāstambhairmanoramaṃ || 3.21 ||
toraṇaiḥ pūrṇakumbhaiśca patākachatramaṇḍitaṃ |
maṇistambhaimauktahāraiḥ nānā śvetāṅkurairapi || 3.22 ||
śrībhāgaṃ kañjasaṃmānaḥ mūleśākhyo harisvayaṃ |
kañjoparīndusūryāgnimaṇḍalānyuparisphuṭaṃ || 3.23 ||
ramyaiva divyaśayyābhūtkamanīyopabarhaṇā |
tasminsvarūpaśeṣābha harirūrdhvaphaṇonnataḥ || 3.24 ||
tasyopari ramācitrāsanarūpā tadūrdhvagā |
prājñātmā sa tu jīveśo jīvoyadvaśagaḥ sadā || 3.25 ||
cakraśaṅkhagadāpadmahastaḥ sa tu caturbhujaḥ |
kirīṭakuṇḍalalasannūpurāṅgadabhūṣaṇaḥ || 3.26 ||
hāraśrīvatsakaṇṭhastha kaustubhādairalaṅkṛtaḥ |
ratnāṅgulīyakaṭakakaṭisūtrādi satkṛtaḥ || 3.27 ||
pitāmbaroti sarvāṅgasaundaryasubhagocitaḥ |
sa viśvatomukhopyekamukhavadbhāti nīladṛk || 3.28 ||
sthāneśa mūleśayuktaḥ samupāsyo niraṃśakaiḥ |
maṇṭapādhaḥ pradeśasthaśaktirādhārarūpiṇī || 3.29 ||
kūrmaśeṣaśca bhūḥ kṣauravārdhipādvīparūpiṇī |
śvetāṃ ramāṃ saṃvicintya niraṃśairityacintayath || 3.30 ||
urustālvorbhruvormadhye śirasyūrdhvamukhāni ca |
kañjāni śvetavarṇāni dviṣaḍvyaṣṭadalaistathā || 3.31 ||
dvābhyāṃ sahasradalakairyuktāni daladevatāḥ |
ādityāḥ prathame padme indrādyāstatradevatāḥ || 3.32 ||
devatāstu trṛtīyebje bhāratī ca sarasvatī |
mūrdhnaiko bahurūpeṇa vāyuḥ sarvadalādhipaḥ || 3.33 ||
nārāyaṇo hariḥ kṛṣṇo vāsudevastadīśvaraḥ |
kṛkalo devadattākhyaḥ sūtranāmā marutpatiḥ || 3.34 ||
marutaḥ saptasaptāpi pañcasvapi hadāriṣu |
suṣumnā nālajātāni padmānyetāni sapta ca || 3.35 ||
guṇatrayātmikānāḍī suṣumnā vedapāragaiḥ |
syaikā śākhādi rahitā garbhechidrānvatāpi ca || 3.36 ||
sarasvatī tadantargā nadī vārisvarūpiṇī |
hairaṇmayī vaidhyutīti vajrikā bhāsvatīti sā || 3.37 ||
nāmabhiḥ saṃyutālokāḥ bhūrādyāḥ tatrasaṃsthitāḥ |
bhūrbhuvasvargalokeśā maharādīśvaraḥ(ścarāḥ) kramāth || 3.38 ||
bhūvighneśendrāniruddhakāmaśeṣābjajā matāḥ |
kaṭyūrudvitaye jānujaṅghagulpo bhayāṅghriṣu || 3.39 ||
atalādyāśritādevā dehe deveśa sevakāḥ |
mitrāgnyastrapavārīśamayamandayamāḥ kramāth || 3.40 ||
dehepyevaṃ virāḍdehadhāriṇaṃ tanmitovati |
suṣumnā mūladeśotthe gaṅgāyāmunavārdhare || 3.41 ||
piṅgalele raktanīlavarṇe savyāpasavyage |
garbhachidrānvite ṣaṭtriṃśatsahasraprabhedinī || 3.42 ||
pṛthakpṛthaksavyanāḍyo mahattatvātmikā matāḥ |
tridhā vibhaktāḥ pādāntaṃ dviṣaṭsāhasrasaṃmitāḥ || 3.43 ||
dakṣiṇe dakṣapādāntaṃ nāḍyastāvanmitāḥ kramāt |
piṅgalā ūrdhvadeheṣu vyāptāstāvanmitāḥ śubhāḥ || 3.44 ||
adhomadhyordhvagāstāstu saptāsya pathamāśritāḥ |
saptasantyeva vivarāḥ ghrāṇāsya śruti netragāḥ || 3.45 ||
saptāsyapathanāmāno golakāḥ saptakīrtitāḥ |
saptordhvanāḍyo hṛtkañjādhasthalādvāmapārśvagāḥ || 3.46 ||
punarhṛtkaṃ cordhvadeśātsavyamāvṛtya piṅgalāḥ |
saptāsya pathagāḥ tatra(sūryo) vahnyādyā vatsareśvarāḥ || 3.47 ||
uttarāyaṇamāseśāḥ dineśāḥ śuklapakṣapāḥ |
paraśuklatrayaṃ hyasminhyeta evābhimāninaḥ || 3.48 ||
sūryādi sarvadevānāṃ nāmavācyāstu piṅgalāḥ |
tasyāṃ brahmābhimānī gī ṛjavastvaparokṣiṇaḥ || 3.49 ||
prāṇāpānā vilāyāṃ ca piṅgalāyāṃ ca vartataḥ |
rūpaiḥ ṣaḍviṃśatsahasraistāsu tau bhāratīyutau || 3.50 ||
tathāniruddhapradyumnau dakṣiṇe puṃ svarūpataḥ |
strīsvarūpeṇa vāme tu tāvadrūpaiśca tāsvalaṃ || 3.51 ||
tādṛkpāṇasvarūpasthau brahmarudrādyupāsitau |
dakṣiṇāyanamāseśāḥ rātrīśāścaiva devatāḥ || 3.52 ||
kṛṣṇapakṣeśvarārudravipaśeṣāḥ sabhāryakāḥ |
ahaṅkārātmakelāyāṃ devāḥ sarvebhimāninaḥ || 3.53 ||
ānandavijñānamayau piṅgalelādhipau kramāt |
vyānātmā sarvasandhisthaḥ udānobrahmanāḍigaḥ || 3.54 ||
suṣumnā brahmanāḍīti samānaḥsarvadehagaḥ |
adho madhyordhvagāḥ saptasaptelānāḍikā kramāth || 3.55 ||
saptāśvamārgapathagāḥ ūrdhvelāḥ piṅgalā yathā |
hṛtkañjādhobhāgatastāḥ savyaṃ prāpya tadūrdhvataḥ || 3.56 ||
apasavyaṃ punaḥ prāpyasaptāśvapathagāḥ kramāt |
bhūmyā(dhūmā)di devatāśabdairilāvācyāsamastaśaḥ || 3.57 ||
ṛgvedasūktabhāgeṣu bṛhatīḥṛksahasrayuk |
sūtradvyaṣṭādaśaikonaiḥ pūrṇarukprabhaviṣyati || 3.58 ||
pādastu navavarṇātmā tādṛkpādacatuṣṭayaiḥ |
ruktatsaṃyuktākṣaretāvarṇādvāsaptatīritāḥ || 3.59 ||
dvāsaptatisahasrāṇi varṇāḥ sarvarugāśritāḥ |
dvāsaptatisahasreṣu piṅgalelāhvayāsu ca || 3.60 ||
nāḍīṣu tattadvargeṣu vācyarūpāṇyacintayet |
suṣumnāyāścaturdikṣu hṛtkañjādhaḥ samāśritaṃ || 3.61 ||
manobuddhirahaṅkāraścittañceti caturvidhaṃ |
tatrendrakāmau bhāryau tau tadviśeṣābhimāninau || 3.62 ||
śrotre digdevatāścandrastvakcāhaṃ prāṇanāmakaḥ |
netrayorbhāskaro jihvāmānī varuṇariritaḥ || 3.63 ||
daśrau ghrāṇāśritāvete tadviśeṣābhimāninaḥ |
vaktre(vāci) vahniḥ karedakṣo jayantaḥ pādayordhvayoḥ || 3.64 ||
mitraḥ svāyaṃbhuvau pāyūpasthayorabhimāninau |
ghrā(prā)ṇo marucchabdamānītvapānaḥ sparśagastataḥ || 3.65 ||
vyānorūpaḥ rasamānī hyudāno gandhasaṃsthitaḥ |
samāna ete mātrāsu viśeṣādabhimāninaḥ || 3.66 ||
bhūdevī māninī pṛthvīḥ abātmā varuṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
agnistejomayo vāyau pravahaḥ prabhurīśvaraḥ || 3.67 ||
ākāśamānī vighneśaḥ bhūteṣvete viśeṣapāḥ |
keśāli(sthi)tvaṅmāṃsanakharūpā bhūrdehagā matāḥ || 3.68 ||
vāriraktātmakaṃ deha jaṭharāgnistu taijasaṃ |
vāyuḥ śvāsātmakaḥ kāye hyavakāśo nabhastanau || 3.69 ||
pratyakṣasiddhasarveṣāṃ satvaṃ bā(bo)dhyaṃ na hi kvacit |
sudurgandhau ghrāṇavedyau dehe jihvendriyapriyaḥ || 3.70 ||
śubhāśubharaso rūpaṃ cakṣurvedyaṃ kalevare |
śītoṣṇādiḥ tvacāvedyaḥ sparśaḥ śabde(bdaḥ) śrutipriye(yaḥ) || 3.71 ||
mātrādhṛṣīkaviṣayāḥ satyābādhyāna hīritāḥ |
vacanādānagamanotsargabhogādisādhanaṃ || 3.72 ||
karmendriyapañcacārthakriyākāreti(riṇa) (bā)badhyate |
satyaṃ śṛṇotispṛśatipaśyatyatti ca jighrati || 3.73 ||
śretratvaṅnetrarasanāghrāṇastatvāni tānyataḥ |
arthakriyākarāṇyeva vibodhyānīhavedagaṃ || 3.74 ||
manaśca śrutihetutvātsaptaṃ(tyaṃ) ca śarīragāḥ |
ahaṅkārāpādakatvātpiṅgalā mamatāspadā || 3.75 ||
nāḍyo daurbalyasamaye bhāntisavyāpasavyayoḥ |
puṃsāṃstrīṇāmāyādivyañjakāḥ prabhavanti hi || 3.76 ||
ataḥ satyācca(śca) bahuṣu jñānājñānapravartakāḥ |
guṇāḥ satvādi dehasthāḥ satyāstadabhimāninaḥ || 3.77 ||
devāḥ satyāḥ kevalasya jaḍasyasthityayogataḥ |
dehānāṃ ca sadehānāṃ dehabandhamatikṛtāṃ || 3.78 ||
duḥkhāsvadatvāddehāptikāraṇaṃ satyameva hi |
karmāṇi dvividhaṃ vedeṣūktānukramasaccasath || 3.79 ||
sukhaduḥkhapradaṃ tena satyāvedāḥsamastaśaḥ |
deśe kāle kṛtaṅkarma phaladattvena tau vṛtau || 3.80 ||
tatkarmaphalabhoktāro jīvāḥ satyāḥ saliṅgakāḥ |
bhagnaliṅgā vimuktāśca śrutyuktāśca kṛtāḥ smṛtāḥ || 3.81 ||
ihānyatrālpa bahulajñānājñānādi yogibhiḥ |
sadasattāratamyaṃ ca sapta(tya)mete svatantrakāḥ || 3.82 ||
ciccetyarūpā eteṣāṃ dehayogavivarjitāḥ |
niyantā puruṣaśreṣṭhaḥ na ca etatprayujyate || 3.83 ||
tasmāllakṣmīpatirvyāptaḥ svatantraḥ śruticoditaḥ |
nirdeṣo nityasuguṇaḥ sadā nārāyaṇo hariḥ || 3.84 ||
evamādhyātmikaṃ satyam(ptam) adhibhūtaṃ bahisthitaṃ |
sarvāṅkurotpādakatvātbhūḥsatvā(tyā)khilakāraṇāth || 3.85 ||
abhasmaranādi yogyatvātpālakaddāhādi sādhanaṃ |
vahniśca śoṣako vāyuravakāśapradāniyath || 3.86 ||
tatkāraṇāni mātrāśca tadgatānīndriyāṇi ca |
satyā(ptā)nyevaṃ samastāni saṃsṛṣṭānyaṇḍagāni ca || 3.87 ||
etanmūlānyasaṃsṛṣṭānyapi satyāni sarvaśaḥ |
saṅgṛhyetadvinirmāyā yāti satyaḥ sahatyajaḥ || 3.88 ||
kecinniraṅgā nirdehā jñānādi guṇavarjitāḥ |
niraṃśo khaṇḍarūpaśca sarvādṛśyonirūpitāḥ || 3.89 ||
anāmarūpo phaladastvanupāsyo vicāritaḥ |
brahmaikantu jaḍaṃ sarve brahmaṇyāropitaṃ mṛṣā || 3.90 ||
śuktau rajatavadrajjau sarpavatpratibhāti ca |
ajñānaṃ kalpitaṃ taddhi śuktijñānāddhi bādhyate || 3.91 ||
vicārite sati hi sā śuktirnarajataṃ bhavet |
ato bodhyaṃ mṛṣābhūtaṃ mithyā tadrajataṃ yathā || 3.92 ||
vastusvarūpaṃ jñānena kalpitatvāccatattathā |
ajñānakalpitaṃ brahmaṇyetadviśvaṃ prakalpitaṃ || 3.93 ||
brahmaṇyāropakṛjjīvo brahmānyo naiva vartate |
niraṃśamapi tadbrahmatvajñānāvṛtamañjasā || 3.94 ||
anantarūpakaṃ bhuṅkte nānāyonisamudbhavaṃ |
tasyajñānamupādhyutthaṃ satyopādhirna vidyate || 3.95 ||
mithyā sājñānajatatvānnājñānaṃ satya(tva)mucyate |
tadājñānena jīvasthaṃ jīvasyābhāvataḥ svataḥ || 3.96 ||
nirviśeṣabrahmaṇi tu jñānājñānena vartataḥ |
ato na brahmaniṣṭaṃ tattajñānatadupādhijaṃ || 3.97 ||
evaṃ mithyābhūtakālakarmadeśāgamādibhiḥ |
bhramadbrahma ca kalpānte brahmamūla svarūpakaṃ || 3.98 ||
jñātvā tatraikyamāpanno na punarjāyate bhuvi |
muktasyāpi na cājñānaṃ nendriyāṇi na kvacith || 3.99 ||
nirviśeṣe parebrahmaṇyaikyametādṛśaṃ khalu |
tadajñānaṃ na sannāsatsadasanna ca tarhikaṃ || 3.100 ||
anirvācyaṃ tannivṛttirmuktirityabhidhīyate |
pūrvāpareṣu kāleṣu sṛṣṭinmetyapi te jaguḥ || 3.101 ||
etaddhi durmataṃ santo nāṅgīkurvantitātvikāḥ || 3.102 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ dvitīyaparicchede tṛtīyodhyāyaḥ
<2.4>
śrīhaṃsa uvāca
sāṅgajñānātmakatanumanantaguṇapūritaṃ |
sāṅgāsadbhīṣayantvīśaṃ vedyaiḥ śāstrairnirūpitaṃ || 4.1 ||
anantanāmarūpāḍhyaṃ sva sva yogyaphalapradaṃ |
vicāritamupāsyaṃ ca ramā brahmādibhiḥ sadā || 4.2 ||
svabhinnasvāvarānantamuktāmuktāṇujīvakaiḥ |
sevitaṃ śrīpatiṃ vyāptaṃ cetanācetanasya tu || 4.3 ||
satyasya sṛṣṭikartāraṃ hariṃ duḥkhādi bhoginaṃ |
jānanti tasya cājñānaṃ kvacitkvāpi na yujyate || 4.4 ||
mithyābhūtājñānamastītyatra mānaṃ na vidyate |
ajñānā bhavitāstaṣṭhopādhirnivaha yujyate || 4.5 ||
siddhau jñāne tu tajjopādhiḥ siddhai tadanvaye |
sarvasyājñānayogastadabhāvāttadevana || 4.6 ||
ajñānābhāvatojñāsī adhikādhi na vidyate |
ajñānābhāvatastannivṛttirūpaṃ na hi kvacith || 4.7 ||
ajñānakalpitaṃ śāstraṃ naivājñānanikṛntanaṃ |
iti heyaṃ tanmataṃ hi baddhasyeśo vimuktidaḥ || 4.8 ||
viśvaṃ satyaṃ naiva mithyā na bādhyaṃ satyakāraṇaṃ |
pratyakṣādipramāṇyaistu siddhaṃ nāropitaṃ jagath || 4.9 ||
nārthakriyākāri śuktau pratītaṃ rajataṃ jagat |
arthakriyākāri tena taddṛṣṭānto na yujyate || 4.10 ||
dṛṣṭāntarahito yattu jaganmithyātvakāraṇaṃ |
na bhavettanmate brahma dṛśyatvācca tathā bhaveth || 4.11 ||
mitākāśādi vedoktaṃ viṣṇuneti natanmṛṣā |
na ca nyāyoktamithyātve mānaṃ tanmatāpi na || 4.12 ||
śrutirnityāpauruṣeyā durvākyairna ca dūṣyate |
iti jñātvā vidhirviṣṇormāhātmyaṃ jñānabodhayath || 4.13 ||
satyāśeṣajagannāthaḥ satyasatsādhanena ca |
satyairjīvaiḥsadopāsyaḥ satyasatyavimuktidaḥ || 4.14 ||
iti merusthito brahmā niśānte yajñamīśvaraṃ |
abhiṣicya sapatnīkaṃ sarge sampārthya yojayath || 4.15 ||
apīpaladhariḥ svargaṃ sarvadevagaṇeḍitaḥ |
tataḥ svāyaṃbhuvamanuṃ śatarūpā samanvitaṃ || 4.16 ||
abhiṣicya vidhirbhūmau barhiṣmatyāmavāsayat |
bhāvīnaśca padasthāśca upadevāḥ sahasraśaḥ || 4.17 ||
manuṃ paryacaranprītyā te niraṃśāstato bhavan |
vidhistato maya(sū)sutamana(ta)vedhādvalāhvayaṃ || 4.18 ||
sa tu ṣaṇṇavatī (prā)māyāḥ svavijṛmbhaṇatosṛjat |
svairiṇyaścaiva kāminyaḥ puṃścalya iti tridhā || 4.19 ||
tajjātābhisamudbhūtāḥ bhoginyo bahuśo matāḥ |
kṛtvākāmyaṃ bhāratesminmartyādhama purogamāḥ || 4.20 ||
adho lokeṣu viharaḥ etābhiḥ saha durmadāḥ |
anekāḥ dānavopetāḥ balastadadhināyakaḥ || 4.21 ||
tadadhastācca vitale trivṛdbhūtagaṇāvṛtaḥ |
kaiścidrakṣau (gu)gaṇairyuktāḥ bha(vā)vantyāste bhavaḥ kila || 4.22 ||
tayā sadā ramati saḥ tadvīryādhāṭakādibhiḥ |
ḥ nadī tadantagā tatra śuṣmatyapyagnivāyubhiḥ || 4.23 ||
yannadīvāripānena adholokeṣu saṃsthitāḥ |
madoddhatā narānāryaḥ krīḍanti suciraṃ sadā || 4.24 ||
tadadhastācca sutale pratimanvantareṣvapi |
nāmarūpavibhedena prahlādaḥ sānugo'vasath || 4.25 ||
vaivasvatāntare viṣṇorvāmanāya padatrayaṃ |
yācamānāya putrāya kaśyapasya mahātmanaḥ || 4.26 ||
balirvirocanasuto dadau lokatrayaṃ vibhuḥ |
sopīndrāya padasthāya tasmādādāya dattavān || 4.27 ||
lokatrayaṃ hi devānāmanujo vaṭurūpadṛk |
payovrataprabhāvenyaivāvirbhūtaḥ sa kaśyapāth || 4.28 ||
indra pravāsataḥ khinnamātṛtuṣṭyai jagatpatiḥ |
baliṃ virocanasutaṃ mahābhāgavatottamaṃ || 4.29 ||
sthāpayāmāsa talloke svayaṃ tadvārapālakaḥ |
babhūva vāmanaḥ sarve svarūpāmarasevitaḥ || 4.30 ||
tasya trikoṭilomabhyo vāyustāvatsvarūpakaḥ |
vāṇyā tāvadrūpavatyā vāmanasyārcako(kā) bhavath || 4.31 ||
vindhyāvalī patistatra balistiṣṭhati sānugaḥ |
tadadhasta(tpri)nmayo viṣṇuḥ bhaktopyakhiladānavaiḥ || 4.32 ||
arthito dānavakule jātastakṣā bhavatpurā ||
tallokapaḥ sadaityānāṃ tripurāṇi vyacīklapath || 4.33 ||
abhedābhedyarūpāṇi svarṇaraupyāyasāni ca |
sa nirmame purāstisraḥ svarṇaraupyāyasīmayaḥ || 4.34 ||
kāmayā teṣu teṣvete saṃsthitā tripuraukasaḥ |
vihārāntare hyasminsarvānjantūnapīḍayan || 4.35 ||
surāstatpīḍayā rudraśaraṇaṃ yayurūrjitāḥ |
rudraḥ pṛthvīrathaṃ kṛtvā rathāṅgaṃ śaśibhaskarau || 4.36 ||
devānkṛtvā hayānabjabhavaṃ kṛtvaiva sārathiṃ |
śeṣaṃ maurvyāṃ vidhāyeśo patatraṃ(taṃ) ca khageśvaraṃ || 4.37 ||
kṛtvā viṣṇuṃ bāṇarūpaṃ svayamekakṣaṇe haraḥ |
tripurāṇi dadāheśaḥ sarvalokasya śaṅkaraḥ || 4.38 ||
tripurāsuraputrādyaiḥ sahā vasati tatra hi |
vaivasvatentare dakṣapatnyāḥ kaśyapabhāryakāḥ || 4.39 ||
trayodaśāsaṃ(śāḥ)stapasā tāsu putrānavāpa saḥ |
vivasvānśakravaruṇamitrāḥ pūṣāryamābhagaḥ || 4.40 ||
savitā tvaṣṭrṛparjanyo dhātāyāṃ vāmano vaṭuḥ |
aditestanayādityāṃ hiraṇyākṣahiraṇyakau || 4.41 ||
danau tu dānavā gāvo mahiṣyaḥ surabhīsutāḥ |
tānmāyā pakṣiṇoriṣṭā yātudhānāśca nirṛtiḥ || 4.42 ||
ilāyāṃ bhūsurā āsanśvādayaḥ paramāsutāḥ |
poṣṭāyā caiva betālāḥ kārdraveyā mahāhayaḥ || 4.43 ||
etanmanvantare ṣaṣṭhisahasrāṇi bhujaṅgamāḥ |
kardrujāsteṣu kecittu tātvikāḥ śeṣasevakāḥ || 4.44 ||
apare karmajājāna stulyāstatputrapautrakāḥ |
sastrīkā maṇibhūdhanyāḥ mahātala nivāsinaḥ || 4.45 ||
mahāviṣāḥ mahāgho(dhā)rāḥ kāmarūpadharāḥ khalāḥ |
sarasvatyāṃ sapatnīkaḥ kaśyapastapa ācarath || 4.46 ||
tasyā vidūre patnīnāṃ bhavanāni vyacīklpat |
upayuktanatadyuktaṃ tadbhṛtyāsteṣu cāvasan || 4.47 ||
kadācidmahimavatpārśvaṃ viyāhi(vipāhi)pati mātarau |
gatvā dṛṣṭvoccaiśravasaṃ nīlavālamahīprasūḥ || 4.48 ||
prāhasvacchanda vinatākadrute vacanaṃ yadi |
satyaṃ dāsī tavāhaṃsyāmanyathātvaṃ mameti ca || 4.49 ||
adyā sarātriśca prātariti te gṛhamīyatuḥ |
tadbālanailyavarṇye tu sutānāha ca sādhavaḥ || 4.50 ||
naiścantānalapadvaihvau kālānnā śobhavettava |
krūrāhayāstathākṛtvā tā cakrurmātṛ kiṃ karīṃ || 4.51 ||
śaptāhayaḥ śeṣavākyātsvarucyā(tsvastryākṣa)kṣamamātmanaḥ |
jñātvā dadurjaratkāroḥ sanāmnīṃ bhaginīṃ muneḥ || 4.52 ||
sa viraktopi santaptaiḥ nirāśyairnarakāsthitaiḥ |
pitṛbhiḥ prārthito gṛhṇaṃstadgarbhe tvāstikaṃ vyadhāth || 4.53 ||
tāṃ tyaktumūrau suṣṭāpa sā sandhyārthevyabodhayat |
nidrābhaṅgakarā gacchetyāhu tatyāja tāṃ muniḥ || 4.54 ||
sāgrajaṃ prāpya suṣuve śikhāyajñopavītinaṃ |
sa āpadi smṛto yāmītyuktogānmātulaṃ prati || 4.55 ||
naimiśaṃ vāsukiḥ prāpya sudhāmamṛtamanthane(te) |
śaptā hīnā tātvikāno tenātyanvipa saṃmatāḥ || 4.56 ||
takṣakaḥ śṛṅgiśāpena vyadaśacca parīkṣitaṃ |
śaśāsa pṛthivīṃ tasya sutaḥ sa janamejayaḥ || 4.57 ||
tadodaṅko gālavasya śiṣyaḥ sevā sukhātmanaḥ |
dakṣiṇāmapi dāsyāmītyuktaḥ sa kupito'diśath || 4.58 ||
asādhya pauṣyabhāryāyāḥ rājñā dehi sukuṇḍale |
ityuktogātpuraṃ tasya mārge gomayabhakṣaṇaṃ || 4.59 ||
kṛtvānācāmya rājānaṃ dṛṣṭvā yācatsva bhāminīṃ |
yācayetyāha tāṃ gehe henāyasya śaptumārabhath || 4.60 ||
nṛpaṃ na śapa māṃ brahmanśuddhaḥ paśyasi bhāminīṃ |
tata ācamya gatvā tāṃ dṛṣṭvā tāṃ prāpa saṅkuṇḍale || 4.61 ||
sāha pūrve takṣakāyemādāmāyā ca tepi ca |
pativratānasaṃdṛśyā(dhṛṣyā)hantu(ntuṃ)śakrohamādṛ(gamadrṛ)taṃ || 4.62 ||
yato bhaveti taṃ prāhagurubhaktaṃ na mā spṛśet |
ityuktau yācitaḥ pauṣyādbhuñjanśrāddhānnagaṃ kacaṃ || 4.63 ||
dṛṣṭvā śatbhavāndha(śapadbhāndha)vastvaṃ iti sopyaśapanmuniṃ |
apatnī kosīti rājye lokasyāndhopi paśyasi || 4.64 ||
iti datvā varaṃ vipro yayau mārgetha takṣakaḥ |
śiṣyo bhūtvā granthivāhaḥ soviśacca rasātalaṃ || 4.65 ||
muniśca yogasāmarthyāttatrāgānnāgamāyayā |
adhostuvadhi(veści)vantasya gomūtraṃ netrayorvyadhāth || 4.66 ||
tato paśyannāgarājo bhiyādātkuṇḍaladvayaṃ |
tadādāya gurordatvā kruddhogājjanamejayaṃ || 4.67 ||
taṃ prāhatvaṃ vṛthājāto pitṛmṛtyuṃ na cāvadhīḥ |
ahaṃ mantrapravaktāsmi sambhārānchaṭayāścalaṃ || 4.68 ||
vinā śeṣaṃ sarvalokānnirbhujaṅgānkaromyahaṃ |
ityuktvā sarpayāgena bhujaṅgānśatakoṭiśaḥ || 4.69 ||
vyahanacchakramagamaṃstakṣakādyā nacāpatan |
tato ṛṣiḥ sahendrotra takṣakaḥ patatāmiti || 4.70 ||
takṣakāyasahendrāya svāhetyajuhadāhutiṃ |
tadākampatsvarāṇṇāgāḥ asmarannāstikaṃ muniṃ || 4.71 ||
sobhītyā pūjito rājñā hyudakenāpi cāstikaḥ |
svābhipretaṃ tadodakamāyā ca janamejayaṃ || 4.72 ||
dāsyerthitamiti prokte dehi mātulajīvanaṃ |
iti takṣakapūrvānsaḥ kālamṛtyorvyamūmucath || 4.73 ||
sācodakaṃ samādhā(dhyā)ya takṣakādīnvadhorvyadhāt |
yajñaprāpya phalaṃ rājñe datvā sarpānathāśapath || 4.74 ||
anāgasaṃ mādaśadhvaṃ nāgasaṃsmaraṇānmama |
anyathā vaḥ śiraḥ śīrṇaṃ bhavediti yayau muniḥ || 4.75 ||
vinatā sevayākhinnā garbhaṃ garbhiṇyatāḍayat |
urupṛṣṭhepurājātānūrupādavivarjitān || 4.76 ||
svāśaktiṃ kālato dāsya prājñāṃrabhagarbhavipaṃ |
proktvā māturyayau yāntā sūryasyāsarathasthitaḥ || 4.77 ||
saudāminyāṃ sutau tasya dvau sampātijaṭāyuṣau |
kālena garuḍo jāto viṣṇorvāho babhūva ha || 4.78 ||
piturvākyādbalaprāptyai babhakṣa gajakacchapau |
gandhamādanago pūrvaṃ gandharvau śāpasambhavau || 4.79 ||
kadrūtamāhātmasutānprati pīyūṣadosicet |
dāsyānmokṣe mātaraṃ ca ityepāstu na cādhikaṃ || 4.80 ||
ityukto'mṛtamādāya prādāttargādvyamocayat |
dāsyātsvamātaraṃ svargagatavaccaṃ sureśvarāḥ || 4.81 ||
prākṣipatṛṇavataṃ ca vidhūya ca sudhāṃ punaḥ |
śakroyadāpa ca svargaṃ(śakrāyadādāpasakhya) tasya kardrumathāvadath || 4.82 ||
kutomṛtaṃ hṛtamiti pṛṣṭe hi vinatāsutaḥ |
mātā tvayā vañcitā me mayā tvaṃ tena vañcitaḥ || 4.83 ||
dehītyuktā sudhāṃ pāyayeti noktamato(hṛ)dhṛtaṃ |
tadādi garuḍaḥ sarpānvañcakānhantumārabhath || 4.84 ||
kardurhisvottamādrohātputraśokāturābhavat |
vañcakāste patanyajñe tadanye garuḍāśinaḥ || 4.85 ||
tadutthitāstasya karaṃ datvā jīvanti kṛtsnaśaḥ |
kovā nirāgasānusardhūgṛdhyajīvitumutsahe || 4.86 ||
ihāmutrāpi duḥkhaṃ te yogyā api bhavanti hi |
kimayogyā rākṣasāstu viśanti tama ūrjitaṃ || 4.87 ||
evaṃ śeṣasya sāmīpyasevāyāmṛtapānataḥ |
sakhyātgarutmataḥ śrīśabhaktito gurubhaktitaḥ || 4.88 ||
niṣkāpaṭyādāstikasya varataḥ śakradāsataḥ |
mātuḥ śāpaṃ tṛṇīkṛtya sukhamāpuḥ sa pannagāḥ || 4.89 ||
kimalabhyaṃ jagatyasti viṣṇuvaiṣṇavasevayā |
mahātale tu(ku) sarpāṇāṃ sthānaṃ vipati bibhrataṃ || 4.90 ||
śivasya varatonyeṣu caranto yānti dur(gar)matiḥ || 4.91 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ dvitīyaparicchede caturtho'dhyāyaḥ
<2.5>
śrībhagavānuvāca
rasātaledhasthāddānavāḥ śatakoṭiśaḥ |
daityāśca yātudhānāśca paulomāḥ kālakeyakāḥ || 5.1 ||
nivātākavacādhīrāḥ rākṣasāśca surāṇakāḥ |
piśācā antyajāsṛkyāḥ mahākumati(mudi)no jarāḥ || 5.2 ||
vasanti cakrabhayato hareḥ sarvabhayādapi |
ye kecittātvikāstatra saṃvasanti surarṣabhāḥ || 5.3 ||
pratimanvantareṣvete varṣesminbhāratekhilāḥ |
harīrasurasadvipragoyoṣidaparāśramān || 5.4 ||
dūṣayitvā pātayitvā kecitpūrṇānyathādhiyaḥ |
nitye vasanti narake hatā haryādi daiḥdvagddha || 5.5 ||
nityaduḥsādhanābhūyo rasātalamupāśritāḥ |
bhūmau bhūtvā brahmarudravarairloko(kā)pakāriṇaḥ || 5.6 ||
sarvāvatārai rāmeṇa kṛṣṇenāpi hatāḥ khilāḥ |
vasanti nityanarake kālādyāsyanti vai tamaḥ || 5.7 ||
tasminhi saramānāmnī śunī brahmavarodbhavā |
sadārtānbhīṣayanti daityānyamasya vaśavartinī || 5.8 ||
kṣīṇāyuṣastatradaityāḥ svapne dṛṣṭvā sudarśanaṃ |
