mṛtāyāsyanti narakaṃ tadvaṃśyā(syā)nāṃ sa āśrayaḥ(maḥ) || 5.9 ||
tadadhastāttu pātāle uttare diśi bhāsure |
sahasravadanaḥ śeṣaḥ sarvaistātvikapannagaiḥ || 5.10 ||
samasta nāgakanyābhiḥ pūjito ratnamaṇḍape |
dhyāyannārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ sadāvasati sāmaraḥ || 5.11 ||
sahasrabhānubhādīpyatkirīṭāvalimaṇḍitaḥ |
sampūrṇaśāradaśaśichavikoṭyadhikadyutiḥ || 5.12 ||
nityāmṛtasravacchubhraphaṇāmaṇḍalamaṇḍitaḥ |
sadaikakuṇḍalo bhāsvanmusalaḥ sahalaḥ prabhuḥ || 5.13 ||
bhānuprabhāmuṇmūrdhanyamaṇiśrīhṛtasarvaruk |
hāranūpuravalayakaṅkaṇāṅgulikādibhiḥ || 5.14 ||
anekamaṇisadbhāsva(tkaṭi)tkirīṭasūtrādikaḥ prabhuḥ |
surāsurakirīṭastharatnāchāditapādukaḥ || 5.15 ||
gandhamālyādisurabhidhyāyannāste virāṭsvayaṃ |
pātāladakṣiṇe bhāge saṃya(mya)minyāṃ puraḥ prabhuḥ || 5.16 ||
śyāmalāvallabho daṇḍadharo mahiṣavāhanaḥ |
samavartī yamaḥ pretapatiḥ nīlāñjanādriruk || 5.17 ||
sarvābharaṇasampannaḥ dharmarāṭrāmacintakaḥ |
bhānuputroścātracitracitraguptau sulekhakau || 5.18 ||
yasyājñāyā bhāratotthapuṇyapāpacayasya tu |
tatra dharmaḥ pravaktāraḥ tattanmanvantarasthitāḥ || 5.19 ||
ṛṣayaḥ sarvadevopadevāstatparicārakāḥ |
ūrdhvakeśā virūpākṣāḥ nīlavarṇabhayaṅkarāḥ || 5.20 ||
tadājñākāriṇāstasya kiṅkarāḥ pāśapāṇayaḥ |
niṣṭhurāḥ nirghṛṇāḥ krūrāḥ mahāparighapāṇayaḥ || 5.21 ||
bhāratātpāpino badhvāhyānīyante yamāntikaṃ |
daśalakṣamatāste tu ghorāstāḍanabharjanaiḥ || 5.22 ||
ghorādhvāne kṛṣantyete nitarāṃ kṛtapāpinaḥ |
sa tānabhyagatāndṛṣṭvā yamadaṇḍaṃ paretarāṭh || 5.23 ||
teṣāṃ mūrdhni sthāpayati yātanā dehināṃ tadā |
puṇyapāpāni te sarve kīrtayanti kṛtāni ca || 5.24 ||
tatra tatvātmikāḥsarve satyamityavadansthitāḥ |
patreṣu tāni(laḥi) likhitānyapi te prapaṭhanti ca || 5.25 ||
sukhaduḥkhātmakaphalaṃ vadanti ṛṣisattamāḥ |
daśalakṣamitāsteṣāṃ yamadūtāstu nārake || 5.26 ||
pātayitvā tāḍayanti yathā pāpānusārataḥ |
viṃśallakṣamitāstatra(sya) sadā rogābhimāninaḥ || 5.27 ||
te vātapittaśleṣmādyāḥ sannipātajvarādayaḥ |
bhagandarātisārāśca vamanārucidurvraṇāḥ || 5.28 ||
hemapīnasabādharyaddhṛ(hṛ)drogādyāstadantike |
vasanti pāpaphaladāḥ kvaciddehakṣayaṅkarāḥ || 5.29 ||
śvāhivṛścikadaṃśāṃśī viṣadurvṛkarūpakāḥ |
viṃśallakṣāstu narake patitānāṃ prabādhakāḥ || 5.30 ||
pātāle saṃvasantyete narakā bahurūpakāḥ |
durgatirnirayaścaiva mahārauravarauravau || 5.31 ||
saṃghātakālasūtrākhyovarcākhyāmanirodhanaṃ |
tailayantrastaptatailo giripātāyasālayau || 5.32 ||
sūcyagnibhūrinirghātau vakrakaṇṭakadānavau |
śvabhojanavratapanau karkaśālimukholbaṇau || 5.33 ||
pūyāsṛṅmūtraviṣṭāśāḥ taptāstroparirambhaṇāḥ |
kālakūṭāgniśakalau sarvaśastrāstrapātanau || 5.34 ||
prāṇarodhamahābhārau krūrāśīviṣavṛścikau |
sarvāṅgachedātmamāṃsau taptavṛkṣābhipātanau || 5.35 ||
śūlārohāpakarṣā dvau durgandhā rākṣasādanaḥ |
bāṇāghāto vaitaraṇī śaṅkamūlakarālakau || 5.36 ||
pipīlikā jalūkādau gṛdhrakaṅkādi bhojane |
āvartanāsphālanau dvau śilāyantraśilādanau || 5.37 ||
karṇavastu pupūrṇār(ṇo) ca dvāra(prakṣe)kṣepaṇabandhanau |
śṛṅkalā taptasikatā sphuliṅgāścāvadhūlayaḥ || 5.38 ||
śiramarmagranthikūṭajalanīrodhanādayaḥ |
santyevāṣṭottaraśatanarakāḥ pātakālayāḥ || 5.39 ||
anityanarakā ete tāmiśrandhatāmiśrakau |
nityau dvau narakau tatra gatāyānti tamaḥ kramāth || 5.40 ||
adho lokeṣu ye devāḥ sarvamanvantareṣvapi |
proktavyāpāraniratāḥ nāmāntaravivarjitāḥ || 5.41 ||
ayogyānāmihaproktanāmarūpāṇi sarvaśaḥ |
ihaiva pūrvamanuṣu(ṣṭu) data evājña(mya)nāmakāḥ || 5.42 ||
ete manulaye sarvetyaktvā sthūlāni sūkṣmataḥ |
svaprājñavaśagā yānti maharlokaṃ tadatyaye || 5.43 ||
prāpyasthūlāni ṛṣibhiḥ svapūrvaṃ pātike(prāntike) kramāth || 5.44 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ dvitīyaparicchede pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ
<2.6>
śrībhagavānuvāca
atalādyā saptalokāḥ bile svargāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
meroradhastātte sarve prāyaśo jñāni saṅkulāḥ || 6.1 ||
eṣāmupari bhūlokaḥ sarvalokatamo mataḥ |
tasminhi bhārate varṣe barhiṣmattākhyapattane || 6.2 ||
śatarūpāpatiḥ pūrvaṃ manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo'bhavat |
devadāsānekakoṭisevitāṅghrisaroruhaḥ || 6.3 ||
śaśāsa medinīṃ etāṃ prajāḍyorasavatsadā |
devahūtistu tatputrī kvaciddharmyagatā bhavath || 6.4 ||
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā saṃganobhūmau gandharvādhīśvaro'patat(bhavat) |
rūpayauvanasampannāṃ tāmāropya rathe svayaṃ || 6.5 ||
bhāryayā saha tatrāgātsarasvatyāṃ taṭaṃ prati |
tatra bindusarastīre tapataḥ kardamasyatāṃ || 6.6 ||
puṇyāśramaṃ pradatvāttu manuḥ kṛchrātsvakaṃ puraṃ |
gate rājani sā devī vastrābharaṇasañcayaṃ || 6.7 ||
nadyāṃ prāsya viraktasya cīrṇaveṇājināmbarā |
vratakṣāmāparyacaratpatiṃ bhaktyā jaṭādharā || 6.8 ||
itthaṃ tayā sevitāṅghrī rājaputrīmabhāṣata |
hitaṃ vadeti sā devī(vavre) taṃ tu yogaprabhāvataḥ || 6.9 ||
saṃyojanāyāmumudyadravirakta(tna)prabhānvitaṃ |
sarvopaskaraṇopetaṃ pakṣivṛkṣajalā(samā)kulaṃ || 6.10 ||
sauvarṇaṃ kālagamataṃ sugandhi sumanoharaṃ |
svayaṃ tadāhṛde snātvā babhau sarvamanoharaḥ || 6.11 ||
sāpi śrīriva saundaryavastrābharaṇabhūṣaṇaiḥ |
tuṣṭvā sa satayā sārdhaṃ remebdaśatakaṃ ṛṣiḥ || 6.12 ||
mervādiṣu carandivyavimānasthaḥ samuktavat |
suśabdasparśasurasarūpagandhamanoharaḥ || 6.13 ||
ninye kālaṃ kṣaṇamiva kusumākaravāyubhiḥ |
ityenaṃ(ratyeta)bindusarasastīra āsannavāṅganāḥ || 6.14 ||
tatputrikānāṃ nāmāni śruṇu me vadataḥ sadā |
kalānasūyā śraddhā ca havirbhūśca gatiḥ kriyā || 6.15 ||
arundhatī ca cittiśca khyātiḥ kardamaputrikāḥ |
kalāṃ marīcaye prādādanusūyāmathā trayī || 6.16 ||
śraddhāmaṅgirase prādātpulastyāya havirbhuvam(bhuvi) |
pulahāya gatiṃ prādātkratave sakriyāṃ sutāṃ || 6.17 ||
arundhatīṃ vasiṣṭhāya cittiṃ ca bhṛgaverpayat |
atharvaṇāya ca khyātiṃ datvā hṛṣṭo babhūva ha || 6.18 ||
tataḥ śatābdaṃ cikrīḍya tatosyāṃ kapilaṃ vyadhāt |
sa kardama tapoyogātsākṣānnārāyaṇo bhavath || 6.19 ||
tyaktvā vimānaṃ bhāryāṃ ca kardamastapase yayau |
autpattibrahmacārī tu tasminbindusarastaṭe || 6.20 ||
svamātre kapilastatvamuktvāmuktiṃ dadau muniḥ |
tattatvaṃ kapilopākhyaṃ karmagranthinikṛntanaṃ || 6.21 ||
marīceḥ kaśyapo jajñe iha vaivasvatāntare |
punarjātasya dakṣasya trayodaśasutāsu saḥ || 6.22 ||
kaśyapāya pradattāsu dityādityādikāsu ca |
pūrvoktāstanayā āsanpūrvamanvantareṣu saḥ || 6.23 ||
kaśyapo nāma bhedeṣu marīcestanayo bhavat |
anyasyāmapi bhāryāyāṃ dakṣajāḥ ṣaṣṭhikanyakāḥ || 6.24 ||
pratimanvantereṣvāsuḥ kaśyapāya trayodaśa |
dattāsyāsurnāmabhedena tā jātāḥ surāsurāḥ || 6.25 ||
pūrvoktā nāmabhedena svasvavyāpārasādhakāḥ |
asminkaśyapanāmāsīdbhāryāputrādayodhunā || 6.26 ||
proktavyāpāranāmānaḥ pūrvavadbhāvi kālake |
sa tātvika ṛṣiproktapuṣkarācca śatāvarāḥ || 6.27 ||
ajāsya dakṣatanayāḥ pūrvamanvantareṣu ca |
saptaviṃśati saṃkhyākāḥ indorbhāryā babhūvire || 6.28 ||
aśvinyādyāstārakāste kālacakrapravartakāḥ |
sarohiṇyāṃ pakṣapātādanyāsu viparītadṛk || 6.29 ||
dakṣaśāpādasantānāḥ sarvamanvantareṣvapi |
bhūtāṅgirakṛśāśvebhyo dve dve datte ca bhānujāḥ || 6.30 ||
bhānunāṃ nakṣatrāṇāṃ manujaḥ … … … |
dattāścataśro dakṣāya daśadharmāya tāsu vai || 6.31 ||
babhūvustanayāḥ prāyo jñānabhaktyādimāninaḥ |
atrinetrodbhavaścandraḥ sarvamanvantareṣvapi || 6.32 ||
vṛkṣāṇāmauṣadhīnāṃ ca nakṣatrāṇāṃ ca vallabhaḥ |
niśākaro himakarastapahṛtsukhadaḥsadā(kṛtsadā) || 6.33 ||
(nirāśakaro ?)
amṛtāṃśosadanneśo hyabdijaḥ śrīsahodaraḥ |
edhamāna kalānāthaḥ śaśāṅko lokabāndhavaḥ || 6.34 ||
anasūyāsutāvatre dattadūrvāsasāvubhau |
yogapravartako yogādbrahmaṇyau brahmacāriṇau || 6.35 ||
datto nārāyaṇaḥsākṣātdūrvāsāḥ śaṅkarāṃśakaḥ |
saṃcaṇḍakopaḥ pṛthayā pūjito manumādiśath || 6.36 ||
tenaiva karṇadharmādīntrīnasūta supūjitā |
sombarīṣādalpaśiṣṭadvādaśyāṃ bhojanerthitaḥ || 6.37 ||
omityuktvā tu kālindyāṃ magno nāgācciraṃ nṛpaḥ |
viprājñayāmbhasā cakre pāraṇaṃ sādhanecchayā || 6.38 ||
tajñātvāthādiśatkṛtyaṃ rājñe sopyasmaradhariṃ |
pūrvamāptaṃ hareścakraṃ kṛtyāṃ hatvā vibhīṣayath || 6.39 ||
sa ṛṣiḥ sarvalokeśaiḥ viṣṇunā ca nirākṛtaḥ |
ambarīśaṃ prāpya tena mocito bhojitobhyagāth || 6.40 ||
sa dūrvāsāḥ rāmakṛṣṇau prāpya kṛṣṇāmayācata |
sadyonnaṃ dīyatāmahyaṃ iti taistuṣṭa āvrajath || 6.41 ||
śraddhayāmaṅgirāḥ prāpa gurumagnimucathyakaṃ |
saṃvartakāgnitanayāḥ prāguktāśca gurorabhūth || 6.42 ||
bhūmāvucathyabhāryāyāṃ antarvartiriraṃsataḥ |
garbhasthadīrghatapasaḥ pādācchannebhage bhuvi || 6.43 ||
patitādretaso jāto muniḥ paramadhārmikaḥ |
bharadvājo marudbhiḥ sa pālitobhūtpurā kila || 6.44 ||
vaivasvatentare candravaṃśe bharatanāmake |
niḥsantāne sati tadā marutastasya taṃ daduḥ || 6.45 ||
sa udūsatpracututvantumutthāpya ca visṛjyatān |
punastapocaraddhīrghakālaṃ bharatavaṃśaje || 6.46 ||
śantanau śyāśitajagadgaṅgāyāṃ caratastapaḥ |
bharadvājaskannamūrvaśī darśanādanu || 6.47 ||
reto dadhāra droṇī(ṇe) saḥ tasmādroṇo bhavadguruḥ |
tasmātkṛpī śaṅkarāṃśamaśvatthāmā samāpa hi || 6.48 ||
bṛhaspatestu tārābhyāṃ kaca āsītsutaḥ purā |
vaivasvatentare sobhūcchukraśiṣyaḥ taponidhiḥ || 6.49 ||
niṣiddhā tena sā vidyā nasyātta iti śāsayat |
kāmāturāpatirmāstu tāni vetya(ttha ?) śapanmuniḥ || 6.50 ||
mṛtasañjīvinīmāpa tenayātāstataḥ kvacit |
devayānīṃ gurusutāṃ vane tadyogamaicchata || 6.51 ||
saha vidhya(dya ?)stu śiṣyeto bhartātestu dvijetaraḥ |
ityāhāto kaviproktaśiṣyeṣvapyadhikaṃ bhaveth || 6.52 ||
devayā(dhā)nī hṛde putryārājñāvedaṣayagharṣaṇaḥ | |
nikṣiptā śukraśiṣyasya tayāmbukrīḍanetarā || 6.53 ||
svavastradhāraṇādātmakṣiptayā duḥkhitābhavat |
hrade'va saddevayānī rājakanyāhṛtāmbarā || 6.54 ||
gatāyāṃ rājanandinyāṃ purataḥ samupāgataḥ |
yayātiścandravaṃśottho mṛgayārthaṃ tadācarath || 6.55 ||
tāṃ vivastrāṃ samudhṛtya svottarīyaṃ dadau nṛpaḥ |
taṃ dhṛtvā sā gṛhaṃ prāparājāgātsvapuraṃ tadā || 6.56 ||
tadarthaṃ kupitaṃ śukraṃ rājānatvā prasādya ca |
guroḥ sutāyai svasutāṃ dāsītve'kalpayannṛpaḥ || 6.57 ||
śukro yayāti nṛpateḥ kanyāṃ svāṃ pradadau mudā |
dāsyā śarmiṣṭayā yuktāṃ dāsī rājñe bhavetsatī || 6.58 ||
yaduḥsuto devayānyāṃ tadvaṃśaḥ kṛtavīryajaḥ |
arjuno yo daśagrīvaṃ nigṛhya punarāpa tath || 6.59 ||
hṛto dattāvitobāhusahasrayugapi sphuṭaṃ |
tadvaṃśajāpsarasutādvasudevādabhūdhariḥ || 6.60 ||
iti śrī prakāśasaṃhitāyāṃ dvitīyaparicchede ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ
